# Fork and distribution polish — file 2 of 3: the three routes

This file is self-contained. It is the second of three `/st-plan` artifacts for **Package 20** (stamity's fork support
and its three distribution routes, end to end). It follows file 1 (`docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md`, fork
correctness and the round trip), whose units all land first, and ships as its own pull request without a release; file 3
(`docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-03.md`) writes the docs, proves every route and cuts 1.12.0.

intent chosen: feature because the request names net-new capability on each route — `stamity upgrade`, a slim APM
package with an `apm-backed` install mode, release channels with a verified promotion, `plugin setup --declare`,
overrides in plugin mode and the review gate on every client — and no dependency moves, so the migration intent does
not fire.

## Context

The three routes as they stand at 1.11.0, measured on 2026-10-01 with the released CLI and the distribution at
`plugins/v1.11.0`:

- **CLI route.** Three clients in generated mode write 181 files (172 at 1.10.0), against 29 in plugin-backed mode; 25
  of them are Codex skill metadata (`agents/openai.yaml`) written with no Codex selected, and 10 skill folders sit
  byte-identical in `.claude/skills/` and `.agents/skills/`. There is no `upgrade` verb, so a fork runs "unpin, sync,
  repin" by hand.
- **APM route.** The package carries agents, commands, rules and skills only, with 36 `${STAMITY:*}` placeholders only
  the CLI fills; an install vendors the whole repository; there is no Renovate preset for APM refs; CI and the release
  test apm-cli 0.30.0 while 0.32.0 is current; and `check` looks for APM duplicates only in plugin mode. The retired
  `stamity-apm` mirror (1.4.0) was the last slim, APM-only source.
- **Plugin route.** No channel exists: `plugin-dist` moves to every release at once. The Claude managed-settings
  template pins a tag, never sets auto-update and always writes the strict allowlist; Copilot's enterprise managed
  settings are generally available (2026-07-01) and stamity ships no template for them. The default branch carries only
  an npm-sourced Claude catalog, so Codex, the Copilot CLI or VS Code adding the repository without a ref get a root with
  no hooks and no runtime. Overrides, packs and model pins never reach a plugin-carried artifact; the review gate blocks
  only on Claude Code; VS Code ignores hook matchers; Cursor's rules live only inside the plugin; and plugin skills never
  call the runtime they bundle.

This file builds each route's missing half. **Out of scope:** file 1's fixes, the docs pages, the route proofs and the
release (file 3), and the drop list.

## Decisions

### The maintainer's walk (2026-10-01, through the question tool; every answer the recommended option unless the row says otherwise)

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| Where the package goes | Its own **Package 20**, after Package 11 track B and before Package 12, so Package 12 re-proves the final distribution once and its "nothing actionable left" close stays true. Order: Package 19 → Package 18 → Track B → Package 20 → Package 12. |
| The 1.11.0 ops-pack regression (`stamity add ops`, then `sync` fails on Cursor and on Codex alone) | Its own `/st-debug` run before Package 19, merged to `main` without a release; not in this plan. |
| Where each release's slim APM package lives | A folder of the distribution tree, fetched alone (`apm install <owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>`); measured first, with an orphan tag as the fallback. |
| What APM alone can do, and how the CLI joins | APM alone works: every placeholder is filled at generation time and the charter rides through APM where a target can take it. A repository that also runs the CLI gets an `apm-backed` mode in which the CLI writes only the charter, hooks, MCP wiring and state, and `check` fails any class delivered twice. |
| How the package ships | **1.12.0 at its end:** one fresh full eval run (about 3.5 h unattended), QA and the tag. It also carries Packages 19 and 18 and the ops-pack fix. |
| Optional plugin items | **All four:** release channels with a verified promotion and a soak; `plugin setup --declare`; overrides in plugin mode; the review gate on every client. |
| Optional CLI and fork items | **`stamity upgrade` only.** Review reasons in `--json` and `release.json`, `stamity status --json` and a release on lane merge go to the drop list. |
| Accounts for live proofs | **VS Code with Copilot on the maintainer's machine only.** The Copilot organization install, the auto-update legs and Cursor's team marketplace stay `Not done:` with their owner. |

### What 1.11.0 already settled (no unit here)

- **Codex no longer grows the shared `AGENTS.md`.** 1.11.0 moved Codex's rules into a root `AGENTS.override.md`; with Codex selected `AGENTS.md` stays 5,330 bytes and the 25,360-byte override is read by Codex alone (measured 2026-10-01 with the released 1.11.0 CLI; at 1.10.0 the same fixture's `AGENTS.md` grew from 5,279 to 25,277 bytes).
- **Codex gets the nine touchpoint workflows** as shared `.agents/skills/` skills (`$st-<id>`).
- **`test/replay/fixture.test.ts` is gone** with the rest of the replay (`d5d3fee7`).
- **Owned by other packages:** the import without `--mirror`, the lane's release-tag-only fetch with the snapshot sweep, the nightly guard and the records move are Package 18 (`docs/plans/014-lean-repository-01.md`, `-02.md`); the paired with/without value evidence (brief Q5) is Package 11 track B.

### Settled by this plan (declared defaults; the maintainer may reverse any before the run starts)

| # | Default | Why |
|---|---|---|
| S1 | Certain waste goes (Codex skill metadata only when Codex is selected); every other double delivery is measured per reader first, removed only where no client loses its copy, and the residue becomes a capability-matrix row with its reason. | VS Code reads `.claude/` and `.github/` copies additively, while Claude Code, the Copilot CLI and Codex each need their own; a blind dedupe would cost a client its copy. |
| S2 | `stamity upgrade --to <version>` runs the target version's `sync` through the pinned `npx -y <package>@<version>` form and moves the `package.json` dev-dependency pin when one exists; it refuses a changed client set without `--allow-client-change`, a downgrade without `--allow-downgrade` and a major jump without `--allow-major`. | The engine that writes the files must be the target version's; the guards keep a fleet run from changing what a team chose. |
| S3 | The slim APM package lives under `apm/` in each release's distribution tree (`<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>`), chosen by a rule declared before the measurement (with apm-cli 0.32.0 a subfolder install costs at most 25% of the whole root's cache, every class deploys on 0.29.1 and 0.32.0, 0.29.1 installs a subfolder); otherwise an orphan `apm/v<version>` tag. The distribution tree keeps its root copy for one compatibility window, so the 1.9–1.11 spec `<owner>/<repo>#plugins/v<version>` keeps working when Renovate moves its ref; the copy goes at the next major release. Kept only if a root install from a tree carrying both copies deploys every primitive once; otherwise the unit stops and the maintainer chooses. Likewise `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` stays as a copy of the new Claude tag `.strict` template until the next major release. | Your convention puts a breaking change in a major release; the old spec is in the wild (release.json named it since 1.9.0). Probe 2026-10-01: a subfolder cost 14.5% of the whole root's cache, because the sparse cone brings the root's top-level files (the four archives, about 7.3 MB). |
| S4 | The APM generator resolves every placeholder with the plugin roots' phrases (REQ-PLUGIN-004); `${STAMITY:CLI}` becomes the pinned call at the package's own version. This reverses the 2026-09-17 decision "APM unchanged", whose revisit trigger (a release of plugin consumers on the phrases) has fired three times. | APM alone has to work. |
| S5 | The charter ships as its own small APM package, `apm-charter/` in the distribution tree, which an APM-only consumer adds beside the main package and an `apm-backed` repository does not (there the CLI's charter, with the detected facts and gates, is the copy of record). Its repo-fact and gate rows read `unknown` (the charter's own honesty rule, REQ-PLUGIN-017) and its invariants version comes from its frontmatter; the main package's 36 other tokens take the plugin phrases (S4). | Probe 2026-10-01 (apm-cli 0.32.0): an instruction with no `applyTo` deploys at install for Claude Code (always-on), Copilot and Cursor, only Codex waits for `apm compile`, `context/` is neither deployed nor folded, and a consumer cannot exclude one instruction of a dependency — so a charter inside the main package would reach every `apm-backed` repository twice. Settles the spec author's marker on REQ-APM-012 with a design none of its three options offered. |
| S6 | The new install-mode value is `apm-backed`, set by `init --install-mode apm-backed` or the config key `installMode`. "Delivered twice" means one client receiving one artifact from APM and from the CLI; one client reading another client's file (Cursor or Copilot listing Codex's touchpoints) is capability-matrix residue, not a failure. `check`'s duplicate row runs in every install mode; a finding whose source is `apm` (read from `apm.lock.yaml`) fails in every mode; findings whose source is `ledger` or `unmanaged` keep today's split — `warn` under `generated`, `fail` under `plugin-backed` — and the accepted mixed state stays. | Settles the marker on REQ-PLUGIN-019 (option a): a repository mid-move to the plugin keeps a green CI, and a class APM and the CLI both deliver still fails. |
| S7 | Hooks and MCP through APM are not built. | microsoft/apm#3129 (Cursor hooks written in the Claude shape and rejected) is open; the Codex fix (PR #3060) merged after 0.32.0 and is unreleased. |
| S8 | Channels: `stamity.distribution.channels` defaults to `{ stable: "plugin-stable" }`, with an optional `canary`; `plugin-dist` stays the newest release and is not a channel; promotion runs behind a GitHub environment with required reviewers; `soakDays` defaults to 0, and the docs recommend at least three days on canary. | Claude Code has no channel concept and detects updates by version; a moving branch per channel, promoted only after verification, is the vendor-documented pattern. |
| S9 | The managed-settings templates render one per channel and one pinned to the release tag, for Claude Code and for Copilot; `strictKnownMarketplaces` is written only on the organization's opt-in. | A strict allowlist blocks every other marketplace and fails closed on a one-character mismatch. |
| S10 | In plugin mode, overrides, patches and model-pinned agents are emitted as project-level copies with a per-client precedence measured at execution; installed pack skills are exempt from the plugin-owned filter on every client; `check` and `plugin status` report per client what reaches. | The maintainer chose "overrides in plugin mode"; Claude namespaces plugin artifacts, the Copilot CLI lets project skills shadow plugin skills, VS Code prefixes plugin skills. |
| S11 | The review gate maps to a client's completion event only after that client's event and payload are measured; where no event can block, the prose ladder stays and `check` and the docs say so. | The maintainer chose "review gate on all clients"; a gate mapped on an unmeasured payload could block every completion or none. |
| S12 | Cursor's rules become repository-owned `.cursor/rules/*.mdc` in plugin mode and leave the Cursor plugin root; `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` is generated in both modes. | The Cursor CLI reads `.cursor/rules`; plugin rules in the CLI are undocumented; Bugbot reads neither plugins nor `.cursor/rules`. |
| S13 | Live proofs in this file use only what the maintainer's machine has: Claude Code, Codex, the Copilot CLI, the Cursor CLI and VS Code with Copilot. | The maintainer's answer on accounts. |
| S14 | `u2-apm-backed-mode` owns an additive extension to file 1's lock reader, `src/detect/apmLock.ts`: `ApmLockDependency` gains `virtualPath` and `version` (both `string \| null`), and `parseApmLock` falls back to the top-level `deployments[]` rows when a dependency carries no `deployed_files`; file 1's fields, read states (`kind`/`detail`) and `repoUrl: string \| null` keep their shapes. The alternative not taken: fold the extension into file 1's `u1-foreign-paths`. | Taken on the PR #73 review (2026-10-02): file 1 declares the reader file 2 reads, and only file 2's units need these fields; the fallback also widens `apmDeployedPaths` for file 1's consumers on a lock with no `deployed_files`, which only makes their foreign-path guard stricter. |

### Before this file runs

- **File 1 has merged** (`docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md`): the per-key settings ownership, the `apm.lock.yaml`
  reader, `fork-identity.mjs --suffix`, `clean` keeping user state and the fork CI job are on `main`, and the fork CI job
  is green there.
- **The maintainer is available for four short steps:** installing VS Code with GitHub Copilot Chat (the `code` command on
  the maintainer's machine opened Cursor on 2026-10-01), adding and later removing one Codex trust entry for a probe
  fixture, the probe runs under the maintainer's own client logins, and — only if wanted — approving arm R of
  `u2-apm-sparse-measure` (a throwaway public repository). A step not performed is `Not done:` with its owner; the units
  proceed with what was measured.

## Spec delta

This slice adds REQ-FLOW-033 and REQ-FLOW-034 to `docs/specs/everyday-flows.md`, REQ-APM-010 to REQ-APM-016 to
`docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md`, and REQ-PLUGIN-034 to REQ-PLUGIN-042 to `docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md`. It
modifies the APM spec's mirror paragraph, client floor, invariants 1 and 2, REQ-APM-001 to REQ-APM-005 (to headings,
with one content change in REQ-APM-002), REQ-APM-009 and its acceptance criteria; the plugin spec's invariants and
REQ-PLUGIN-004, -011, -014, -015, -016, -019, -023, -028 and -029; REQ-FLOW-002 and REQ-FLOW-026; and REQ-UPSTREAM-017. Every
`path:line` was read at `88fcfd32`. `/st-work` merges it at its Prove phase. `<date>` is the merge date and `<unit>` the
unit id; each entry names the unit that implements it.

File 1 (`docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md`) and Packages 18 and 19 merge before this file. Package 18 amends
REQ-APM-002 and the APM spec's vendored-tree figures (`docs/plans/014-lean-repository-02.md`, its spec delta C), and
file 1 amends REQ-PLUGIN-016, REQ-PLUGIN-028 and REQ-UPSTREAM-017 in other places. Where an earlier change moved a line
cited here, the merging unit re-reads it at intake and applies this change to the text it finds. Each `####` heading
lands in its spec as a `###` heading in that spec's own form, without the parenthetical. The ids were confirmed free by
reading the four target specs whole at `88fcfd32`; the repository-wide `rg` over `docs/` could not run in the drafting
session, so the merging unit runs it first.

### A. `docs/specs/everyday-flows.md`

Each requirement's criteria land under `## Acceptance criteria` as a bold block headed by the requirement's id, in id order.

#### REQ-FLOW-033 — Each client gets each artifact once where its readers allow (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-repo-file-waste`. Generated mode writes no file that no selected client reads, and no
second copy of an artifact a selected client already gets, wherever removing it costs no client its copy.

- **Certain waste, removed.** Codex skill metadata (`agents/openai.yaml` beside a `SKILL.md`) is written only when Codex
  is selected, and only under `.agents/skills/`, the one skills folder Codex reads; no copy goes under `.claude/skills/`.
- **Measured first, then removed or documented.** The nine touchpoint skills in `.agents/skills/` that also reach
  Copilot, which has them in `.github/prompts/`; the rule-skills Codex adds to the shared `.agents/skills/` that Cursor and
  Copilot read; skill folders written byte-identical to both `.claude/skills/` and `.agents/skills/` (VS Code reads both);
  `.claude/rules/` and `.github/instructions/`, both read by VS Code's Local agent. Each is removed only on a dated vendor
  citation per reader showing no client loses its copy.
- **The residue** — every double delivery that stays — is a row in the capability matrix, per client pair, with its
  reason.

- **Units:** `u2-repo-file-waste`.
- **Evidence (before):** the touchpoint companion is `TOUCHPOINT_POLICY_FILE = "agents/openai.yaml"`
  (`src/emit/skillsProjection.ts:529`), emitted with every touchpoint row (`:563-593`) whenever Codex or Cursor emits;
  the bundled skills ship their own `agents/openai.yaml` (`:525-528`). Measured 2026-10-01: three-client generated mode
  (claude, cursor, copilot) writes 181 files (172 at 1.10.0) against 29 plugin-backed, 25 of them `agents/openai.yaml`
  with no Codex selected; 10 skill folders are byte-identical in `.claude/skills/` and `.agents/skills/`.
- **Proof:** `test/emit/skillsProjection.test.ts`, `test/emit/touchpointSkills.test.ts`, the cross-client goldens,
  `test/emit/capabilityMatrix.test.ts`; the measurement record per reader; QA.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-033**):
- GIVEN tools `[claude, cursor, copilot]` WHEN `sync` runs THEN no emitted path ends in `agents/openai.yaml`; GIVEN tools
  `[claude, codex]` THEN no path under `.claude/skills/` ends in `agents/openai.yaml`.
- GIVEN tools `[codex, cursor]` THEN each of the nine touchpoint directories carries `agents/openai.yaml`; WHEN codex is
  then deselected and `sync` runs THEN every `agents/openai.yaml` is reclaimed and each touchpoint `SKILL.md` is
  byte-identical.
- GIVEN each dedupe the unit applies THEN the measurement record cites a dated vendor page or a measured run for every
  reader of that path, and the client that loses the copy still lists the artifact.
- GIVEN the capability matrix THEN every double delivery left for a selected pair is a row naming both paths and the
  reason it stays.
- GIVEN the three-client configuration THEN the emitted file count is recorded beside the 181 baseline.

#### REQ-FLOW-034 — `stamity upgrade --to <version>` moves the pins and rewrites the engine's files in one step (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-upgrade-verb`. A new verb:
`stamity upgrade --to <version> [--json] [--allow-client-change] [--allow-downgrade] [--allow-major]`. It resolves
`<version>` in the running package's own registry, runs that version's `sync` through the pinned
`npx -y <package>@<version> sync` form, moves the `package.json` dev-dependency pin when one exists, never touches user
state, and lists the files it changed. It refuses, writing nothing, when the target would change the selected client set
(unless `--allow-client-change`), when the target is older (unless `--allow-downgrade`), and when the target is a
different major version (unless `--allow-major`).

- **Units:** `u2-upgrade-verb`.
- **Evidence (before):** no `upgrade` verb exists (exit 2), and `sync --help` says none does (`src/cli/commands/sync.ts:63-74`).
  A new verb moves seven hand-kept surface pins (learning `surface-pins-are-literals-that-drift`), two of them arrays in
  `test/docsPages.test.ts`.
- **Expand/contract:** additive; no existing verb changes. Rollback is `upgrade --to <prior> --allow-downgrade`, or the
  pinned sync at the prior version.
- **Proof:** `test/cli/commands/upgrade.test.ts` over fixture repositories with a stubbed registry;
  `test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts`; `test/docsPages.test.ts`; QA.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-034**):
- GIVEN a setup at version A with `@<pkg>` pinned in `devDependencies` WHEN `upgrade --to B` runs (B newer, same major)
  THEN the pin reads B, the manifest's `generatedBy` reads B, and the files written equal what `npx -y <pkg>@B sync`
  writes on a copy of the same tree.
- GIVEN the same with no `package.json` THEN the files change and no `package.json` is created.
- GIVEN learnings, handoffs, overrides and run records THEN each is byte-identical after the upgrade.
- GIVEN a target whose sync would change the selected clients THEN the verb exits 1 naming the change and writes nothing;
  with `--allow-client-change` it proceeds.
- GIVEN an older target THEN it exits 1 unless `--allow-downgrade`; GIVEN a different major THEN it exits 1 unless
  `--allow-major`.
- GIVEN a version the registry does not have THEN it exits 1 naming the version and writes nothing.
- GIVEN `--json` THEN one document lists every changed path, with `ok`, `command` and `version`.
- GIVEN the README's commands list, the CLI reference and the docs pins THEN each names `upgrade`.

#### REQ-FLOW-002 — Every CLI call the flows and hooks make resolves after the documented npx setup (MODIFIED)

Replaces `docs/specs/everyday-flows.md:79-81`, "`.claude/` bodies carry the rendered form; the APM bodies under `.apm/`
ship the token raw, as they ship every token (the accepted APM state)." New text:

(REQ-APM-011). (Amended `<date>`, plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-placeholders`; it read "the APM bodies under `.apm/`
ship the token raw, as they ship every token (the accepted APM state)".)

The criterion at `:632-634` read "… and the APM package's copy of the charter body carries `${STAMITY:CLI}` raw." New
text: "… and no file of the APM package carries `${STAMITY:`."

### B. `docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md`

#### REQ-APM-010 — Each release ships a slim APM package (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, units `u2-apm-sparse-measure` and `u2-apm-slim-package`. Each release's distribution tree carries
the APM package — `apm.yml` and `.apm/` — under `apm/`, installed as `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>`, and
`release.json` names that spec as `apm.installSpec`. The release generates the copy with the same generator; a test holds
it byte-identical to the source tree's projection for the same commit. The source tree keeps its root `apm.yml` and
`.apm/`, so `<owner>/<repo>#v<version>` keeps working. The distribution tree also keeps its root copy for one
compatibility window, so a consumer pinned to the 1.9–1.11 spec `<owner>/<repo>#plugins/v<version>` keeps working when
Renovate moves its ref; the docs mark that spec deprecated, and the root copy goes at the next major release.

A measurement comes first, with apm-cli 0.32.0 and 0.29.1 and a rule declared before the run: the folder form stands when,
with 0.32.0 in its default cache mode, a subfolder install's cache is at most 25% of the whole distribution root's, a local
fixture with the package under `apm/` deploys every class for every target on both clients, and 0.29.1 installs a
subfolder through its subdirectory route; otherwise the slim package moves to an orphan tag `apm/v<version>` holding only
the package. The same run installs the root spec from a tree carrying both copies and keeps the root copy only when every
primitive deploys once; when one deploys twice, the unit stops and the maintainer chooses between a breaking change at the
next major release and a dependency-only root `apm.yml` that redirects to the folder.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-sparse-measure` (the research gate), `u2-apm-slim-package`.
- **Evidence:** the distribution tree carries `apm.yml` and `.apm/` at its root today
  (`scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:9-13`, `:513`); `release.json`'s `apm.installSpec` is built and validated in
  `scripts/plugins/releaseManifest.mjs:33`, `:215-223`. Probe 2026-10-01 (apm-cli 0.32.0, fresh cache each run): the whole
  distribution root `#plugins/v1.11.0` cost 65,240 KB of cache and vendored 2,883 files; the one-file subfolder
  `admin#plugins/v1.11.0` cost 9,480 KB (14.5%), because the sparse cone always brings the root's top-level files, the four
  plugin archives (about 7.3 MB) among them.
- **Expand/contract:** expand — the slim copy and the new spec are added and the old spec keeps working through the
  window. Rollback: the next release builds without `apm/`, and consumers keep the root spec.
- **Proof:** `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`, `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts`, `test/ci/apmPackage.test.ts`; the
  measurement record; the slim-ref smoke of REQ-APM-016.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the measurement record THEN it names both client versions, every arm with its cache bytes, `apm_modules` bytes and
  file count, deployed file count and exit code, its date, the verdict line, and the dual-copy result.
- GIVEN a distribution build (folder form) THEN `apm/apm.yml` and `apm/.apm/**` exist, every file under `apm/` is
  byte-identical to the checkout's `apm.yml` and `.apm/` for that commit, and the root copy is byte-identical too.
- GIVEN `release.json` THEN `apm.installSpec` equals `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>` (or the fallback tag's spec,
  when the record chose it), and the validator refuses any other shape.
- GIVEN installs from the slim spec, the root distribution spec and the source-tree spec at one version THEN the deployed
  files are byte-identical.

#### REQ-APM-011 — The APM package carries no unresolved placeholder (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-placeholders`. The APM generator resolves every `${STAMITY:*}` token at generation time
with the mapping REQ-PLUGIN-004 states for plugin roots: the gate and fact tokens read as the charter-reference phrases,
and `${STAMITY:CLI}` as the pinned call at the package's own version. `--check` fails, naming the file and the token,
when any `${STAMITY:` remains. This reverses the 2026-09-17 decision "APM unchanged".

- **Units:** `u2-apm-placeholders`.
- **Evidence:** 36 `${STAMITY:*}` occurrences in 11 files over 6 tokens (`CLI` ×12; `VERIFY_GATE_ALL`, `_TEST`, `_LINT`,
  `_TYPECHECK` and `TEST_FRAMEWORK` ×24) ship unresolved on purpose (`scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:92-99`); the phrases
  are `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs`, bound to `REPO_SUBSTITUTION_TOKENS` (REQ-PLUGIN-004).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/apmPackage.test.ts`; `node scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs --check`; a test binding the APM mapping
  to the plugin mapping.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the committed `.apm/` and `apm.yml` THEN no file contains `${STAMITY:`.
- GIVEN a corpus body with an unknown `${STAMITY:` token WHEN the generator runs THEN it exits 1 naming the file and the
  token, and writes nothing.
- GIVEN one token's phrase changed in `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs` WHEN `--check` runs on the committed tree THEN it
  exits 1, so the two mappings cannot part.
- GIVEN the APM body of `st-handoff` THEN its CLI calls read `npx -y <package>@<version>` at the package's version.

#### REQ-APM-012 — The charter reaches APM-only consumers as its own package (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-charter`. The charter ships as its own small APM package, `apm-charter/` in each
release's distribution tree (`<owner>/<repo>/apm-charter#plugins/v<version>`), which an APM-only consumer adds beside the
main package and an `apm-backed` repository does not: there the CLI's charter, with the detected facts and gates, is the
copy of record. The charter package holds one instruction with no `applyTo`; its repo-fact and gate rows read `unknown`,
the charter's own honesty rule for unknown facts, and its invariants version comes from the charter's frontmatter, so no
`${STAMITY:` token remains. The docs list, per target, how the charter reaches the consumer: Claude Code, Copilot and
Cursor take it at install as an always-on instruction; Codex takes it through `apm compile`. A target that cannot deliver
it is listed as such, never presented as covered.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-charter`.
- **Evidence:** the generator leaves the charter out by settled scope (`scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:161-170`); the
  charter's fact and gate rows are `content/charter/stamity-charter.md:23-35` and its invariants token `:38`. Probe
  2026-10-01 (apm-cli 0.32.0): `apm install` deploys an instruction with no `applyTo` straight away — to `.claude/rules/`
  with no frontmatter (always-on), to `.github/instructions/` and to `.cursor/rules/` — and only codex waits for
  `apm compile`; a `context/` primitive is neither deployed nor folded; a consumer cannot exclude one instruction of a
  dependency (the filters are `skill_subset` and `target_subset`). So the charter cannot ride in the main package without
  reaching every `apm-backed` repository twice, with `unknown` facts beside the detected ones.
- **Proof:** `test/ci/apmPackage.test.ts`; the APM smoke per target; the docs pin.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the generated charter package THEN it carries exactly one instruction with no `applyTo`, holding no `${STAMITY:`
  token, its repo-fact and gate rows reading `unknown`, and the main package carries no charter.
- GIVEN apm-cli 0.29.1 and 0.32.0 WHEN a consumer installs both packages for each target THEN the deployed or compiled
  output contains the charter's `## Invariants` heading exactly once per target the docs list as delivered (measured at
  execution, per target).
- GIVEN a consumer in mode `apm-backed` that installed the charter package anyway WHEN `check` runs THEN the duplicates row
  fails naming the charter's two copies.
- GIVEN the docs' per-target table THEN every target the smoke covers has a row, and each row says delivered or not
  delivered, and how.

#### REQ-APM-013 — An APM-backed install mode, and a duplicate check in every mode (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-backed-mode`. The install mode gains the value `apm-backed` beside `generated` and
`plugin-backed`, set by `init --install-mode apm-backed` or the config key `installMode`. In it, the CLI writes only what
APM does not carry: the charter, hooks, MCP wiring and state, and — because APM gives Codex no commands — Codex's nine
touchpoint skills. `check`'s duplicates row runs in every install mode and reads the deployed paths `apm.lock.yaml`
records (the reader file 1 introduced). "Delivered twice" means one client receives one artifact through two
routes, APM and the CLI, for that client; one client reading another client's file (Cursor or Copilot listing Codex's
touchpoints in `.agents/skills/`) is residue, listed in the capability matrix and not failed. An unparseable `apm.yml` or
`apm.lock.yaml` is a failing line of the duplicates row naming the file. A dependency on a mirror under another owner is
matched through the lock's deployed paths, not by name.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-backed-mode`. The manifest schema is a shared contract (REQ-PLUGIN-014 as amended below).
- **Evidence (before):** the mode is one value per repository, `generated` or `plugin-backed`
  (`src/manifest/manifest.ts:1172`); duplicate detection returns `pass` when no client records a plugin
  (`src/cli/commands/check.ts:996-999`); the `apm` source matches dependency lines by name
  (`src/cli/commands/plugin/probe.ts:628-632`, `:665-677`, `:704-723`); an unparseable `apm.yml` reports nothing and a
  mirror under another owner is not reported (`docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:769-774`; two inbox rows).
- **Expand/contract:** expand — the value, the key and the flag are added; `generated` and `plugin-backed` behave as
  before apart from the duplicates row's reach. An engine at 1.11.0 refuses a manifest whose mode it does not know
  (the REQ-PLUGIN-014 "exactly"), so a downgrade fails loudly rather than re-emitting APM's classes. Rollback:
  `config set installMode generated`, then the prior version's sync.
- **Proof:** `test/manifest/`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/init.test.ts`, the coexistence leg of
  REQ-APM-016; QA.

Criteria:
- GIVEN mode `apm-backed`, tools `[claude, copilot]` and a lock deploying the stamity package's agents, commands, rules
  and skills WHEN `sync` runs THEN it writes the charter, hook configuration, MCP documents and state, and 0 files of
  those four classes.
- GIVEN mode `generated` and the same lock WHEN `check` runs THEN it exits 1 with a duplicates row naming each class, its
  paths and the source `apm`; GIVEN mode `apm-backed` THEN that row passes.
- GIVEN mode `apm-backed` with Codex and Cursor selected THEN the nine touchpoint skills are written, the row passes, and
  the capability matrix lists the cross-read.
- GIVEN an `apm.lock.yaml` or an `apm.yml` that does not parse THEN `check` exits 1 with a duplicates line naming the
  file.
- GIVEN a lock deploying the stamity package from `acme/stamity-mirror` THEN the duplicates row finds it.
- GIVEN `init -y --install-mode apm-backed` and `config set installMode <value>` THEN both record the mode, and any other
  value exits 1 naming the three values.

#### REQ-APM-014 — The client floor is checked and the current client is tested (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-floor-current`. CI and the release's `apm-route` job test 0.29.1 (the floor) and
0.32.0 (current), and CI keeps 0.29.0 as the `--expect-failure` witness. A new doctor row, `apm-client`, reads
`apm_version` from the consumer's `apm.lock.yaml` and fails below 0.29.1, naming the found version, the floor and the
upgrade remedy; with no lock it passes, saying so.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-floor-current`.
- **Evidence (before):** CI tests 0.29.1, 0.30.0 and 0.29.0 `--expect-failure` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:378-450`,
  `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:109-132`); the release `apm-route` job tests 0.30.0 only (`.github/workflows/release.yml:554-623`,
  `:652`). Newest is 0.32.0 (2026-09-25); 0.31.0 and later exit 1 on a zero deploy (microsoft/apm PR #2806), re-measured at
  execution. `apm_version` exists only in the lockfile (`docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:88-89`).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`; the smoke runs.

Criteria:
- GIVEN `ci.yml` THEN the APM smoke matrix is exactly 0.29.1, 0.32.0 and 0.29.0 with `--expect-failure` on 0.29.0 only.
- GIVEN `release.yml`'s `apm-route` job THEN it runs the smoke at 0.29.1 and 0.32.0.
- GIVEN a lock whose `apm_version` is `0.29.0` WHEN `check` runs THEN the `apm-client` row fails naming `0.29.0`, `0.29.1`
  and the upgrade command; GIVEN `0.29.1` or newer THEN it passes; GIVEN no lock THEN it passes with "no apm.lock.yaml".

#### REQ-APM-015 — A Renovate preset tracks the slim APM ref (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-renovate-preset`. A new preset, `renovate/apm.json`, configures Renovate's `apm`
manager with `pinDigests` and one group for stamity's APM pins, matched by dependency name (`matchDepNames`), because the
manager's package name is the repository for both the source-tree and the slim dependency. Rule 0 covers the
distribution packages (`<owner>/<repo>/apm`, `<owner>/<repo>/apm-charter`) with regex versioning on `plugins/v<X.Y.Z>`;
rule 1 covers the source tree (`<owner>/<repo>`) with semver versioning on `v<X.Y.Z>`. The canonical preset is strict.
`scripts/fork-identity.mjs` retargets both rules to the fork's slug, and with `--suffix <id>` requires the fork's own
`-<id>.<n>` in both: rule 0's template becomes `tagVersioningTemplate({ prefix: "plugins/v", suffix: <id>, required:
true })` (file 1), and rule 1 gains `ignoreUnstable: false` and an `allowedVersions` pattern that needs the
suffix — required, unlike the plugins preset, because a fork imported by Package 18's recipe carries upstream's `v*` tags,
which an optional suffix would offer to its consumers. Its description and the docs say the manager reads only the string
form `owner/repo[/path]#<ref>` and drops object entries silently, and runs `apm install`, so `apm` must be on Renovate's
`PATH` with `GITHUB_APM_PAT` for a private repository.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-renovate-preset`.
- **Evidence:** `renovate/` holds only `plugins.json` and `companion.json`; the `apm` manager reads `depName` as the text
  before `#` and `packageName` as the first two segments, parses `LooseArray(z.string()).catch([])`, defaults to
  `semver-coerced` versioning and runs `apm install` (renovatebot/renovate `lib/modules/manager/apm/`, read 2026-10-01;
  the upstream-lane spec records the ordering rule).
- **Proof:** a preset test beside `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`; `test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the canonical `renovate/apm.json` THEN it parses as a Renovate preset with `pinDigests: true` on both rules, rule
  0's pattern matches `plugins/v1.12.0` and neither `plugins/v1.12.0-rc.1` nor `plugins/v1.12.0-acme.1`, and no rule
  carries `ignoreUnstable: false`.
- GIVEN `fork-identity.mjs --repository <fork> --suffix acme` THEN both rules name the fork's dependencies, rule 0 matches
  `plugins/v1.12.0-acme.1` and `plugins/v1.12.0-acme.2` and not `plugins/v1.12.0` or `plugins/v1.12.0-other.1`, and rule 1
  allows `v1.12.0-acme.1` and not `v1.12.0`.
- GIVEN the APM docs THEN they state the string-form limit and the `PATH` and token needs.

#### REQ-APM-016 — The APM route is proved next to another APM package, and a fork release smoke-tests its slim ref (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-coexistence-fork`. A CI leg installs the slim package from a local distribution build
next to another APM fixture package. `fork-release.yml` gains an `apm-route` job with `contents: read`, after the
publish job, that smoke-tests the fork's slim ref and prints the install spec; the publish job, which holds the write
credentials, runs no package manager.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-coexistence-fork`.
- **Evidence (before):** `fork-release.yml` runs only `generate-apm-package.mjs --check` (`:315-321`, `:842`); no leg
  installs beside another package; the publish job's own comment keeps package managers out of it
  (`.github/workflows/release.yml:670-672`, mirrored in the fork workflow).
- **Proof:** the CI leg; `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`; `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the leg WHEN both packages install THEN no deployed path collides, and the lock records both.
- GIVEN that consumer in mode `apm-backed` WHEN `sync` and `check` run THEN no class is written twice and `check` exits 0.
- GIVEN the same consumer in mode `generated` WHEN `check` runs THEN it exits 1 with the duplicates row.
- GIVEN `fork-release.yml` THEN its `apm-route` job needs the publish job, holds `contents: read` only, runs the smoke
  against `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>` and prints that spec; a dry run prints the spec it would test.

#### APM spec — "The intentional difference from the mirror" (MODIFIED, context)

Replaces `docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:80-83`, "The cost is disk and clone time on the consumer's side; it is
accepted rather than worked around, because every workaround (a subdirectory package, a second repository, stripping
tracked files) reintroduces the second copy this decision removes. If APM gains a package-side include list, the
projection can adopt it in the generator." New text:

That cost holds for an install from the source tree (`<owner>/<repo>#v<version>`). Since `<date>` each release also ships
a slim package: the same projection, generated by the release with the same generator, placed under `apm/` in the
distribution tree and installed as `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>` (REQ-APM-010). It is a release artifact, not a
second maintained copy; a test holds it byte-identical to the source projection. The measurement of `<date>` recorded
what APM fetches for it and chose the folder form or the orphan-tag fallback. (Amended `<date>`, plan 016 file 2, unit
`u2-apm-slim-package`; it read as quoted above. Package 18 amends the figures at `:77-78` first.)

### C. `docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md`

#### REQ-PLUGIN-034 The default branch carries a catalog per client that resolves to the full release root (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-main-catalogs`). A client that adds this repository without a ref reads the
default branch. The default branch carries one catalog per client that reads it — `.agents/plugins/marketplace.json`
(Codex), `.github/plugin/marketplace.json` (Copilot CLI and VS Code) and `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` (Claude Code,
and the fallback both others read) — and each entry points at the release's own root in the distribution, at the
`plugins/v<version>` tag, through the source kind that client supports. No default-branch entry has an npm source. The
catalogs are regenerated at each release and by `scripts/fork-identity.mjs`, and `--check` fails on drift.

- **Units:** `u2-main-catalogs`.
- **Evidence (before):** the default branch carries only `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`, npm-sourced
  (`.claude-plugin/marketplace.json:12-16`), and no Codex or Copilot catalog. Codex without `--ref` reads the default
  branch's Codex catalog, then its Claude catalog, and installs a root with no runtime and no hooks while both commands
  exit 0 (walked 2026-09-24, `docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:923-927`). The Copilot CLI searches `marketplace.json`, then
  `.plugin/`, then `.github/plugin/`, then `.claude-plugin/` (research return, 2026-10-01). The per-client source kinds
  are the plugin spec's (`docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:372-375`).
- **Proof:** a catalog test beside `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`; `node scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs --check`;
  file 3's route proofs.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the default branch at a release THEN the three catalogs exist, each entry's `version` equals the release version
  where the client documents one, and each entry resolves to that client's root at `plugins/v<version>`.
- GIVEN any of the three THEN no entry carries `"source": "npm"`.
- GIVEN a fork identity applied by `fork-identity.mjs` THEN every entry names the fork's repository, and none names the
  canonical one.
- GIVEN a catalog edited by hand WHEN the generator's `--check` runs THEN it exits 1 naming the file.
- GIVEN `codex plugin marketplace add <owner>/<repo>` with no `--ref` WHEN `codex plugin add stamity@stamity` runs THEN the
  installed root carries `runtime/` and its hook configuration (measured at execution on the maintainer's machine).
  Whether VS Code resolves the Copilot catalog's source kind is measured at execution as well.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-035 Release channels move only through a verified promotion (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-channels-promote`). `stamity.distribution.channels` names moving branches:
`stable` (default `plugin-stable`) and an optional `canary`. It is validated like `branch`. `plugin-dist` stays the
newest release. A `promote.yml` workflow, shared by the canonical repository and forks, takes a tag and a channel on
dispatch and runs in an environment with required reviewers. `scripts/promote-channel.mjs` verifies before it moves
anything: the tag's orphan commit message reads `plugins: v<version> from <sha>`, `release.json`'s version equals the
tag's, every archive matches its release-asset `.sha256`, and, when `soakDays` is set, the tag is at least that many days
old (default 0). Then it force-pushes the channel to the tag's commit. Rollback is promoting the previous tag.

- **Units:** `u2-channels-promote`.
- **Evidence (before):** no channel exists; `plugin-dist` is force-pushed to each release's orphan commit
  (`.github/workflows/release.yml:916-1003`, `.github/workflows/fork-release.yml:671-751`); `branch` is validated at
  `scripts/distribution-identity.mjs:25`, `:113-120`, `:229-250`; each catalog entry pins `ref: plugins/v<version>` with
  the release version (`scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs:158-165`, `:181`), and Claude detects updates by version (research
  return, 2026-10-01).
- **Expand/contract:** additive; `plugin-dist` and the tags are unchanged. Rollback is a promotion.
- **Proof:** `test/ci/promoteChannel.test.ts` over a fixture remote; `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`;
  `test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN a valid tag WHEN `promote-channel.mjs --tag plugins/v<X> --channel stable` runs THEN `plugin-stable` points at the
  tag's commit and nothing else moves.
- GIVEN a commit message, a `release.json` version or an archive digest that does not match THEN it exits 1 naming the
  check, and no ref moves.
- GIVEN `soakDays: 3` and a tag one day old THEN it exits 1 naming the remaining days; GIVEN three days THEN it promotes.
- GIVEN the channel at `plugins/v<B>` WHEN `plugins/v<A>` (older) is promoted THEN the channel points at A's commit.
- GIVEN a `channels` value that names `plugin-dist`, fails the branch-name rule or carries an unknown key THEN identity
  resolution exits 1 naming it.
- GIVEN `promote.yml` THEN its job names an environment and takes `tag` and `channel` inputs only.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-036 Admin templates per channel and per tag for Claude Code and Copilot (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-admin-templates`). The distribution builder renders managed-settings templates
for Claude Code and for Copilot under `admin/<client>/`: one per channel (`managed-settings.stable.json`, and
`managed-settings.canary.json` when a canary is configured) and one pinned to the release tag
(`managed-settings.tag.json`), each with a `.strict.json` twin. A channel template points the marketplace at the channel
and carries `autoUpdate: true` beside the source; a tag template pins the tag and carries no `autoUpdate`. Only the
`.strict` twin writes `strictKnownMarketplaces`, so the strict allowlist is the organization's choice of file, with no
build switch and no config key. The Copilot templates are the files an enterprise commits to its `.github-private`
repository as `copilot/managed-settings.json`: `extraKnownMarketplaces` with a `github` source (`repo`, `ref`),
`enabledPlugins`, per-entry `autoUpdate`, and `strictKnownMarketplaces` in the `.strict` twin; they write no key the
Copilot reference does not document. `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` stays for one compatibility window, byte-identical to
`admin/claude/managed-settings.tag.strict.json`, and goes at the next major release.

- **Units:** `u2-admin-templates`.
- **Evidence (before):** only `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` exists, pinned to the tag, with no `autoUpdate`, and
  `strictKnownMarketplaces` always written (`scripts/plugins/managed-settings.mjs:24-37`, `:100-112`); the builder writes
  it at `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:565-569`. Claude Code reads a managed `autoUpdate` beside the entry's
  `source` and locks the user's toggle (its plugins and org pages, read 2026-10-01). Copilot's enterprise managed
  settings are GA since 2026-07-01, legacy path `.github/copilot/settings.json` (research return, 2026-10-01).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`. A live install through an
  organization's Copilot settings needs a Copilot Business or Enterprise organization, which this project lacks; that
  leg is `Not done:` with its owner in file 3's route proofs.

Criteria:
- GIVEN a build for the canonical identity (no canary) THEN `admin/` holds exactly
  `claude/managed-settings.{tag,tag.strict,stable,stable.strict}.json`, the same four under `copilot/` and the legacy
  `claude-managed-settings.json` (equal to the Claude tag `.strict` file); with a canary configured, two more per client;
  a build of Cursor alone writes no `admin/`.
- GIVEN the Claude stable template THEN its keys are `extraKnownMarketplaces`, `enabledPlugins` and
  `requiredMinimumVersion` in that order, the marketplace entry is `{source: {source: "github", repo, ref:
  "plugin-stable"}, autoUpdate: true}`, `requiredMinimumVersion` is at or above 2.1.277, and there is no
  `strictKnownMarketplaces`.
- GIVEN any `.strict` twin THEN its last key is `strictKnownMarketplaces`, holding exactly one entry that equals the
  declared source field for field.
- GIVEN a tag template THEN its `ref` is `plugins/v<version>` and its entry carries no `autoUpdate`.
- GIVEN the Copilot stable template THEN its keys are exactly `extraKnownMarketplaces` and `enabledPlugins`, and its key
  set matches the vendor page the unit cites with its access date.
- GIVEN two builds of one commit THEN every template is byte-identical and the leak gate over `admin/` reports 0 hits.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-037 `plugin setup --declare <channel|tag>` writes the project's plugin declaration as ledger-owned keys (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-declare`). `plugin setup --declare <channel|tag>` writes
`extraKnownMarketplaces` and `enabledPlugins` into `.claude/settings.json` when Claude Code is set up (the Copilot CLI
reads it too) and into `.github/copilot/settings.json` when Copilot is set up (the Copilot CLI, the cloud agent and VS
Code's recommendations), with identical values in both. Each declared key is owned at its nested path — the JSON
Pointers `/extraKnownMarketplaces/<marketplace>` and `/enabledPlugins/<plugin>@<marketplace>` — through file 1's
per-key ownership, so sibling entries a client or another plugin wrote stay foreign, and a value the client had already
written before setup stays foreign too. The command refuses a repository whose `extraKnownMarketplaces` already declares
the marketplace, and a setup that selects neither Claude Code nor Copilot. `clean` removes exactly the declared keys.

- **Units:** `u2-declare`, which depends on `docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md (u1-settings-key-ownership)`.
- **Evidence:** `claude plugin install … --scope project` writes `enabledPlugins` alone (measured on 2.1.280,
  `docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:126-133`); Claude Code does not install a plugin whose catalog entry is an external
  source from project settings, so each contributor runs `claude plugin install <plugin>@<marketplace> --scope project`
  once (its plugins pages, read 2026-10-01).
- **Proof:** `test/cli/commands/pluginSetup.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/plugin.test.ts`,
  `test/merge/settingsKeyOwnership.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN `plugin setup --client claude,copilot --declare stable` THEN both files carry `extraKnownMarketplaces` naming the
  channel branch and `enabledPlugins` naming the plugin, and the ledger records the two pointers for each file.
- GIVEN `--declare plugins/v<version>` THEN the declared `ref` is that tag.
- GIVEN either file already holding `extraKnownMarketplaces.<marketplace>` THEN the command exits 1 naming the file and
  writes nothing.
- GIVEN `.claude/settings.json` holding only `enabledPlugins["<plugin>@<marketplace>"]: true` written by the client THEN
  setup succeeds, that key is not recorded, and a later `clean -y` leaves it in place.
- GIVEN a declared setup and a foreign key added later WHEN `clean -y` runs THEN only foreign content remains, a file the
  engine created with nothing foreign left is gone, and no `.bak` exists.
- GIVEN `--client cursor --declare stable` THEN the command exits 1 naming Claude Code and Copilot.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-038 Overrides, patches, packs and model pins reach plugin-mode clients (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-plugin-overrides`). Under a plugin-backed setup, an override or patch of a
plugin-owned artifact, and an agent with a `stamity config` model pin, are emitted as project-level copies. Each client
has a defined precedence between the project copy and the plugin's: Claude namespaces plugin artifacts
(`/stamity:<id>`), the Copilot CLI lets project skills shadow plugin skills, VS Code prefixes plugin skills
`/<plugin>:`. Installed pack skills are exempt from the plugin-owned filter on every client. `check` and `plugin status`
report, per client, what reaches it.

- **Units:** `u2-plugin-overrides`.
- **Evidence (before):** `ownedClassOfRow` reads only `artifactType` (`src/emit/ownership.ts:123-129`) and
  `withoutPluginOwnedRows` drops every owned class (`:147-161`); only Claude passes `packSkillPaths`
  (`src/adapters/claude.ts:557-622`; not codex `:496`, copilot `:327`, cursor `:514`); the plugin generator's synthetic
  manifest has no `models` (`scripts/generate-plugin-packages.mjs:490-501`) while pins are read from `ctx.manifest.models`
  (`src/adapters/claude.ts:692-693`); neither `check` nor `validate` reports it.
- **Proof:** adapter tests per client, `test/emit/ownership.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`; the precedence per
  client measured at execution and recorded with client versions.

Criteria:
- GIVEN a plugin-backed setup and a replacement override of a plugin-owned skill WHEN `sync` runs THEN each client gets a
  project-level copy at its skill path carrying the override's bytes.
- GIVEN a model pin for `stamity-reviewer` THEN each plugin-backed client that carries agents gets a project-level agent
  carrying the pinned model.
- GIVEN an installed pack skill THEN every client, plugin-backed or not, gets the pack's copy.
- GIVEN `check` and `plugin status --json` THEN each lists, per client, every override, patch and pinned agent with
  `reaches: true` or a reason, and the `plugin-duplicates` row does not flag these copies.
- GIVEN each client's measured precedence THEN the docs state which copy wins, with the client version measured.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-039 The review gate enforces on every client that has a blocking completion event (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-review-gate-all`). The review gate holds a run's completion on each client
whose completion event can hold it and whose round event carries the verdict it needs. The unit measures each client's
events and payloads first, Claude Code included: Claude Code (`Stop` beside `TaskCompleted`, which fires only through
the task tools — off by default on models newer than Opus 4.7 since Claude Code 2.1.233, per its hooks and tools pages),
the Copilot CLI and VS Code (`Stop`, `SubagentStop`), Cursor (`stop`, `subagentStop`), Codex (`Stop`). A client is wired
only when its completion event fired headless, a hold continued the agent, its round event carries the finishing agent
and its final text, and the run key matches between the two. A hold uses each client's own continue form (exit 2,
a JSON `decision: block`, or Cursor's `followup_message`), and a run is held at most three consecutive times without a
new recorded round, then released with the reason code `CONTINUATION_CAP`. Where no blocking event exists, the prose
ladder stays, and `check` and the capability matrix say so for that client.

- **Units:** `u2-review-gate-all` (after `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`, which captures the payloads).
- **Evidence (before):** the gate holds only Claude's `TaskCompleted`, with rounds counted on `SubagentStop`
  (`src/hooks/model.ts:161-189`; `REVIEW_GATE_COMPLETION_EVENTS`, `src/hooks/scripts.ts:1984`); Copilot's
  `subagentStop` is named and not wired.
- **Proof:** hook tests per adapter over captured, scrubbed payload fixtures; the measurement record per client, dated,
  with client versions.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the measurement record THEN each of the four clients has a row naming its completion and round events, whether a
  hold continued the agent, and the payload fields the gate reads, or the reason it stays on the prose ladder.
- GIVEN a wired client's captured round payload with `verdict: request-changes` and then its completion payload for the
  same run THEN the gate holds in that client's form; after a round with `verdict: approve` THEN the completion passes.
- GIVEN three consecutive held completions with no new round THEN the fourth passes with `CONTINUATION_CAP` on stderr.
- GIVEN a completion for a run with no recorded round THEN the gate passes and the state file is unchanged.
- GIVEN `check` on a repository selecting all four clients THEN a `review-gate` row names each client once, "enforced on
  <events>" or "prose ladder — <reason>", and the capability matrix shows the same.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-040 Plugin hooks filter themselves, and the Copilot root loads in VS Code (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`). Every hook decides from its own payload whether to
act, so it is correct on a client that ignores matchers: the portable runner applies a row's matcher to the payload's
tool name itself and skips a payload that names another event. The Claude-layout scripts also skip a run another client
started from `.claude/settings.json` — Cursor runs those hooks by default and the Copilot CLI reads them as a repository
source — identified only by a payload or environment signature that appears in every captured payload of that client
and in no captured Claude payload. The Copilot root loads in VS Code with `chat.plugins.enabled`, its hooks run and
filter there, and its README says so.

- **Units:** `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`.
- **Evidence (before):** VS Code's Local hooks ignore matcher values (its hooks page, read 2026-10-01);
  `src/adapters/copilot.ts:925-934` emits PascalCase events with matchers, and the runner reads no matcher
  (`src/hooks/portableRunner.ts:138-209`); `SessionEnd` is not a VS Code event; Cursor's third-party hooks setting is on
  by default (its hooks reference, read 2026-10-01); the root README says "VS Code is not on it (read 2026-09-20)"
  (`scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs:267-269`).
- **Proof:** runner and script tests over captured payload fixtures; one VS Code load walk on the maintainer's machine
  (VS Code is not installed there today: the `code` command opens Cursor, so the walk installs VS Code first).

Criteria:
- GIVEN the copilot runner and a row with matcher `Edit|Write` WHEN it receives a `bash` tool payload THEN it exits 0,
  writes nothing and never starts the hook; WHEN it receives an `edit` payload THEN the hook runs once.
- GIVEN a row for one event and a payload that names another THEN the runner exits 0 and writes nothing.
- GIVEN each captured Cursor payload and each captured Copilot import payload WHEN the four Claude-layout scripts run on it
  THEN each exits 0 with nothing on stdout and a stderr line naming the client; GIVEN each captured Claude payload THEN
  none skips, and the guard and the gate behave as before.
- GIVEN the Copilot root's hook configuration THEN every event key is in VS Code's documented list.
- `judgment: maintainer` · GIVEN VS Code with the Copilot root installed WHEN a session starts and a tool runs THEN the
  record names the VS Code version, the session target, the listed skills, the session-start hook line and the filtered
  matcher hook; a walk not performed is `Not done:` with its owner.
- GIVEN the Copilot root README THEN it no longer says VS Code is not supported.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-041 Cursor's rules are repository-owned in plugin mode, and Bugbot gets `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot`). In plugin mode, Cursor's rules are written to the
repository as `.cursor/rules/*.mdc` and leave the Cursor plugin root, so the IDE gets them once and the Cursor CLI reads
them. `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` is generated from the review rules in both modes, because Bugbot reads neither plugins nor
`.cursor/rules`.

- **Units:** `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot`.
- **Evidence (before):** Cursor's 12 rules live only inside the plugin (`scripts/plugins/clients/cursor.mjs:57-59`); the
  Cursor CLI reads `.cursor/rules`, `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md`, and plugin rules in the CLI are undocumented; Bugbot
  reads `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` (research return, 2026-10-01).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/pluginPackages.cursor.test.ts`, `test/adapters/cursor.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/plugin.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN `plugin setup --client cursor` THEN `.cursor/rules/` holds one `.mdc` file per Cursor rule, and the Cursor root's
  capability file declares `rule` as `repository-owned` with a reason.
- GIVEN a Cursor plugin build THEN its root carries no rule file.
- GIVEN generated or plugin mode with Cursor selected THEN `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` exists, derives from the review rules, and
  stays under the size limit the vendor page states, read at execution; the generator exits 1 above it.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-042 Plugin skills reach the bundled runtime before any network fallback (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`). In a plugin root, every body that runs the CLI calls
the bundled runtime first, as `node "<root>/runtime/locate.mjs" -- <verb>`, where `<root>` is the plugin root — the
nearest folder above the body that holds `stamity-plugin.json` (a client that sets its root variable in the shell names
the same folder; the Copilot CLI and Codex set none for a skill's shell). Only when the locator cannot run does the body
fall to `npx --no stamity <verb>`, then to the pinned `npx -y <package>@<version>` call, and then to the `Not done:` line
the everyday-flows spec requires when a CLI cannot run. The CLI-route bodies are unchanged.

- **Units:** `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`.
- **Evidence (before):** `st-learn`, `st-handoff`, `st-debug`, `st-work` and `st-quick` run `npx --no stamity …`, then
  `npx -y @zomarit/stamity@1.11.0 …`, else a `Not done: … CLI unavailable` note (`content/skills/st-learn/SKILL.md:50`
  and the sentence pinned at `test/corpus/cliCallForm.test.ts:32-33`); the four plugin roots reference the bundled
  locator nowhere; the locator runs the repository's own install when its version fits, else the bundled copy, and
  never the network (`scripts/plugins/locate.mjs:12-32`).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/pluginModules.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginLocate.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginPackages.*.test.ts`,
  `test/corpus/cliCallForm.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN each built plugin root THEN every body that runs the CLI names `runtime/locate.mjs` before any `npx` form.
- GIVEN a stub runtime, a `PATH` holding only Node and an unreachable registry WHEN the locator runs `learn capture`
  THEN the stub receives exactly that verb and the locator exits with the stub's status, fetching nothing.
- GIVEN the CLI-route bodies under `.claude/` THEN they are byte-identical to before, and `node dist/cli.js check`
  reports no drift.

   the container carries rules; a plugin-shipped body never writes a repository-owned file." New text: "The plugin owns
   agents, skills, commands and hooks, and rules only where the container carries rules and no requirement keeps them in

   file."

   support is additive." New text: "Plugin support is additive: plugin generation moves no byte of a CLI-only install,
