#### REQ-PLUGIN-028 One command sets a fork's identity (MODIFIED)

Replaces the Renovate-presets bullet as file 1 amended it (the one naming `--suffix`), and the bullet at `:1321`, "- regenerates the plugin and APM manifests." New
text:

- moves the three Renovate presets — `renovate/plugins.json`, `renovate/companion.json` and `renovate/apm.json` — and
  with `--suffix` writes the suffix-aware patterns into each (file 1's suffix rule, REQ-APM-015).
- regenerates the plugin and APM manifests and the default branch's per-client catalogs (REQ-PLUGIN-034).

(Amended `<date>`, plan 016 file 2, units `u2-apm-renovate-preset` and `u2-main-catalogs`; they read as quoted.)

Criteria:
- GIVEN `fork-identity.mjs --repository <fork>` THEN `renovate/apm.json` and the three default-branch catalogs name the
  fork, and a second run reports them unchanged.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-029 A Claude Code managed-settings template for an organization's rollout (MODIFIED)

Replaces `:1365-1371`, from "The distribution builder renders `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` from the fork's
identity:" through the four-key list ending "`requiredMinimumVersion` at least 2.1.277, the first client where an invalid
allowlist fails closed." New text:

The distribution builder renders Claude Code managed-settings templates from the fork's identity under `admin/claude/`,
one per channel and one pinned to the release tag, each with a `.strict` twin (REQ-PLUGIN-036):

- the company marketplace in `extraKnownMarketplaces`, at the channel with `autoUpdate: true`, or at the tag;
- the plugin on for everyone in `enabledPlugins`;
- `requiredMinimumVersion` at least 2.1.277, the first client where an invalid allowlist fails closed;
- in the `.strict` twin only, that marketplace alone admitted by `strictKnownMarketplaces`, as the last key.

The criterion at `:1377-1380` read "GIVEN any identity WHEN the template renders THEN it holds exactly the four keys in a
fixed order, …". New text: "GIVEN any identity WHEN a template renders THEN it holds `extraKnownMarketplaces`,
`enabledPlugins` and `requiredMinimumVersion` in that order, and its `.strict` twin adds `strictKnownMarketplaces` last;
the marketplace key and plugin id equal the rendered Claude catalog's, and an allowlist entry equals the declared
marketplace source field for field. An unequal pair blocks every marketplace for every user." (Amended `<date>`, plan 016
file 2, unit `u2-admin-templates`; it read as quoted.)

#### REQ-PLUGIN-004 Charter-reference substitution of tokens in plugin bodies (MODIFIED)

Replaces, in the paragraph "Amended 2026-09-30" (`docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:178-185`), the sentence "The tenth,
`${STAMITY:CLI}`, maps to a literal rather than a phrase: the pinned call `npx -y <package>@<plugin version>`, or
`npx --no <package>@<plugin version>` for a package with no npm channel, from inputs the plugin build passes in, so the
value is fixed at build time and never read off the consuming repository; a build that passes none leaves the token
unresolved and the body is refused". New text:

The tenth, `${STAMITY:CLI}`, maps to a literal rather than a phrase. In a plugin root it is the locator call
`node "<root>/runtime/locate.mjs" --`, and the running-CLI sentence of the four call sites is rewritten to name the
locator first, then `npx --no stamity`, then the pinned call `npx -y <package>@<plugin version>` (or
`npx --no <package>@<plugin version>` for a package with no npm channel) as the last fallback (REQ-PLUGIN-042). A caller
that passes no runtime — the APM generator (REQ-APM-011) — keeps the pinned call. The value is fixed at build time and
never read off the consuming repository; a build that passes no inputs leaves the token unresolved and the body is
refused. (Amended `<date>`, plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`; it read as quoted.)

### D. `docs/specs/enterprise-upstream-lane.md`

#### REQ-UPSTREAM-017 — Private bootstrap and operational recovery (MODIFIED)

Replaces `docs/specs/enterprise-upstream-lane.md:551-553`, "A private package has no npm channel, so the regenerated
marketplace entry carries a `github` source naming the repository instead of an npm package the fork never publishes
(`scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs`, its `marketplaceSource` binding)." New text:

The regenerated catalogs on the default branch point at the fork's own distribution tag (REQ-PLUGIN-034), so a private
package, which has no npm channel, gets entries naming its own repository and the release's `plugins/v<version>` tag —
never an npm package the fork never publishes, and never the source checkout's paths. (Amended `<date>`, plan 016 file 2,
unit `u2-main-catalogs`; it read as quoted.)

- **Evidence:** the checked-in entry's private-fork `github` source addresses the source checkout's `content/…` paths,
  which no `plugins/v*` tag satisfies (`docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:377-382`, ledger row build/18).

## Units

Measurements marked **(probe 2026-10-01)** were run by this plan's drafters with apm-cli 0.32.0 and 0.29.1 in scratch
Python virtual environments and with the clients installed on the maintainer's machine; nothing in the repository was
written. Each unit that rests on a measurement repeats it at execution and records it, dated, in the run record (after
Package 18, through `scripts/records.mjs`). No unit edits `content/**`, so no eval case `source:` range moves;
`u2-repo-file-waste`, `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode` and `u2-review-gate-all` change emission and re-sync the dogfood.

### u2-apm-sparse-measure — measure what an APM subfolder install fetches, and choose the folder or the orphan tag

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-apm-sparse-measure |
| `requirements` | REQ-APM-010 |
| `files` | none in the repository. The executing run's own record (`.stamity/runs/<run>/apm-fetch-measure.md`, published through plan 014 file 2's records flow, `scripts/records.mjs`); scratch venvs, consumers and caches live in the session scratchpad only |
| `interfaces` | **Why.** The distribution tree carries `apm.yml` + `.apm/` at its root today (`scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:9-15`, `:513`), and an install of `<slug>#plugins/v<ver>` vendors the whole tree (inbox row at `.stamity/inbox.md:347`: 2,882 files, 21.5 MiB at `plugins/v1.11.0`); the source tree costs about 20 MiB (`.stamity/inbox.md:348`; spec `docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:74-83`). **How apm-cli fetches a subfolder (source read 2026-10-01).** 0.32.0: a dependency `owner/repo/<path>#<ref>` is a virtual subdirectory package (`apm_cli/deps/github_downloader.py:1373` `download_subdirectory_package`). With the default persistent cache it uses a partial bare (`blob:none`) plus a sparse cone (`apm_cli/cache/git_cache.py:268-280`); with `APM_NO_CACHE=1` it uses a shared depth-1 bare clone (`apm_cli/deps/bare_cache.py:122-168`, `--depth=1 --branch <ref>`) materialized sparse; either way it vendors only the subfolder to `apm_modules/<owner>/<repo>/<path>/` (`apm_cli/models/dependency/materialization.py:158-170`). `--verbose` prints `[i] perf: materialize sparse=<yes\|no> size=<MB>`. Cache root: `APM_CACHE_DIR`, `APM_NO_CACHE=1` (`apm_cli/cache/paths.py:5-15`). 0.29.1 carries the same three paths (`deps/github_downloader.py:1363-1591`). **Preview (probe 2026-10-01, 0.32.0, `--target claude --verbose`, fresh `APM_CACHE_DIR` each run):** full distribution root `zomarit/stamity#plugins/v1.11.0` — cache 65,240 KB, `apm_modules` 29,800 KB / 2,883 files, exit 0; one-file subfolder `zomarit/stamity/admin#plugins/v1.11.0` — cache 9,480 KB, `materialize sparse=yes size=7.31 MB`, exit 1 (not a package; bytes still count); plugin-root subfolder `zomarit/stamity/claude#plugins/v1.11.0` — cache 16,572 KB, `apm_modules` 6,612 KB / 760 files, `sparse=yes size=12.39 MB`, exit 0; the same with `APM_NO_CACHE=1` — shared bare `--depth=1 --branch plugins/v1.11.0`, peak temp 17,812 KB. Finding: cone mode always materializes the root's top-level files, so the four `stamity-plugin-<client>-<ver>.zip` archives at the distribution root (about 7.3 MB) ride along with any subfolder; the vendored copy holds the subfolder only. **Procedure.** (1) Clients: `python3 -m venv <scratch>/apm-0.32.0 && <scratch>/apm-0.32.0/bin/pip install apm-cli==0.32.0`, the same for `0.29.1` (Python >= 3.10; CI pins 3.13, `ci.yml:409-413`; never inside the repository, `ci.yml:414-421`). (2) A `git` shim first on `PATH` that appends `git <args>` and the exit status to `$GIT_MEASURE_LOG` and runs the real git. (3) Arms, each in a fresh `git init` consumer whose `apm.yml` names one dependency, `apm install --target claude,copilot,cursor,codex --verbose`, a fresh `APM_CACHE_DIR`, then again with `APM_NO_CACHE=1` and a fresh `TMPDIR` sampled for peak size every 0.2 s: **T** `zomarit/stamity#v1.11.0`; **F** `zomarit/stamity#plugins/v1.11.0`; **S1** `zomarit/stamity/admin#plugins/v1.11.0` (the floor cost of any subfolder of today's distribution tree); **S2** `zomarit/stamity/claude#plugins/v1.11.0`; **L** a local fixture: `node scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs --out <scratch>/dist --runtime <scratch>/runtime` (runtime from `scripts/build-plugin-runtime.mjs`), then `apm.yml` and `.apm/` moved into `<scratch>/dist/apm/`, `git init` and one commit, consumer dependency `<scratch>/dist/apm` (declarative local route, the one CI uses, `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:42-51`). **D** (dual copy, local): L's tree with the package BOTH at its root and under `apm/`, `git init` and one commit, consumer dependency on the tree's root; its deployed file count must equal a root-only fixture's (the 1.11.0 layout) — every primitive once — for the root copy to stay as the compatibility alias of `u2-apm-slim-package` (verdict line `APM root copy: KEEP`, else `APM root copy: STOP`). **R** (optional, only on the maintainer's approval because it is outward-facing): a throwaway public repository `<maintainer>/stamity-apm-probe` holding L's tree at tag `plugins/v0.0.1`, measuring `…/apm#plugins/v0.0.1` end to end, plus a root `apm.yml` that declares only `dependencies: { apm: [<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v0.0.1] }` (the candidate redirect for consumers of the old `#plugins/v*` spec); deleted after. (4) Metrics per run: exit code; the `perf:` lines; `du -sk $APM_CACHE_DIR`; `du -sk` and file count of `apm_modules/`; deployed file count (lock `deployed_files` entries that are files); wall time; the shim's git lines (`--filter=blob:none`, `--depth=1`, `sparse-checkout set <path>`). (5) **Decision rule, declared before the run:** FOLDER (the package under `apm/` of the distribution tree, spec `<slug>/apm#plugins/v<ver>`) iff (a) with 0.32.0 in the default cache mode, S1's cache bytes are at most 25% of F's; (b) L deploys every class for every target on both clients; (c) 0.29.1 installs S2 through the subdirectory route (a sparse or a full clone is acceptable at the floor). Otherwise FALLBACK: an orphan tag `apm/v<ver>` whose tree holds only `apm.yml` and `.apm/`. With the preview numbers (9,480 / 65,240 = 14.5%) the rule picks FOLDER. (6) Record: one table (client, arm, cache mode, every metric), the verdict lines `APM slim route: FOLDER` or `APM slim route: FALLBACK` and `APM root copy: KEEP` or `APM root copy: STOP`, and the cone-mode residue; the same table enters the plan's spec delta for REQ-APM-010 beside the 2026-09-09 probe table (`docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:48-58`). **Follows:** `docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:48-58` (a dated probe table with client, route and result) |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** the procedure has run, **then** the record holds at least 14 measured rows (T, F, S1, S2 for 0.32.0 and 0.29.1 in the default cache mode, plus S1, S2 and F for both clients with `APM_NO_CACHE=1`), each carrying exit code, cache KB, `apm_modules` KB and file count, deployed file count and the `perf:` line or "no perf line". **Given** arm L's rows, **then** each lists per target the count of agents, prompts, instructions and skills deployed, every count above 0, on both clients. **Given** arm D's rows on both clients, **then** each deployed count equals the root-only fixture's and the record carries `APM root copy: KEEP`; a larger count carries `APM root copy: STOP`, and `u2-apm-slim-package` does not start until the maintainer chooses. **Given** the record's table, **when** rule (a) is recomputed with `node -e` from the two cache figures it names, **then** the result matches the verdict line. |
| `edgeCases` | A GitHub rate limit or a network failure → the run is repeated later; no row is ever estimated. 0.29.1 fails S2 → FALLBACK, unless (c) holds through another documented route the record names. S1 exits 1 because `admin/` is no package → expected; its bytes still count. A cache hit from an earlier run → impossible by construction, each run gets a fresh `APM_CACHE_DIR`. The shim never sees apm's git (apm resolves git elsewhere) → the `perf:` lines carry the verdict and the record says the shim saw nothing. Arm R not approved → the redirect stays unmeasured and is not built (a follow-up). |
| `depends_on` | none |
| `verify` | the record contains the table, the verdict line and the cone-mode note; `node -e` recomputation of rule (a) from the recorded S1 and F cache figures equals the verdict |

### u2-repo-file-waste — write Codex skill metadata only where Codex reads it, measure the other duplicates per reader, and document what stays

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-repo-file-waste |
| `requirements` | REQ-FLOW-033, REQ-FLOW-026 |
| `files` | `src/emit/skillsProjection.ts` (`projectSkills` `:297-366`; `nativeSkillRows` `:501-522`; `projectTouchpointSkills` `:563-593`); `src/adapters/cursor.ts` (the touchpoint call `:466-476`; the facts `citations` `:355-364`); `src/adapters/codex.ts` (the facts `citations` `:366-381`); `src/adapters/claude.ts` (the facts `citations` `:496-502`); `src/adapters/copilot.ts` (the facts `citations` `:228-262`; the command rows only if candidate C3 is admitted); `src/emit/planner.ts` (`AdapterDialectFacts` `:225-245`; `projectionReaders` `:840-842` only if candidate C5 is admitted); `src/emit/capabilityMatrix.ts` (`CapabilityMatrixInputs` `:94-131`; `requireCitations` `:437-457`; `clientSection` `:760-806`; `LIVE_CAPABILITY_INPUTS` `:396-407`; `renderCapabilityMatrixFrom` `:1101-1165`; new `RESIDUE_ROWS`, `residueSection`); `docs/capability-matrix.md` (regenerated); `test/emit/skillsProjection.test.ts` (`:66`, `:141`, `:147-156`); `test/emit/touchpointSkills.test.ts`; `test/emit/capabilityMatrix.test.ts`; `test/emit/crossClientGoldens.test.ts` (a new refresh-ledger entry at `:186`) and `test/emit/__snapshots__/crossClientGoldens.test.ts.snap` (regenerated); `test/ci/pluginPackages.cursor.test.ts` (`:409-410`); `test/emit/repoFileCensus.test.ts` (new); `.claude/skills/{st-browser-evidence,st-dep-audit,st-design-system-detect,st-handoff,st-learn,st-onboard,st-qa,st-verify}/agents/openai.yaml` (deleted by the dogfood sync) and `.stamity/manifest.json`; `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** `projectSkills(ctx, options)` (`src/emit/skillsProjection.ts:297`) projects every regular file of each admitted skill through `projectOneSkill` (`:765-803`), so each of the eight bundled skills that ships `content/skills/<id>/agents/openai.yaml` (st-browser-evidence, st-dep-audit, st-design-system-detect, st-handoff, st-learn, st-onboard, st-qa, st-verify) lands in `.agents/skills/<id>/agents/openai.yaml`, owned by the selected shared-tree readers (`src/emit/planner.ts:840-865`). `nativeSkillRows(rows, tool, demoted)` (`:501-522`) re-targets those core rows into `.claude/skills/` for claude, companions included. `TOUCHPOINT_POLICY_FILE = "agents/openai.yaml"` (`:529`) and `TOUCHPOINT_POLICY = "policy:\n  allow_implicit_invocation: false\n"` (`:537`); `projectTouchpointSkills(commands, ctx)` (`:563-593`) returns a `SKILL.md` row and a policy row per command, and both cursor (`src/adapters/cursor.ts:469`) and codex (`src/adapters/codex.ts:406`) emit them as their own, so the composer merges each file with two owners. Measured 2026-10-01 at `88fcfd32` (the planning run's native probe): `init -y --tools claude,cursor,copilot` writes 181 files, 25 of them `agents/openai.yaml` (17 under `.agents/skills/`: 8 bundled + 9 touchpoints; 8 under `.claude/skills/`); single-client counts claude 8, copilot 8, cursor 17, codex 17. **Vendor facts read 2026-10-01:** Codex reads repository skills only from `.agents/skills` (`$CWD/.agents/skills`, `$CWD/../.agents/skills`, `$REPO_ROOT/.agents/skills`), and `agents/openai.yaml` is its optional skill metadata (`interface`, `policy.allow_implicit_invocation`, `dependencies`) — https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/build-skills (the target of developers.openai.com/codex/skills); Cursor loads project skills from `.agents/skills/`, `.cursor/skills/`, `.claude/skills/` (compatibility) and `.codex/skills/` (compatibility), and its page never mentions `agents/openai.yaml` — https://cursor.com/docs/skills. **Step 1 — certain waste (no census).** `const codexSelected = ctx.manifest.tools.includes("codex")`. In `projectSkills`, a row of a corpus, fork or override skill whose path relative to its skill directory equals `TOUCHPOINT_POLICY_FILE` is dropped when `!codexSelected` (installed-pack skill rows are projected verbatim by the pack lane and stay untouched). `projectTouchpointSkills(commands, ctx, opts: { policy: boolean })` emits the policy row only when `opts.policy`; codex passes `{ policy: true }`, cursor passes `{ policy: false }`, so with tools `[cursor, codex]` the `SKILL.md` stays co-owned and the companion is codex's alone (the spec delta's REQ-FLOW-026 amendment: "Codex alone owns it"). `nativeSkillRows` never emits `<dir>/agents/openai.yaml` into `.claude/skills/` (Codex never reads that tree; see Concerns C6). **Step 2 — the reader census.** `AdapterDialectFacts` (`src/emit/planner.ts:225-245`) gains `locationReaders: readonly LocationReader[]` with `interface LocationReader { location: string; surface: string; reads: "yes" \| "no" \| "unverified"; note?: string; citation: { url: string; accessDate: string } }`. Locations: `.agents/skills/`, `.claude/skills/`, `.codex/skills/`, `.claude/rules/`, `.github/instructions/`, `.github/prompts/`, `agents/openai.yaml beside a SKILL.md`. Surfaces: claude → `Claude Code`; cursor → `Cursor IDE`, `Cursor CLI`; copilot → `VS Code`, `Copilot CLI`, `Copilot cloud agent`, `Visual Studio`, `JetBrains`, `Eclipse`, `Xcode`; codex → `Codex CLI`. A page silent on a location is `unverified`, never `no`. `requireCitations` (`src/emit/capabilityMatrix.ts:437-457`) checks these citations too (non-empty URL, ISO date). Pages to read on the run date, recording the URL actually read: Claude Code `https://code.claude.com/docs/en/skills`, `/memory`, `/slash-commands`; Cursor `https://cursor.com/docs/skills`, `https://cursor.com/docs/context/rules`; VS Code `https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/copilot/customization/agent-skills`, `/custom-instructions`, `/prompt-files`; GitHub `https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/concepts/agents/about-agent-skills` and the per-IDE support matrix `https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/custom-instructions-support`; Codex `https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/build-skills`. **Step 3 — the decision rule, per candidate:** a copy at location L is dropped for a selection S only when every surface in S that reads L also reads another location S still writes with the same artifact id, no surface in S has L as its only source, and no reading involved is `unverified`. **C3** Copilot's `.github/prompts/st-*.prompt.md` when cursor or codex is selected (the nine touchpoints also sit in `.agents/skills/st-*`): admitted only if every Copilot surface that reads `.github/prompts/` reads `.agents/skills/` and starts a `disable-model-invocation: true` skill by `/name`; code if admitted: the copilot adapter skips its command rows while `manifest.tools` includes cursor or codex. Expected: residue (the IDEs read prompt files). **C4** codex's rule-skills in the shared tree: admitted only if a repository location exists that Codex reads and Cursor and no Copilot surface reads; Codex reads only `.agents/skills` and Cursor also reads `.codex/skills/` → residue (the existing disclosure `sharedTreeDuplicateRules`, `src/emit/capabilityMatrix.ts:392`, stays). **C5** bundled skills in both `.claude/skills/` and `.agents/skills/` when claude and a shared-tree reader are selected: admitted for a selection only if every surface of every selected shared-tree reader reads `.claude/skills/`; Codex never does, Cursor does; code if admitted: in `src/emit/planner.ts:840-842` a tool whose every `locationReaders` row for `.claude/skills/` reads `yes` leaves `projectionReaders` while claude is selected, so the `.agents/skills/` copies of non-pack `skill` rows lose that owner (touchpoint rows, which are `command` rows owned by cursor and codex, are unaffected). **C6** `.claude/rules/` and `.github/instructions/` (VS Code reads both): never admitted (Claude Code's rule location is `.claude/rules/`; the Copilot CLI, cloud agent and IDEs read `.github/instructions/`) → residue, naming a user-side VS Code setting if the census finds one. **Rendering.** `src/emit/capabilityMatrix.ts` gains `export interface ResidueRow { selection: string; copies: string; readers: string; why: string }` and `export const RESIDUE_ROWS: readonly ResidueRow[]` (no ISO date literal: this module carries none by contract, `:372-374`), passed as `residue` on `CapabilityMatrixInputs` and rendered as `## Repository-file residue` between `## Always-on cost by client` and `## Plugin containers`; each `### <tool>` client section gains a "Locations read" table built from `locationReaders` with "accessed <date>". Regenerate with `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs`. **CHANGELOG** `### Changed` under `## [Unreleased]`: "**Codex skill metadata is written only where Codex reads it.** A setup without Codex no longer writes `agents/openai.yaml` beside each skill and touchpoint (25 files in a Claude, Cursor and Copilot setup), and Claude's own skills tree never carries it; the next sync removes the old copies. The duplicates that stay, and why, are a new section of the capability matrix." **Follows:** `src/emit/skillsProjection.ts:470-522` (`nativeSkillRows`, a filter over the shared projection that drops a copy no reader of that root needs) and `src/emit/capabilityMatrix.ts:307-358` (`REVISIT_TRIGGERS`, declared rows a test pins to the adapters' declarations) |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** tools `[claude, cursor, copilot]` over the golden fixture, **when** emission is planned, **then** no planned path ends with `/agents/openai.yaml` and the planned file count equals 156 (181 − 25), asserted in `test/emit/repoFileCensus.test.ts` beside the 181 baseline; if C5 is admitted the test pins the lower count and names the census rows it follows from. **Given** tools `[codex, cursor]`, **then** each of the nine touchpoint directories and each of the eight bundled skill directories under `.agents/skills/` carries `agents/openai.yaml`, the touchpoint companions are owned by codex alone, and no `.claude/skills/*/agents/openai.yaml` is planned. **Given** a repository synced with `[codex, cursor]`, **when** codex is deselected and `sync` runs, **then** every `agents/openai.yaml` is reclaimed and each touchpoint `SKILL.md` is byte-identical (`test/emit/touchpointSkills.test.ts`). **Given** `renderCapabilityMatrix()`, **then** the page holds `## Repository-file residue` with one row per `RESIDUE_ROWS` entry and every client section lists its `locationReaders` with an ISO access date; `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs` rewrites `docs/capability-matrix.md` and `npx vitest run test/emit/capabilityMatrix.test.ts` passes its byte-compare. **Given** a residue row claiming a surface reads two locations, **then** a test finds both claims as `reads: "yes"` rows of that client's `locationReaders`. **Given** a `locationReaders` row with `accessDate: "2026-13-01"`, **then** `renderCapabilityMatrixFrom` throws naming the client and the URL. **Re-pins** (each with a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` comment naming this unit): `test/emit/skillsProjection.test.ts:66` `VERIFY_FILES` loses `"agents/openai.yaml"` for the default context (no codex) and `:141`'s length follows it; the companion assertions at `:147-156` move into a context whose tools include codex; `test/ci/pluginPackages.cursor.test.ts:410` `expect(companions).toHaveLength(9)` → `0`; the cross-client snapshot regenerates with `npx vitest run test/emit/crossClientGoldens.test.ts --update` and the refresh-ledger entry names, per selection, the removed paths (claude, copilot and cursor lose their companions; all-four and codex lose only the `.claude/skills/` ones). **Given** `npm test -- --coverage`, **then** `src/emit/skillsProjection.ts` holds its floor (statements 98, branches 90, functions 100, lines 100, `vitest.config.ts:149-175`) with both branches of the codex check exercised. |
| `edgeCases` | An installed pack skill that ships `agents/openai.yaml` → projected verbatim by the pack lane, unchanged. A user override under `.stamity/overrides/skills/<dir>/agents/openai.yaml` → follows the corpus rule (dropped without codex). Plugin roots: the claude and copilot roots lose `skills/*/agents/openai.yaml` (8 each) because their builds plan with `tools: [client]` (`scripts/generate-plugin-packages.mjs:488-513`); the codex root keeps all of them; `scripts/plugins/clients/cursor.mjs:64-70`'s companion branch becomes unreachable in the build and stays as a guard. The APM package keeps `.apm/skills/*/agents/openai.yaml` (an APM consumer with the codex target reads it; a follow-up row records the residue for other targets). A census page unreachable on the run date → that row stays `unverified` and blocks the drop it bears on. |

json? }> }` (`src/cli/kit/program.ts:112-125`); the funnel registers `--json`, `-y`, and `--dry-run` for `mutating` modules and wraps the payload with `ok`, `command`, `version` (`:26-62`, `:270-291`). `pinnedCliPrefix(packageName, version, { npmChannel })` (`src/shared/cliCall.ts:92-108`) refuses a name outside the npm grammar and a version that is not semver. `packageName()`, `hasNpmChannel()`, `resolveOwnPackageFacts()` (`src/cli/kit/packageName.ts:141`, `:160`, `:92`) answer the running package's name, channel and `publishConfig.registry`. `detectPackageManager(rootDir)` (`src/detect/packageManager.ts:67`) → `{ name: "bun" \| "pnpm" \| "yarn" \| "npm"; lockfile: string \| null; fromPackageJsonField }`. `syncJsonPayload` (`src/cli/commands/sync/report.ts:314-365`) carries `dryRun`, `entries[{ path, action: "create" \| "update" \| "unchanged" \| "collision", adapter, artifactId }]`, `refused`, `reclaim`, `wrote`, `provenance`, `pluginOwned` and no client set. `updatePathHelp()` (`src/cli/commands/sync.ts:62-72`) ends "no separate update command exists". **New, sync payload:** `syncJsonPayload` gains `tools: [...plan.manifest.tools]`, the post-migration client set (additive). **New, the verb** (`src/cli/commands/upgrade.ts`): `export const upgradeCommand: CommandModule = { name: "upgrade", summary: "move this setup to another release: run that release's sync and move the package pin", mutating: true, configure(cmd) { cmd.requiredOption("--to <version>", "the exact release to move to (no range, no tag)"); cmd.option("--allow-client-change", "proceed when that release would change the selected clients"); cmd.option("--allow-downgrade", "proceed when --to is older than the release that wrote this setup"); cmd.option("--allow-major", "proceed when --to is another major release"); }, run }`, and a pure orchestration the tests drive: `export async function runUpgrade(input: UpgradeInput, deps: UpgradeDeps): Promise<UpgradeReport>` with `interface UpgradeInput { rootDir: string; to: string; allowClientChange: boolean; allowDowngrade: boolean; allowMajor: boolean; dryRun: boolean; packageName: string; npmChannel: boolean; registry: string \| null; runningVersion: string }`, `interface ExecResult { status: number \| null; stdout: string; stderr: string }`, `interface UpgradeDeps { exec(command: "npm" \| "npx", args: readonly string[], cwd: string): Promise<ExecResult>; readFile(path: string): Promise<string \| null>; writeFile(path: string, text: string): Promise<void>; packageManager(rootDir: string): Promise<{ name: string; lockfile: string \| null }> }`, `interface UpgradeReport { from: string; to: string; noop: boolean; dryRun: boolean; clientSet: { before: string[]; after: string[] \| null }; changed: string[]; reclaimed: string[]; refused: string[]; pin: { field: "devDependencies" \| "dependencies"; from: string; to: string } \| null; pinNote: string \| null; lockfileStale: string \| null; next: string \| null }`. **Order** (every refusal is an `EngineError` with `code: "VALIDATION_ERROR"`, exit 1, written nothing, its message naming the flag that would allow it): (1) `readManifest(rootDir)` null → "this repository has no stamity setup; run `<packageCommand("init")>` first"; `from` = `manifest.generatedBy` when semver-valid, else `runningVersion`. (2) `pinnedCliPrefix(packageName, to, { npmChannel })` validates `--to`; a `+build` suffix is refused. `semver.eq(to, from)` → `noop: true`, no exec, exit 0. `semver.lt(to, from)` without `--allow-downgrade` → "--to <to> is older than <from>, the release that wrote this setup; re-run with --allow-downgrade". `semver.major(to) !== semver.major(from)` without `--allow-major` → "…; re-run with --allow-major". (3) `npmChannel === false` → "this package has no npm channel, so npx cannot fetch <name>@<to>: install it into this project, then run `npx --no <name> sync`". `registry` non-null and not parsing with `new URL()` to the protocol `http:` or `https:` → "publishConfig.registry <registry> is not an http(s) URL; fix it in the package's package.json" (the self-read checks only that it is a non-empty string, `src/cli/kit/packageName.ts:100-110`). (4) `exec("npm", ["view", `${name}@${to}`, "version", "--json", ...(registry ? ["--registry", registry] : [])], rootDir)`; a non-zero status or stdout other than the JSON string `"<to>"` → "<name>@<to> is not published at <registry, or the registry npm is configured to use for this scope>". (5) Preflight `exec("npx", ["-y", `${name}@${to}`, "sync", "--dry-run", "--json"], rootDir)`; stdout is parsed as one JSON document; `command !== "sync"` or `version !== to` → "npx ran <version> instead of <to>"; `ok === false` → the target's own `error.message`. `before` = `manifest.tools`; `after` = the envelope's `tools` when it is a string array, else `null`. `after === null` without `--allow-client-change` → "the <to> release does not report its client set; review `npx -y <name>@<to> sync --dry-run`, then re-run with --allow-client-change"; `after` differs from `before` as a set without the flag → "the <to> release would change the clients from <before> to <after>; re-run with --allow-client-change". (5a) Prepare the pin, before any sync: `package.json` at `rootDir` read when present; the dependency located by its key `<name>` in the parsed document, under `devDependencies`, else `dependencies`; an exact semver spec → `to`; `^x.y.z` or `~x.y.z` → the same operator with `to`; any other spec → unchanged, `pinNote: "package.json pins <name> as <spec>; left as written"`. The edited text replaces exactly the quoted JSON spelling of the current value once, searched from the end of the quoted key `"<name>"` that follows the section's own quoted key (`"devDependencies"` or `"dependencies"`), so a top-level `"version"` spelled the same is never the match; the result is re-parsed and must equal the parsed original with only that value changed (the technique of `retargetPreset`, `scripts/fork-identity.mjs:197-214`, whose first-match search is safe only because its refusal comes before every write). A key or value the search cannot find, or a re-parse that differs → "package.json: the <name> pin could not be rewritten in place, so nothing ran; write it as a plain JSON member and re-run". (6) `--dry-run` → report `changed` = preflight entries with action `create` or `update`, `reclaimed` = preflight `reclaim` candidates' paths, `refused` = entries with action `collision`, and `pin` and `pinNote` as step 5a prepared them; exit 0, nothing written. (7) Apply `exec("npx", ["-y", `${name}@${to}`, "sync", "--json"], rootDir)`; a non-zero status or a non-empty `refused` → exit 1 after reporting, and no pin move. (8) Pin: when step 5a prepared a moved pin, `writeFile` writes exactly the prepared text — no second read and no second search — so every other byte survives. When the pin moved: `lockfileStale` = the detected lockfile, `next` = `"<npm \| pnpm \| yarn \| bun> install"`. (9) Human output: one line per step result, `changed N file(s), reclaimed M`, the pin line, `next: <command>` then `git diff`. **Spawn:** npm runs as the JavaScript program it is, as `runNpm` runs it (`scripts/plugins/runtime.mjs:244-274`), never through a shell while an entry resolves. **New, `src/shared/npmEntry.ts`** (node builtins only; `PLAN_MAP` `"src/shared/npmEntry.ts": { unit: "u2-upgrade-verb", wave: 1 }`): `export function npmJsEntry(command: "npm" \| "npx", probe: { execPath: string; npmExecpath: string \| undefined; exists(path: string): boolean } = { execPath: process.execPath, npmExecpath: process.env.npm_execpath, exists: existsSync }): string \| null` — `runNpm`'s two probes (`:226-242`): `npmExecpath` when it names an existing `npm-cli.[cm]?js` (another manager's entry is ignored), answering it for `npm` and its sibling `npx-cli.js` for `npx`; then `node_modules/npm/bin/<npm\|npx>-cli.js` beside `execPath` and `../lib/node_modules/npm/bin/<npm\|npx>-cli.js`; else `null`. `runtime.mjs` keeps its own copy: it runs before `dist/` exists, and `files: ["dist"]` ships no script (`package.json:41-43`). Production `exec` = `createExec({ platform: process.platform, entry: npmJsEntry, spawn })`, exported from `upgrade.ts`: with an entry it runs `spawn(process.execPath, [entry, ...args], { cwd, shell: false })` on every platform. Only when both probes miss does it fall back to the PATH name — `<cmd>` with `shell: false` off Windows; on win32 `<cmd>.cmd` with `shell: true`, since Node 18.20.2 / 20.12.2 (CVE-2024-27980) spawning a `.cmd` without a shell throws `EINVAL` — and in that win32 fallback, because cmd.exe takes the argv unquoted (`runtime.mjs:248-253`), an argument containing a space or one of `&\|<>^%"` → a `VALIDATION_ERROR` refusal naming it, before spawning. Every argument the verb passes is validated before it gets there: the package grammar and semver (`pinnedCliPrefix`), literals, and `registry` (step 3). **Exit:** 0 done, no-op or dry run; 1 refusal or a failed target sync; 2 a missing `--to` (commander). **JSON** (`--json`): the `UpgradeReport` fields under the funnel's envelope. **Surface pins this verb moves:** (1) `src/cli.ts` — import, `upgradeCommand` directly after `syncCommand` in `COMMANDS`, the docblock's "thirteen CommandModules" → "fourteen", "Ten advertised, thirteen registered" → "Eleven advertised, fourteen registered", and its help-order list; (2) `test/cli/surface.e2e.test.ts` — `ADVERTISED` gains `"upgrade"` after `"sync"`, "registers exactly 13" and `.toBe(13)` → 14, "--help lists exactly the 10 advertised commands" → 11; (3) `test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts` `PLAN_MAP` `"src/cli/commands/upgrade.ts": { unit: "u2-upgrade-verb", wave: 14 }` (its imports sit at waves 1, 2, 4 and 13: `shared/cliCall` and `shared/npmEntry`, `detect/packageManager`, `manifest/manifest`, the kit) and `"src/shared/npmEntry.ts": { unit: "u2-upgrade-verb", wave: 1 }` beside the other shared leaves (`:261-269`); (4) `docs/cli-reference.md` regenerated with `node scripts/generate-docs.mjs --page cli` (byte-compared by `test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts`); (5) `test/docsPages.test.ts` README array `:1176-1193` gains `"upgrade"` after `"sync"`, `toHaveLength(10)` → 11, `"ten verbs"` → `"eleven verbs"`; (6) `test/docsPages.test.ts` getting-started array `:2449-2462` gains `"upgrade"`; (7) `README.md:78-80` — the `·` list in CLI order and "ten verbs" → "eleven verbs"; beyond the learning's seven: (8) `src/cli/docs/llmsIndex.ts:115` "the ten verbs" → "the eleven verbs" and `llms.txt` regenerated with `node scripts/generate-docs.mjs --page llms`; (9) `docs/getting-started.md` `## The ten verbs` (`:197`), its `·` list (`:199-200`), the glossary row (`:395`) and the manifest bullet (`upgrade` needs the manifest); (10) `updatePathHelp()` and its two pins: channel line "`update = npx -y <name>@<version> upgrade --to <version> — runs that release's sync and moves the package.json pin; npx -y <name>@<version> sync regenerates without moving it.`", no-channel line "`update = install the newer release into this project, then npx --no <name> sync — upgrade needs an npm channel, which this package does not have.`"; (11) `docs/getting-started.md` "Keeping your setup current" (`:366-376`) leads with `npx -y @zomarit/stamity@<current> upgrade --to <version>` and keeps the sync line. **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**`stamity upgrade --to <version>` moves a setup to another release in one step.** It checks the release exists in the package's own registry, previews that release's sync, refuses a changed client set, a downgrade or a major jump unless told otherwise, runs the sync through the pinned `npx` call, moves the `package.json` pin, and lists what changed (`--json` for an orchestrator)." **Follows:** `src/cli/commands/sync.ts:139-168` (a thin `CommandModule` over an engine function the tests drive) and `src/cli/commands/plugin/probe.ts:125-200` (a spawn behind a seam with a bounded, total outcome) |
| `testCriteria` | All in `test/cli/commands/upgrade.test.ts` with a stub `exec` unless named. **Given** a fixture setup written by 1.12.0 with tools `["claude"]` and `devDependencies["@zomarit/stamity"] = "1.12.0"`, a stub `npm view` answering `"1.13.0"`, a preflight and an apply envelope `{ ok: true, command: "sync", version: "1.13.0", tools: ["claude"], entries: [{ path: "AGENTS.md", action: "update" }], reclaim: [] }`, **when** `runUpgrade({ to: "1.13.0" })` runs, **then** `exec` was called exactly three times with the argv of steps 4, 5 and 7 in order, `report.changed` equals `["AGENTS.md"]`, `package.json` reads `"1.13.0"` and differs from the original in that one value only. **Given** `--to 1.11.0`, **then** exit 1, the message contains `--allow-downgrade`, `exec` never ran. **Given** `--to 2.0.0`, **then** exit 1 with `--allow-major` and no exec. **Given** a preflight `tools: ["claude", "codex"]`, **then** exit 1 naming both sets; with `allowClientChange` the apply runs. **Given** a preflight envelope without `tools`, **then** exit 1 unless `allowClientChange`. **Given** envelope `version: "1.12.9"` for `--to 1.13.0`, **then** exit 1 "npx ran 1.12.9". **Given** `npm view` exit 1 with `publishConfig.registry` set, **then** exit 1 and the message names that registry. **Given** `--dry-run`, **then** `writeFile` is never called, the apply exec never runs, and `report.pin` equals the move step 5a prepared. **Given** a `package.json` whose top-level `version` is `"1.12.0"` before `devDependencies["@zomarit/stamity"] = "1.12.0"`, **then** the upgrade moves only the dependency value and `version` still reads `"1.12.0"`. **Given** the dependency key spelled with a JSON escape (`"@zomarit\/stamity": "1.12.0"`), **then** exit 1 with `VALIDATION_ERROR` naming the pin, `exec` called exactly twice (view, preflight), and `writeFile` never called. **Given** `"^1.12.0"`, **then** it becomes `"^1.13.0"`; **given** `"latest"`, **then** unchanged and `pinNote` names it; **given** a `package-lock.json`, **then** `lockfileStale` is `"package-lock.json"` and `next` is `"npm install"`. **Given** `npmChannel: false`, **then** exit 1 before any exec. **Given** `--to` equal to `generatedBy`, **then** exit 0, `noop: true`, no exec. **Given** the apply reports `refused: [".claude/settings.json"]`, **then** exit 1 and `package.json` is unchanged. **Given** learnings, handoffs, overrides and run records in the fixture, **then** every one is byte-identical afterwards (the verb writes only `package.json`; the stub sync writes nothing). **Given** `createExec` over a stub `spawn` and an entry resolver that answers, on `platform` `"win32"` and `"linux"` alike, **then** `npx` spawns `process.execPath` with `[<…>/npx-cli.js, ...args]` and `npm` with `[<…>/npm-cli.js, ...args]`, both with `shell: false`. **Given** a resolver that answers `null` on `win32`, **then** a validated argv spawns `npx.cmd` with `shell: true`, and **when** `runUpgrade` runs over that exec with a registry containing `&` (`https://r.example/a&b`) or a space, **then** exit 1 with `VALIDATION_ERROR` and the stub `spawn` never called. **Given** `registry` `"file:///srv/npm"` or `"not a url"`, **then** exit 1 before any exec. **Given** `npmJsEntry` with `npmExecpath` naming an existing `npm-cli.js`, **then** `npm` resolves to it and `npx` to its sibling `npx-cli.js`; **with** `npmExecpath` naming another manager's entry, **then** the copy beside `execPath` answers; **with** neither present, **then** `null`. **Given** the built CLI, **when** `stamity upgrade --help` runs, **then** exit 0 and the four flags are listed; `stamity upgrade` without `--to` exits 2. **Given** a sync run, **then** `syncJsonPayload(...).tools` equals the manifest's tools (`test/cli/commands/sync.test.ts`). **Given** the surface suites, **then** `npx vitest run test/cli/surface.e2e.test.ts test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts test/cli/docs/llmsIndex.test.ts test/cli/commands/sync.test.ts` exits 0 with the pins above moved, each with a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` line. |
| `edgeCases` | No `package.json` → `pin: null`, `pinNote: "no package.json; the pin lives in the npx call your scripts or orchestrator run"`, nothing created. A `package.json` without the dependency → `pin: null`. A pin present but not rewritable in place (its key or value spelled with a JSON escape, or a re-parse that would change another member) → the step-5a refusal, exit 1, before the apply sync and before any write. A workspace monorepo → only the root `package.json` is read; the note says so. A dirty tree → the target sync's own dirty-tree warning passes through on stderr. A registry that needs credentials → npm reads the user's `.npmrc`; a 401 is the step-4 refusal. A fork named `@acme/stamity` with `publishConfig.registry` → `npm view @acme/stamity@<to> --registry <url>` and `npx -y @acme/stamity@<to>`. A downgrade allowed past a manifest-schema change → the older sync refuses the newer manifest and its error is reported verbatim, exit 1. |

### u2-main-catalogs — the default branch carries one catalog per client that installs the full release root

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-main-catalogs |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-034 (and, at Prove, the spec delta's MODIFIED REQ-PLUGIN-028 bullet, REQ-UPSTREAM-017 and invariant 2 at `docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:57-58`) |
| `files` | `scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs` (header `:29-36`; new exports after `:290`), `scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs` (header `:47-54`, `:110-123`; `:261-279` `isPrivate`; `:380-413` tarball helpers; `:439-496` the marketplace block; `:541-546` `MANIFESTS`), `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` (regenerated), `.github/plugin/marketplace.json` (new, generated), `.agents/plugins/marketplace.json` (new, generated), `scripts/plugins/clients/codex.mjs` (`renderReadme` `:183-187` only), `docs/plugins.md` (the `--ref` paragraph `:267-277` only), `CONTRIBUTING.md` (`:209` only), `test/ci/pluginManifests.test.ts` (`:165-172`, `:211-231`, `:337-353`, `:431-512`), `test/ci/forkIdentity.test.ts` (`:264-306`), `test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts` (`:213-226`), `test/ci/pluginPackages.codex.test.ts` (`:649-654`), `test/docsPages.test.ts` (the comment of the case at `:2392-2403` only), `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Why `main` is npm-sourced today, and what that protected.** c458bf45 (2026-08-26) added the four root manifests when they were the only plugin route; the source was npm because "npm is this package's published channel … the tarball is what trusted publishing signs and what a user already installs" (`scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs:442-444`). The distribution tree (`plugin-dist`, `plugins/v*`) arrived 2026-09-17 (cba661df) and froze the root files rather than re-deciding them (`docs/plans/008-plugin-lifecycle-01.md:61-62`). 9127b70d (2026-09-19) made a private package's entry `{source:'github', repo}` with checkout paths and no ref (`:461-463`), because an npm source on a private copy "advertises a package name that resolves to nothing (or, worse, to somebody else's package of that name)" (`:446-450`). What the npm root delivers: the tarball (`files: ["dist"]`) has no `hooks/`, no `runtime/`, no `stamity-plugin.json`, and 11 declared bodies still carry `${STAMITY:*}` tokens (compared with `origin/plugin-dist:claude/`, research return 2026-10-01); Codex without `--ref` falls back to it and installs that (walked 2026-09-24, `scripts/plugins/clients/codex.mjs:183-187`); the Copilot CLI reads `marketplace.json`, `.plugin/marketplace.json`, `.github/plugin/marketplace.json`, then `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` (docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-plugin-reference), so it lands on the same file. **Kept from the old decision:** (1) a ref-less add installs a released, immutable, pinned artifact — now the release root at `plugins/v<version>`, a tag the release step refuses to move (`.github/workflows/release.yml:977-983`); (2) a private fork's catalog never names a package or source it does not publish — every entry addresses the identity's own repository, whose tag the fork's `fork-release.yml` publishes (`.github/workflows/fork-release.yml:671-751`); (3) `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, `.cursor-plugin/plugin.json` and `plugin.json` keep their bytes (the APM 0.29.0 witness job installs a clean export that carries them, `.github/workflows/ci.yml:448-450`; `docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:101-103`); (4) every catalog is generated from `package.json` and byte-checked (`ci.yml:327`, `:714`, `fork-release.yml:321`). **Current code.** `scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs:163-165` Claude default `{source:'git-subdir', url, path:'claude', ref: tag}`; `:256-270` `codexSource` returns `{source:'local', path:'./codex'}` when the URL is the identity's own (`:265`), right inside the tree and wrong on `main`, which has no `codex/`; `:222-237` the Copilot entry is the relative path `./copilot` because the object form's keys were "not stated on the pages read" (2026-09-20, `:29-35`). The reference now documents it: `{"source":"github","repo":"owner/repo","ref":"v1.0.0","path":"plugins/my-plugin"}` (cli-plugin-reference, read 2026-10-01). Cursor catalogs take only a relative directory path (`:22-28`), so `main` cannot carry a Cursor catalog that reaches a tag. **New exports in `scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs`:** `export const DEFAULT_BRANCH_CATALOG_PATHS = { claude: '.claude-plugin/marketplace.json', copilot: '.github/plugin/marketplace.json', codex: '.agents/plugins/marketplace.json' }` and `export function renderDefaultBranchCatalog(client, identity, version) → object`. It always addresses `identity.defaultGitUrl` / `identity.slug` at `releaseTag(identity, version)` and never reads `identity.distribution.sources` (those name where an organization's mirror serves roots; the default branch names this repository's own releases). `claude` → the `renderClaudeMarketplace` shape (`:169-190`: `name`, `owner{name,url}`, `description`, `version`, one plugin with `name`, `source`, `description`, `version`, `author`, `homepage`, `repository`, `license`, `keywords`) with `source: {source:'git-subdir', url: identity.defaultGitUrl, path:'claude', ref}` and no component paths (the release root's own manifest and default discovery carry them, `scripts/plugins/clients/claude.mjs:12-35`). `copilot` → the `renderCopilotMarketplace` shape (`:222-237`) with `source: {source:'github', repo: identity.slug, ref, path:'copilot'}` in that key order. `codex` → the `renderCodexMarketplace` shape (`:273-290`) with `source: {source:'git-subdir', url: identity.defaultGitUrl, path:'./codex', ref}`. `cursor` (or any other client) throws `catalogs: cursor catalogs accept only a directory path, so the default branch cannot address a release tag; Cursor serves a team marketplace from a distribution branch.` No `sha`: the distribution commit is unknown when the version commit lands. The header paragraph `:29-36` records the Copilot object form as documented (2026-10-01). **`scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs`:** `marketplace` (`:475-496`) becomes `renderDefaultBranchCatalog('claude', catalogIdentity, version)` with `catalogIdentity = buildCatalogIdentity(pkg, identity)` imported from `./plugins/catalogs.mjs`; `MANIFESTS` (`:541-546`) gains `.github/plugin/marketplace.json` and `.agents/plugins/marketplace.json` rows, so `--check` verifies 6 files ("Verified 6 plugin manifest(s)"); `isPrivate` (`:261-279`), `TARBALL_ROOT`, `tarballPath`, `marketplacePrefix`, `marketplacePath` (`:385-413`, `:464-465`) are deleted with their comments, and the header paragraphs "TWO TREES" (`:110-117`) and "A PRIVATE PACKAGE HAS NO TARBALL" (`:119-123`) are replaced by one paragraph stating the rule above. `scripts/fork-identity.mjs` needs no edit: it already runs this generator (`:61`). **Assumed from `docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md (u1-renovate-suffix)`:** `fork-identity.mjs --suffix <id>` writes suffix-aware patterns into the Renovate presets and leaves `package.json` `version` to the operator's `<X.Y.Z>-<id>.<n>`; the catalogs read that version through `releaseTag`, so no catalog code knows the suffix. **Codex README** `scripts/plugins/clients/codex.mjs:183-187` becomes: "Pin with `--ref`. Without it the client checks out the repository's default branch, whose `.agents/plugins/marketplace.json` names this root at the release tag of that branch's version — which can sit behind the newest tag, or ahead of a release still publishing." **Docs** `docs/plugins.md:267-277` ("The `--ref` is not optional …") becomes the same two facts in the page's voice; the add lines keep their `--ref` (`test/docsPages.test.ts:2392-2403` stays true). **CONTRIBUTING** `:209` row gains `.github/plugin/marketplace.json` and `.agents/plugins/marketplace.json`. **CHANGELOG** `### Changed` under `## [Unreleased]`: "**The default branch's catalogs install the full release root.** `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` named the npm package, which carries no hooks, no bundled runtime and unresolved placeholders, and Codex and the Copilot CLI fell back to it when a marketplace was added without a ref. | Field | Content |

| `files` | `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs` (header `:1-23`; new exports; `substitute` `:96-119`), `scripts/plugins/corpusStage.mjs` (the doc comment of `stageSubstitutedCorpus` `:108-122` only), `scripts/generate-plugin-packages.mjs` (the staging call `:458-464`), `test/ci/pluginModules.test.ts` (new cases beside `:426-447`), `test/ci/pluginLocate.test.ts` (one new case beside `:432-452`), `test/ci/pluginPackages.cursor.test.ts` (the oracle's staging call `:197-206`; one new case), `test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts` (the `cli` rule `:176-197`, `:346-354`), `test/ci/pluginPackages.claude.test.ts` and `test/ci/pluginPackages.codex.test.ts` (one new case each), `test/ci/pluginPackages.registryless.test.ts` (its CLI-form assertions, if any name the plugin bodies' fallback — re-read at intake), `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** Four corpus bodies carry one byte-identical sentence, pinned as `RUNNING_CLI_SENTENCE` in `test/corpus/cliCallForm.test.ts:32-33` at the call sites `content/skills/st-handoff/SKILL.md:23`, `content/skills/st-learn/SKILL.md:50`, `content/commands/st-debug.md:30` and `content/commands/st-work.md:160` (as the bullet `- **CLI calls.** …`): "Every `stamity <verb>` call in this file runs as `npx --no stamity <verb>`, which runs an installed copy — a `stamity` bin the project's own `package.json` declares, one in `node_modules/.bin` here or in a parent folder, or a global one — and never downloads a package; where npm refuses because no copy is installed, the call runs as `${STAMITY:CLI} <verb>`, the version this setup was generated with. Never `@latest`, and never `stamity <verb>` typed bare at the shell." `content/commands/st-quick.md:123-124` carries the token without the sentence. The plugin build stages the corpus once for all four clients (`scripts/generate-plugin-packages.mjs:458-464`, `cli: { packageName, version: releaseVersion, npmChannel }`), and `tokens.substitute` maps `${STAMITY:CLI}` to `pinnedCliPrefix(cli)` = `npx -y <pkg>@<ver>` (`npx --no` without an npm channel) (`scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs:50-62`, `:107-119`), so every plugin body names `npx --no stamity` first and the network form second, and no body names `runtime/locate.mjs` (0 hits in the four roots). The locator spawns the companion install when its version fits the plugin's range, else the bundled `runtime/dist/cli.js`, never PATH and never the network (`scripts/plugins/locate.mjs:12-32`, `:408-422`). A command's shell gets no plugin-root variable on the Copilot CLI (measured 1.0.87, `scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs:31-41`) or on Codex (exported to hook processes only, `scripts/plugins/clients/codex.mjs:208-210`). **New exports in `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs`:** `RUNNING_CLI_SENTENCE` (the exact sentence above, token unresolved); `LOCATOR_CALL = 'node "<root>/runtime/locate.mjs" --'`; `pluginCliSentence(cli) → string`, returning exactly: "Every `stamity <verb>` call in this file runs first as `node "<root>/runtime/locate.mjs" -- <verb>`, through the runtime this plugin bundles: `<root>` is this plugin's root, the nearest folder above this file that holds `stamity-plugin.json` (where your client sets `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT`, `CURSOR_PLUGIN_ROOT` or `PLUGIN_ROOT` in the shell, that variable names the same folder), and the locator runs the repository's own stamity install when its version fits this plugin and the bundled copy otherwise, and never downloads a package. Where the locator cannot run, the two npm forms apply: `npx --no stamity <verb>`, which runs an installed copy and never downloads a package, and where npm refuses because no copy is installed, `<PINNED> <verb>`, the version this plugin was built with. Never `@latest`, and never `stamity <verb>` typed bare at the shell." with `<PINNED>` = `pinnedCliPrefix(cli)`. `substitute(body, cli)`: when `cli.runtime === 'bundled'`, it first replaces every occurrence of `RUNNING_CLI_SENTENCE` with `pluginCliSentence(cli)`, then maps `${STAMITY:CLI}` to `LOCATOR_CALL` instead of the pinned call; any other `runtime` value throws `Cannot render the CLI call: runtime must be "bundled" when it is given.`; without `runtime`, behaviour is byte-identical to today (the APM generator and every other caller keep the pinned call). The phrase "neither form" in each site's next sentence then reads as the two npm forms. **Generator:** `scripts/generate-plugin-packages.mjs:463` passes `cli: { packageName, version: releaseVersion, npmChannel, runtime: 'bundled' }`. No per-client staging: `<root>` is client-neutral because two of the four clients export no root variable to a shell. **CHANGELOG** `### Changed`: "**Plugin skills run the runtime their plugin bundles.** In a plugin root, `st-learn`, `st-handoff`, `st-debug`, `st-work` and `st-quick` call the CLI through the root's `runtime/locate.mjs` first, which runs the repository's own install or the bundled copy and never downloads one; the `npx` forms are the fallback." **Follows:** `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs:107-119` (one pure substitution pass, unresolved tokens reported) and `test/ci/pluginModules.test.ts:426-437` (staging driven through `stageFor`). **Surface pins moved:** none. |

| `interfaces` | **Current.** `primitive(head, item)` = `composeFrontmatter(head, item.body)` (`scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:570-572`): bodies ship verbatim, tokens included — 36 occurrences in 11 files over 6 tokens (`CLI` ×12, `VERIFY_GATE_ALL` ×7, `VERIFY_GATE_TEST` ×6, `VERIFY_GATE_LINT` ×5, `VERIFY_GATE_TYPECHECK` ×5, `TEST_FRAMEWORK` ×1; `rg -no '\$\{STAMITY:[A-Z_]*\}' .apm` at `88fcfd32`), on the reasoning of `:92-99` and the maintainer decision of 2026-09-17 ("APM unchanged", `.stamity/inbox.md:17`). The plugin side: `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs` exports `TOKEN_PREFIX`, `CLI_TOKEN`, `pinnedCliPrefix({ packageName, version, npmChannel })` (`:50-62`), `CHARTER_REFERENCE_PHRASES` (eight tokens → phrases, `:70-79`, bound to `REPO_SUBSTITUTION_TOKENS` by `test/ci/pluginModules.test.ts`), and `substitute(body, cli)` → `{ text, unresolved }` over the bounded `TOKEN_PATTERN = /\$\{STAMITY:[^}\n]*\}/g` (`:95`, `:107-119`). `scripts/generate-plugin-packages.mjs:332-334`: `npmChannel = !(pkg.private === true \|\| pkg.private === 'true') \|\| (typeof pkg.publishConfig?.registry === 'string' && pkg.publishConfig.registry !== '')`. **New, tokens.mjs:** `export function substituteWith(body, values)` — `values: Readonly<Record<string, string>>`, token → replacement; the same bounded pattern, `$` literal (replacer function), unknown tokens left standing and listed once each in first-appearance order. `substitute(body, cli)` keeps its exact results by delegating: `substituteWith(body, { ...CHARTER_REFERENCE_PHRASES, ...(cliValue === null ? {} : { [CLI_TOKEN]: cliValue }) })`. **New, the generator:** it computes `npmChannel` as above and `const cli = { packageName, version, npmChannel }`, refusing once (exit 1) when `pinnedCliPrefix(cli)` throws; `APM_TOKEN_VALUES = { ...CHARTER_REFERENCE_PHRASES, [CLI_TOKEN]: pinnedCliPrefix(cli) }`; `primitive(head, item)` → `composeFrontmatter(head, resolveBody(item))` with `resolveBody(item) = substituteWith(item.body, APM_TOKEN_VALUES)`; any `unresolved` → `fail("<item.relativePath>: <tokens> is not resolvable in an APM body — a package is built once for every consumer, so a token here would reach the agent as a broken variable")` before anything is written. The APM bodies take the plain pinned `npx` call through `substituteWith`, never through `substitute`, so a plugin-root-only rendering of `${STAMITY:CLI}` (u2-skills-bundled-runtime) cannot reach them. Skill companion files (`references/`, `scripts/`, `agents/openai.yaml`) stay byte-verbatim (`scripts/plugins/corpusStage.mjs:11-19`'s opaque rule). `--check`: after the byte comparison, every rendered and every committed `.md` under `.apm/` is scanned with `TOKEN_PATTERN`; a hit adds the drift line `<path>: unresolved ${STAMITY:…} token(s): <list>`. The header paragraph `:92-99` is rewritten: bodies carry the plugin roots' charter-reference phrases (REQ-PLUGIN-004) and the CLI call pinned to this package and version; the 2026-09-17 decision is reversed by plan 016. Result: `.apm/skills/st-learn/SKILL.md`'s `${STAMITY:CLI} learn capture` → `npx -y @zomarit/stamity@<package.json version> learn capture`; `.apm/agents/stamity-test-runner.agent.md:27`'s `${STAMITY:VERIFY_GATE_TEST}` → "the Tests command listed under Verification gates in AGENTS.md"; a registry-less private fork renders `npx --no @acme/stamity@<version>`. The unterminated prose `${STAMITY:` at `content/agents/stamity-test-runner.md:79` stays (the bounded pattern does not match it). **Docs:** `docs/getting-started.md`'s APM section says the package carries no template token: gates and facts read as pointers into the consuming repository's `AGENTS.md`, CLI calls are pinned to the package's version. **Inbox:** the row at `.stamity/inbox.md:17` leaves with one line in the run record (the inbox header's retirement rule, `:3`). **CHANGELOG** `### Changed`: "**The APM package carries no template token.** Gate and fact tokens read as the same pointers into `AGENTS.md` the plugin roots carry, and every CLI call is the pinned `npx -y @zomarit/stamity@<version>` call; `generate-apm-package.mjs --check` fails on any token left. The CLI calls name the package version, so `.apm/` moves with every release." **Follows:** `scripts/plugins/corpusStage.mjs:64-101` (`stageDirectory`: substitute bodies, refuse unresolved tokens by file, copy companions opaquely) |

A fork layer body carrying a token → resolved the same way. `${STAMITY:INVARIANTS_VERSION}` in a non-charter body → unresolved, refused (as the plugin build refuses it). A `pinnedCliPrefix` refusal (a version like `1.12.0+build`) → one refusal before any render, exit 1. |
| `depends_on` | u2-upgrade-verb, u2-skills-bundled-runtime (both edit `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs`, this unit calls the substitution without the plugin runtime) |
| `verify` | `node scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs && node scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs --check && npx vitest run test/ci/apmPackage.test.ts test/ci/pluginModules.test.ts test/ci/apmDownstream.test.ts test/ci/apmInstall.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts && npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm test` |

| Field | Content |

The smoke takes only `owner/repo[#ref]` (`scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:491-494`) and reads expectations from `apm_modules/<owner>/<repo>/.apm` (`:496-509`); apm-cli 0.32.0 vendors `owner/repo/<path>` to `apm_modules/<owner>/<repo>/<path>/` (`apm_cli/models/dependency/materialization.py:158-170`, read 2026-10-01). **New (FOLDER):** `runGenerator('generate-apm-package.mjs', ['--out-dir', join(outDir, 'apm')])` beside today's root run, which stays for one compatibility window (decision S3: the 1.9–1.11 spec `<slug>#plugins/v<version>` keeps working when Renovate moves its ref; the root copy goes at the next major release, and only if u2-apm-sparse-measure recorded `APM root copy: KEEP`); `apm: { manifest: 'apm/apm.yml', primitives: 'apm/.apm', installSpec: `${identity.slug}/apm#${tag}` }` (the key set does not move); README bullet "the APM package in `apm/` — `apm/apm.yml` plus `apm/.apm/` — installed on its own with `apm install <slug>/apm#<tag>`; nothing else in this tree is vendored", and under `## APM` the install line plus "A dependency written `<slug>#plugins/v<version>` keeps working until the next major release; change it once to `<slug>/apm#plugins/v<version>` for the slim fetch — Renovate moves the ref, not the path." Header `:9-15` follows. The smoke: `looksLikeGitSpec(spec)` = `/^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+(?:\/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+)*(?:#[^\s]+)?$/.test(spec) && !isDirectory(spec)`; `vendoredSource(consumerDir, virtualPath = null)` checks `apm_modules/<o>/<r>/<virtualPath>/.apm` first when the spec carries a path, then today's walk; `USAGE` names `owner/repo[/path][#ref]`. `.github/workflows/fork-release.yml:842` reads "APM consumers can pin `${{ github.repository }}#v$VERSION` (the source tree) or `${{ github.repository }}/apm#$PLUGINS_TAG` (the APM package alone, from the distribution)." **New (FALLBACK):** `apm: { manifest: 'apm/apm.yml', primitives: 'apm/.apm', installSpec: `${identity.slug}#${apmTag}` }` with `apmTag` from `stamity.distribution.apmTagPattern`, and the push step publishes `<out>/apm/` as its own orphan commit under that tag (never moved, idempotent, as the plugin push). **Unchanged:** the source tree's root `apm.yml` and `.apm/` (installs from `<slug>#v<version>` keep working, `docs/getting-started.md:100-107`). **Byte identity:** a test hashes every file under `<dist>/apm/`, the distribution root's `apm.yml` plus `.apm/`, and the committed root package, and requires the same relative file set and the same sha256 per file across all three; `node scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs --check --out-dir <dist>/apm` exits 0. **Docs:** `docs/getting-started.md` gains the slim route as the recommended pin (`apm install zomarit/stamity/apm#plugins/v<version> --target <…>`), keeps the source-tag route, and rewrites the vendored-tree paragraph (`:126-129`): the slim route vendors only `apm/` (about 0.5 MB, 58 files); APM's cache also fetches the distribution root's top-level files (about 7.3 MB of archives) because a sparse cone always includes them. `docs/enterprise-forks.md:1004-1005` names `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>`. **CHANGELOG** `### Changed`: "**The distribution tree carries its APM package alone under `apm/`.** Install it with `apm install zomarit/stamity/apm#plugins/v<version>`; APM fetches that folder rather than the whole tree. A dependency written `zomarit/stamity#plugins/v<version>` keeps working until the next major release, which removes the distribution tree's root copy; change it once to `zomarit/stamity/apm#plugins/v<version>` — Renovate moves only the ref. Installs from `zomarit/stamity#v<version>` are unchanged." **Spec delta** (for the plan's section, REQ-APM-010 and invariant 1, `docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:99-100`): as drafted in `spec-delta-02.md` B. **Follows:** `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:497-513` (the build composes sibling generators into one tree) |

A fork with a custom `tagPattern` → `installSpec` uses its tag. The distribution root's archives ride in APM's cache (cone mode) → documented; moving them is a follow-up. A local `--source` that is a path containing `/` and exists → still a directory (the `!isDirectory` guard). |
