### u2-apm-charter — carry the charter to APM-only consumers as its own small package, and document per target what reaches

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-apm-charter |
| `requirements` | REQ-APM-012, REQ-APM-009 |
| `files` | `scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs` (usage `:6-15`; the charter-gap paragraph `:161-180`; `USAGE` `:278`; argument parsing `:345-359`; a new `renderCharterPackage()` beside `renderPackage` `:626-713`); `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs` (a second generator call after the `apm/` one; README `## APM`; the `apm` block); `scripts/plugins/releaseManifest.mjs` (`APM_KEYS` `:33`; `buildReleaseManifest` `:120`; `validateApm` `:215-224`); `test/ci/apmPackage.test.ts` (a new `describe("the charter package")`); `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts` (`:72`, `:109`; new cases); `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts` (`manifest.apm`; the README line); `test/ci/pluginDownstream.test.ts` (the fork's charter spec and CLI call); `docs/getting-started.md` (APM section: the APM-only flow and the per-target table); `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (`:708-711`); `test/docsPages.test.ts` (`REATTESTATION_DATE`); `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** The generator leaves the charter out by settled scope (`scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:161-170`). The charter template is `content/charter/stamity-charter.md` (frontmatter `description`, `invariants_version: 1.1.0`, `invariants_ratified: 2026-08-31`, `invariants_amended: 2026-09-27`; body tokens `LINTER`, `TEST_FRAMEWORK`, `CI_PROVIDER`, `MATURITY_TIER`, `CLI` at `:23-26`, the four gates at `:32-35`, `INVARIANTS_VERSION` at `:38`). `readCharterTemplate(contentRoot?)` (`src/content/charter.ts:452`) returns `{ frontmatter, invariants: { version, ratified, amended } \| null, body, … }`; `renderInvariantsVersion(invariants)` (`src/emit/substitution.ts:277-279`) renders `<version> · ratified <date> · last amended <date>`; `DETECTION_UNKNOWN = "unknown"` (`src/emit/substitution.ts:38`) is the engine's sentinel for a fact nothing detected, and the charter itself defines it ("The literal value `unknown` means detection found nothing — treat that item as unconfigured and report it", `content/charter/stamity-charter.md:20-21`). **Measured (probe 2026-10-01, apm-cli 0.32.0, a fixture package):** an instruction with no `applyTo` is deployed by `apm install` to `.claude/rules/<id>.md` with no frontmatter, to `.github/instructions/<id>.instructions.md` and `.cursor/rules/<id>.mdc` with `description` only, and to nothing for codex ("Instructions installed for codex. Run 'apm compile' to update AGENTS.md."); `apm compile --target codex` and `--target cursor` fold it into a new `AGENTS.md` under `## Global Instructions`; `--target copilot` writes no `AGENTS.md` while `.github/instructions/` is populated ("pass --force-instructions to write AGENTS.md"); `--target claude` writes no `CLAUDE.md` ("Claude Code reads .claude/rules/ directly"); compile never overwrites a hand-authored `AGENTS.md` ("Protected AGENTS.md: hand-authored file will not be overwritten"); a `.apm/context/*.context.md` primitive is neither deployed nor folded; per-dependency filters are `skill_subset` and `target_subset` only (`apm_cli/models/dependency/reference.py:104-105`), so a consumer cannot exclude one instruction of a package. **Hence the shape (see Concerns C1):** the charter is NOT an instruction of the main package, which every APM-backed repository installs and which would then deliver it twice; it is its own package, `apm-charter/` in the distribution tree, which APM-only consumers add and APM-backed repositories do not. **New, the generator:** `--charter` renders, under `--out-dir` (required with `--charter`), exactly two files: `apm.yml` = `name: <packageId>-charter`, `version`, `description: "The stamity charter as one always-on APM instruction: repository facts left unknown, the floor invariants, the touchpoint index."`, `author`, `license`, `type: instructions`; and `.apm/instructions/stamity-charter.instructions.md` = frontmatter `{ description: <charter frontmatter description> }` (no `applyTo`, see the edge case) over the charter body resolved with `substituteWith(body, { the four fact tokens and the four gate tokens: DETECTION_UNKNOWN, [INVARIANTS_VERSION_TOKEN]: renderInvariantsVersion(invariants), [CLI_TOKEN]: pinnedCliPrefix(cli) })` (`substituteWith` from u2-apm-placeholders); an unresolved token or a charter without invariants keys → exit 1 before writing. `--charter --check --out-dir <dir>` byte-compares the two files and reports any extra file as drift. The charter-gap paragraph `:161-180` is rewritten to state this. **New, the build:** after the `apm/` call, `runGenerator('generate-apm-package.mjs', ['--charter', '--out-dir', join(outDir, 'apm-charter')])`; `apm` gains `charterInstallSpec: `${identity.slug}/apm-charter#${tag}`` (FALLBACK: the fallback tag, the charter package pushed beside the main one). `scripts/plugins/releaseManifest.mjs`: `APM_KEYS` = `['manifest', 'primitives', 'installSpec', 'charterInstallSpec']`, the builder copies it when present, and the validator checks it as a tree path **only when present**, so a `release.json` of an earlier release (no such key) still validates — the promote and rollback path of u2-channels-promote reads older manifests. README `## APM`: "APM-only repositories add the charter too: `apm install <slug>/apm-charter#<tag>`. A repository that also runs the CLI does not — the CLI writes the charter." **Measurement at execution** (clients from u2-apm-sparse-measure, declarative local route, a fresh consumer per target, 0.32.0 and 0.29.1): per target the deployed path and its frontmatter; then `apm compile --target <t>` (and `--force-instructions` for copilot) in a consumer with no `AGENTS.md`, recording whether `AGENTS.md` or `CLAUDE.md` is written and carries `## Invariants`; then the same with a hand-authored `AGENTS.md`, recording "Protected". **Docs:** `docs/getting-started.md` gets the APM-only flow (`apm install`, then `apm compile`, with `--force-instructions` when copilot is a target) and a per-target table — claude: `.claude/rules/stamity-charter.md`, loaded every session (Claude Code loads a rule without `paths` at session start, `https://code.claude.com/docs/en/memory`, read on the run date); copilot: `.github/instructions/stamity-charter.instructions.md` without `applyTo` (what VS Code does with it, from `https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/copilot/customization/custom-instructions` read on the run date) and the compiled `AGENTS.md` with `--force-instructions`; cursor: an agent-requested rule plus the compiled `AGENTS.md`; codex: the compiled `AGENTS.md` only; and a "not delivered" line for anything the measurement shows missing. `docs/enterprise-forks.md:708-711` ("This package does not deliver stamity's charter…") is rewritten to point at the charter package. **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**APM-only repositories can install the charter.** Each release's distribution carries `apm-charter/`, a one-instruction package holding the charter with its repository facts left `unknown`; `apm install zomarit/stamity/apm-charter#plugins/v<version>` adds it, and the getting-started page lists what each target loads. A repository that also runs the CLI leaves it out." **Follows:** `scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:626-713` (`renderPackage`: render everything into a map, write only after every refusal had its chance) |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** `node scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs --charter --out-dir <tmp>`, **then** `<tmp>` holds exactly `apm.yml` and `.apm/instructions/stamity-charter.instructions.md`; `apm.yml` carries `name: stamity-charter`, `type: instructions` and the `package.json` version; the instruction's frontmatter keys are exactly `["description"]`; its body contains `Invariants version 1.1.0 · ratified 2026-08-31 · last amended 2026-09-27`, `- Linter: unknown`, `` - Tests: `unknown` ``, `` `npx -y @zomarit/stamity@<version> config` `` and no `${STAMITY:`. **Given** two runs, **then** the bytes are equal; **given** `--charter --check --out-dir <tmp>` on that output, **then** exit 0; **given** a seeded edit or an extra file, **then** exit 1 naming it. **Given** `--charter` with no `--out-dir`, **then** exit 2. **Given** a built distribution, **then** `dist/apm-charter/apm.yml` exists, `release.json`'s `apm.charterInstallSpec` equals `${SLUG}/apm-charter#plugins/v${VERSION}`, and `validateReleaseManifest` returns `[]`. **Given** a manifest with the key removed, **then** `validateReleaseManifest` returns `[]` (an earlier release); **given** `charterInstallSpec: "/abs"`, **then** it returns `apm.charterInstallSpec: must be a relative path inside the distribution tree`; **given** `apm.other`, **then** the existing unsupported-key message. Re-pin `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts:109` (key order) with `TEST CHANGE, justified:`. **Given** the fork fixture, **then** the charter spec names the fork's slug and the CLI call names `@<scope>/stamity`. **Given** the measurement record, **then** it has one row per target per client (8 rows) and the getting-started table states each row's outcome as delivered or not delivered. |
| `edgeCases` | 0.29.1 refuses an instruction with no `applyTo` → the record shows it and the instruction takes `applyTo: "**"` instead, the table saying Claude then loads it when a file is read rather than at session start. A repository running both the charter package and the CLI → `u2-apm-backed-mode`'s duplicates row fails with the remedy to remove the charter dependency. A fork's renamed package `@acme/stamity-private` → `name: stamity-private-charter`. A registry-less fork → the charter's CLI call reads `npx --no …`. APM-only codex receives no touchpoints (APM's codex profile carries no command class) while the charter's Touchpoints paragraph promises skills to a `.agents/skills/` reader → documented in the per-target table; the wording fix is a follow-up. |
| `depends_on` | u2-apm-slim-package, u2-apm-placeholders, u2-apm-sparse-measure |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/ci/apmPackage.test.ts test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts test/ci/pluginDownstream.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts && npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm test` |

### u2-channels-promote — release channels that move only through a verified promotion

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-channels-promote |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-035 |
| `files` | `scripts/distribution-identity.mjs` (`:21-25` defaults and `DISTRIBUTION_KEYS`; `:221-276` `resolveDistribution`), `scripts/promote-channel.mjs` (new), `.github/workflows/promote.yml` (new), `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs` (`renderReadme` `:376-438`: a `## Channels` section after `## APM`), `test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts` (`:88-99`, `:113-116`; new cases), `test/ci/promoteChannel.test.ts` (new), `test/ci/workflow.test.ts` (`:4141-4176` secrets list; `:4199-4241` writers list; a new `describe("promote.yml …")`), `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts` (a case beside `:535-584`), `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (a new H2 section `## Promote a release to a channel` after `## Release your fork` (`:904`) and before `## Roll the plugin out to your organization`, plus the page header's re-attestation line), `test/docsPages.test.ts` (one new case; `REATTESTATION_DATE` if the header moves), `.github/release-controls-checklist.md` (a new "Control 4 — channel promotion" section after `:129`), `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** `plugin-dist` is force-pushed to each release's orphan commit and `plugins/v<version>` is created once (`.github/workflows/release.yml:923-1003`; `fork-release.yml:671-751`, held byte-equal to it by `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`); the commit message is `plugins: v$VERSION from $GITHUB_SHA` and both git dates are `release.json`'s `sourceCommitDate` (`release.yml:969-970`), so the commit dates cannot measure a soak; the GitHub release is created under `v$VERSION` (`release.yml:1074`, `fork-release.yml:793`). `scripts/distribution-identity.mjs:25` `DISTRIBUTION_KEYS = ['branch', 'tagPattern', 'ownerEmail', 'sources']`; `:42` `REF_NAME = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._/-]*$/`; `:231-236` the branch check; `:272-275` result key order `branch, tagPattern, ownerEmail?, sources`. Each catalog entry pins `ref: plugins/v<version>` and the entry `version` (`scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs:158-165`, `:181`); Claude "detects updates by comparing versions" and "has no release-channel concept" (code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/host-marketplace, "Run release channels"). Rulesets today: `main-policy` (default branch) and `release-tags` (`refs/tags/v*`); nothing covers `plugin-dist`, channel branches or `refs/tags/plugins/v*` (`gh api` read, research return 2026-10-01). A workflow's `GITHUB_TOKEN` cannot be a ruleset bypass actor for `non_fast_forward` (field reports of HTTP 422 "Actor GitHub Actions integration must be part of the ruleset source or owner organization"; bypass actor types are `Integration`, `OrganizationAdmin`, `RepositoryRole`, `Team`, `DeployKey`, `User` — docs.github.com/en/rest/repos/rules; deploy keys became bypass actors on 2024-04-30). Environment required reviewers gate a `workflow_dispatch` job, and environment secrets are released only after approval; on Free, Pro and Team plans required reviewers work on public repositories only (docs.github.com deployments-and-environments). **New config** in `package.json` `stamity.distribution`: `channels` — an object whose keys are `stable` and optionally `canary`, each a branch name; default when absent `{ "stable": "plugin-stable" }`; and `soakDays` — an integer 0..90, default 0. `resolveDistribution` checks them after `tagPattern`: `DISTRIBUTION_KEYS` becomes `['branch', 'tagPattern', 'channels', 'soakDays', 'ownerEmail', 'sources']`; `channels` must be a plain object (`package.json stamity.distribution.channels must be an object keyed by channel.`), unknown keys refused through `refuseUnknownKeys(value, ['stable', 'canary'], 'channels')`, `stable` required when `channels` is given (`package.json stamity.distribution.channels.stable is required: a channel set always has a stable channel.`), each value passes the `branch` rule (`:232`) or throws `package.json stamity.distribution.channels.<name> must be a git branch name: …`, no value equals `branch` or another channel (`… must differ from stamity.distribution.branch and from every other channel.`), and no value matches the tag pattern's rendered shape (`tagPattern` with `<version>` → `\d+\.\d+\.\d+…`; `… must not look like a distribution tag.`); `soakDays` not an integer in 0..90 throws `package.json stamity.distribution.soakDays must be a whole number of days from 0 to 90.` The resolved object's key order becomes `branch, tagPattern, channels, soakDays, ownerEmail?, sources` (the catalog renderers read only `branch`, `tagPattern`, `ownerEmail` and `sources`, so no catalog byte moves). **`scripts/promote-channel.mjs`** (new; Node builtins plus `./distribution-identity.mjs`, `./plugins/catalogs.mjs`, `./plugins/releaseManifest.mjs`, `./plugins/version.mjs`). Usage: `node scripts/promote-channel.mjs --tag <tag> --channel <stable\|canary> --remote <git-url> [--verify-only] [--expect-commit <sha>] [--json]`. Exports for tests: `parseArguments(argv)`, `planPromotion({ identity, tag, channel, git, release, now }) → Promise<PromotionPlan>`, `pushPromotion(plan, git) → Promise<PushOutcome>`, where `git(args) → {status, stdout, stderr}` runs `git` with `shell: false` (injected), and `release` is `{ assetSha256(version, archive) → Promise<string\|null>, publishedAt(version) → Promise<string\|null> }` (the CLI binds it to `gh release download "v<version>" --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" --pattern '<archive>.sha256' --output -` and `gh release view "v<version>" --json publishedAt --jq .publishedAt`). `PromotionPlan` = `{ ok: boolean, tag, version, channel, branch, commit: string\|null, previous: { version: string, commit: string } \| null, rollback: boolean, soak: { requiredDays: number, publishedAt: string\|null, ageDays: number\|null } \| null, problems: string[] }`. Two refusals come before any read and exit 2: `channel` not configured (`--channel canary: stamity.distribution.channels names no canary branch.`), and `tag` not equal to `releaseTag(identity, v)` for a `PLUGIN_VERSION`-valid `v` (`<tag> is not a distribution tag of this identity (<tagPattern>).`). Then the checks, in order, each failure appending one problem (exit 1) and stopping later network reads: (1) `git ls-remote <remote> refs/tags/<tag>` names a commit (`<tag> does not exist on the remote.`); (2) after `git fetch --depth=2 <remote> refs/tags/<tag>`, the commit has no parent (`<tag> is not an orphan distribution commit.`); (3) its subject equals `plugins: v<version> from <40-hex>` (`<tag>'s commit message is not the release step's.`); (4) `git show <commit>:release.json` parses, `validateReleaseManifest` returns no defect, `version === <version>`, `distribution.tag === <tag>`, `distribution.branch === identity.distribution.branch` (each mismatch named); (5) for every `packages[]` entry the archive blob at `<commit>` hashes to `entry.sha256`, the `<archive>.sha256` blob reads `<sha256>  <archive>\n`, and `release.assetSha256` returns the same line (`<archive>: the release asset .sha256 differs from the tree.` or `… is missing on release v<version>.`); (6) with `soakDays > 0`, `release.publishedAt` is at least `soakDays` days before `now` (`soak: release v<version> was published <n> day(s) ago; the channel needs <soakDays>.`); (7) the channel head, when present, is an orphan whose subject has the release shape, read as `previous` (`<branch>'s head is not a distribution commit; refusing to move a branch with history.`); `rollback` is true when `previous.version` is semver-greater than `version`; the same commit is a no-op (`ok: true`, nothing pushed, message "already at v<version>"). `pushPromotion` runs `git push --force-with-lease=refs/heads/<branch>:<previous.commit or empty> <remote> <commit>:refs/heads/<branch>`; a refusal whose stderr carries `GH013`, `non-fast-forward`, `protected branch` or `Cannot force-push` exits 1 with: `A rule on refs/heads/<branch> refused the push. Give the promotion a bypass: store a write deploy key as the STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY secret of the plugin-promotion environment and add "Deploy keys" to that ruleset's bypass list, or allow force pushes on <branch>. GITHUB_TOKEN cannot bypass a ruleset.` The remote URL is never printed (it may carry a token); every message names the branch and tag only. Exit codes: 0 promoted, verified or no-op; 1 a check or the push failed; 2 bad arguments or configuration. `--verify-only` stops after the plan; `--expect-commit` refuses (exit 1) when the planned commit differs from the one the verify job printed. **`.github/workflows/promote.yml`** (new): `name: Promote plugin channel`; `on: workflow_dispatch` with exactly two inputs, `tag` (string, required) and `channel` (choice: `stable`, `canary`); workflow `permissions: contents: read`; `concurrency: { group: promote-${{ inputs.channel }}, cancel-in-progress: false }`. Job `verify` (`permissions: contents: read`): SHA-pinned `actions/checkout` and `actions/setup-node` at the pins `release.yml` uses, then `node scripts/promote-channel.mjs --tag "$TAG" --channel "$CHANNEL" --remote "https://github.com/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY.git" --verify-only --json` with `GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}` and inputs passed through `env:` (never interpolated into `run:`), writing the plan to the step summary and `commit` to job outputs. Job `promote` (`needs: verify`, `if: needs.verify.outputs.ok == 'true'`, `environment: plugin-promotion`, `permissions: contents: write`): runs the script with `--expect-commit "${{ needs.verify.outputs.commit }}"`; when the environment secret `STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY` is set it pushes over SSH (`git@github.com:$GITHUB_REPOSITORY.git`, key written to a `0600` temp file, `GIT_SSH_COMMAND="ssh -i <file> -o IdentitiesOnly=yes -o StrictHostKeyChecking=yes -o UserKnownHostsFile=<file holding GitHub's published ed25519 host key>"`), else over HTTPS with `GITHUB_TOKEN`. No `zomarit/stamity` literal: the identity comes from the checked-out `package.json`, so the same file serves the canonical repository and every fork. **Distribution README** (`renderReadme`): a `## Channels` section naming the channel branches from the identity, "the tree at `<tag>` reaches a channel only through the `Promote plugin channel` workflow (`.github/workflows/promote.yml`), which verifies the tag before it moves the branch", and "roll back by promoting the previous tag". **Docs:** the enterprise-forks section `## Promote a release to a channel` — its own H2, because `test/docsPages.test.ts:2300-2318` holds every `STAMITY_` name inside `## Release your fork` to the settings `fork-release.yml` reads — names the workflow, its two inputs, the `plugin-promotion` environment, `STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY` and the ruleset bypass, rollback, and the Team-plan limit; the release-controls checklist's Control 4 lists the environment's required reviewer and the channel ruleset (restrict deletions; block force pushes with "Deploy keys" in the bypass list, or leave force pushes open). **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**Release channels move only through a verified promotion.** `stamity.distribution.channels` names a `stable` branch (default `plugin-stable`) and an optional `canary`; the `Promote plugin channel` workflow checks the tag's commit, its `release.json`, every archive digest against the release assets and an optional `soakDays` before it moves a channel, behind an environment with required reviewers. Rollback is promoting the previous tag. `plugin-dist` still carries the newest release." **Follows:** `.github/workflows/release.yml:923-1003` (the push step's shape checks, remote read-first, orphan-head refusal) and `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:89-187` (argument sinks, exit 2 on usage). **Surface pins moved:** the closed secrets list (`test/ci/workflow.test.ts:4166-4174`) and the writers list (`:4228-4234`), each with a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` line; no CLI verb, flag or `stamity config` key (the new keys live in `package.json` `stamity.distribution`, read by scripts only). |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** a fixture bare remote (`file://` URL) holding an orphan distribution commit tagged `plugins/v1.2.0` with subject `plugins: v1.2.0 from <40 hex>`, a valid `release.json` built with `buildReleaseManifest` and two archive blobs with matching `.sha256` blobs, and a release stub returning matching asset lines, **when** `planPromotion` then `pushPromotion` run for `stable`, **then** `git ls-remote <remote> refs/heads/plugin-stable` names the tag's commit and `plugin-dist` and every tag are unchanged (in `test/ci/promoteChannel.test.ts`); **when** run again, **then** it exits 0 with "already at v1.2.0" and pushes nothing. **Given** each defect in turn — a subject `plugins: v1.2.1 from …`, `release.json` `version` `1.2.1`, one archive byte changed, the stub's asset line differing, the tag absent, the commit having a parent — **then** the plan has `ok: false`, exactly one problem naming that check, and the remote's refs are byte-identical before and after (`git for-each-ref` compared). **Given** `soakDays: 3` and the stub's `publishedAt` one day before `now`, **then** the problem reads "published 1 day(s) ago; the channel needs 3"; with three days, **then** it promotes. **Given** the channel at `plugins/v1.3.0` and a promotion of `plugins/v1.2.0`, **then** the channel points at 1.2.0's commit and the plan carries `rollback: true` and `previous.version: "1.3.0"`. **Given** a channel head with a parent, **then** nothing is pushed and the problem names the branch. **Given** a remote whose `pre-receive` hook prints `GH013: Repository rule violations found` and exits 1, **then** `pushPromotion` exits 1 with the message naming `STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY` and "Deploy keys", and stderr does not contain the remote URL. **Given** `channels` naming `plugin-dist`, a value `-x`, a key `beta`, a missing `stable`, or `soakDays: 1.5`, **then** `resolveDistributionIdentity` throws naming that path (in `test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts`); the resolved-shape pins at `:88-99` move to `["branch","tagPattern","channels","soakDays","sources"]` with `channels: {stable:"plugin-stable"}` and `soakDays: 0` under a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` comment. **Given** `promote.yml`, **then** its dispatch inputs are exactly `tag` and `channel`, `promote` names `environment: plugin-promotion`, holds the only write grant and carries an `if:`, every action is SHA-pinned, and no line carries `zomarit/stamity` (in `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`). **Given** a full distribution build, **then** the README's `## Channels` section names `plugin-stable` and `.github/workflows/promote.yml` (in `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`). **Given** the enterprise-forks page, **then** its `## Promote a release to a channel` section names `STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY`, `plugin-promotion` and both inputs (in `test/docsPages.test.ts`). |
| `edgeCases` | A fork on a GitHub Team plan with a private repository gets no required reviewers: the `promote` job runs without approval, the verify checks still bind, and the docs section says so. A first promotion creates the channel branch (`--force-with-lease=refs/heads/<branch>:` expects it absent). Two dispatches for one channel queue on the `concurrency` group and the second one's lease protects against a race. A release asset missing because the release step partly failed fails the promotion, never the reverse. `canary` dispatched where no canary is configured exits 2 before any network read. Windows: the tests drive git through `execFileSync`; the `pre-receive` hook is a `#!/bin/sh` script that Git for Windows runs through its bundled shell — if the Windows leg cannot run it, that one case is skipped there with the reason written in the test, never marked passed. |
| `depends_on` | u2-main-catalogs (the CHANGELOG writer order and `test/docsPages.test.ts`), u2-apm-slim-package (both edit `renderReadme` in `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs`) |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/ci/promoteChannel.test.ts test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts test/ci/workflow.test.ts test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts && npm run lint && npm run typecheck` |

### u2-admin-templates — managed-settings templates per channel and per tag, for Claude Code and Copilot

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-admin-templates |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-036 (and, at Prove, the spec delta's MODIFIED REQ-PLUGIN-029) |
| `files` | `scripts/plugins/managed-settings.mjs` (whole module: `:1-37` header and key list, `:95-112` renderer; new renderer and `adminTemplates`), `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs` (header `:24-27`; `:77` `CLAUDE_MANAGED_SETTINGS`; `:244-249` `installNote`; the Copilot section of `clientRoutes` `:291-304`; `:561-570` the write), `test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts` (`:80-145` and new cases), `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts` (`:586-648`), `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (`## Roll the plugin out to your organization` `:1023-1086` only, plus the header line if `u2-channels-promote` has not moved it), `test/docsPages.test.ts` (`:2320-2337`), `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** `renderClaudeManagedSettings(identity, { ref, minimumVersion = MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION })` (`scripts/plugins/managed-settings.mjs:100-112`) returns `extraKnownMarketplaces: {<name>: {source: {source:'github', repo:<slug>, ref}}}`, `enabledPlugins: {"<name>@<name>": true}`, `strictKnownMarketplaces: [<same source>]`, `requiredMinimumVersion` (`MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION = '2.1.277'`, `:21`); `MANAGED_SETTINGS_KEYS` (`:24-37`) fixes that order; `REF_NAME` (`:44`) mirrors the identity's rule and is pinned to it (`test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts:197-205`). The builder writes only `admin/claude-managed-settings.json`, at the release tag, when Claude is built (`scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:561-570`), and the Claude README section names it (`:244-249`). **Vendor shapes (read 2026-10-01).** Claude: a managed `extraKnownMarketplaces` entry is `{ "source": {…}, "autoUpdate": true }` — `autoUpdate` sits beside `source`, and a managed value locks the user's toggle (code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/org, "Require a marketplace" and "Set update policy"); allowlist matching is exact on `repo`, `ref` and `path` (code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference); `requiredMinimumVersion` is managed-only, invalid values are ignored (code.claude.com/docs/en/managed-settings). Copilot (`.github-private` repository → `copilot/managed-settings.json` on its default branch; GA 2026-07-01; legacy `.github/copilot/settings.json` still read): `enabledPlugins {"PLUGIN@MARKETPLACE": bool}`; `extraKnownMarketplaces {<name>: {source, autoUpdate?}}` with `source` `{source:"github", repo:"OWNER/REPO", ref?, path?}`, `{source:"git", url, ref?, path?}` or `{source:"directory", path}`; `strictKnownMarketplaces` an array of source objects, `[]` = lockdown, ref matching undocumented; no `requiredMinimumVersion` key (docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/enterprise-administrators/enterprise-managed-settings). **New in `managed-settings.mjs`.** `renderClaudeManagedSettings(identity, { ref, autoUpdate = false, strict = false, minimumVersion = MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION })` returns, in this key order: `extraKnownMarketplaces: {<name>: autoUpdate ? {source, autoUpdate: true} : {source}}`, `enabledPlugins`, `requiredMinimumVersion`, then `strictKnownMarketplaces: [source]` only when `strict` (the order the spec delta's REQ-PLUGIN-029 text fixes: the opt-in key last). `MANAGED_SETTINGS_KEYS` becomes `['extraKnownMarketplaces', 'enabledPlugins', 'requiredMinimumVersion', 'strictKnownMarketplaces']`. `autoUpdate` and `strict` must be booleans (`managed-settings: autoUpdate must be a boolean.`). New `renderCopilotManagedSettings(identity, { ref, autoUpdate = false, strict = false })` returns `extraKnownMarketplaces` (same entry shape), `enabledPlugins`, and `strictKnownMarketplaces` only when `strict`; `COPILOT_MANAGED_SETTINGS_KEYS = ['extraKnownMarketplaces', 'enabledPlugins', 'strictKnownMarketplaces']`; it never writes a key the Copilot reference does not document. New `adminTemplates(identity, { tag, clients }) → { path: string, document: object }[]`, pure: for each of `claude` and `copilot` that is in `clients`, in that order, for each variant in order `tag` (ref = `tag`, `autoUpdate: false`), `stable` (ref = `identity.distribution.channels.stable`, `autoUpdate: true`), `canary` (only when configured; ref = its branch, `autoUpdate: true`), it yields the plain file and then the strict one: `admin/<client>/managed-settings.<variant>.json` and `admin/<client>/managed-settings.<variant>.strict.json`. Eight files for the canonical identity (no canary), twelve with a canary. The opt-in is the admin's choice of the `.strict` file: no build switch and no config key, so the canonical tree serves organizations that want the allowlist and those that do not. **Builder:** `:561-570` writes every `adminTemplates(...)` document with `jsonDocument`; `CLAUDE_MANAGED_SETTINGS` (`:77`) is replaced by the function; the Claude `installNote` (`:247-249`) names `admin/claude/managed-settings.stable.json` and says "the `.strict` file of the same name also admits only this marketplace; `tag` pins this release"; the Copilot section gains an `installNote`: "An enterprise rolls this plugin out by committing `admin/copilot/managed-settings.stable.json` to its `.github-private` repository as `copilot/managed-settings.json` (see the fork guide, "Roll the plugin out to your organization")." The legacy path `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` stays for one compatibility window (decision S3 of file 2) as a byte copy of `admin/claude/managed-settings.tag.strict.json` (the same four keys, the allowlist now last), written by the builder beside `adminTemplates`'s output, and goes at the next major release. **Docs** `docs/enterprise-forks.md:1023-1086`: the JSON block becomes the Claude stable template rendered for this repository (`ref: "plugin-stable"`, `autoUpdate: true`, `requiredMinimumVersion`), the key list explains `autoUpdate` and says the allowlist is in the `.strict` files; the opening paragraph ("one file declares the marketplace, allows only that one, and turns the plugin on") no longer claims the allowlist by default. The rest of the section is file 3's (u3-rollout-guides). **CHANGELOG** `### Changed`: "**The managed-settings templates follow a channel, and the allowlist is opt-in.** The distribution tree carries Claude Code and Copilot templates under `admin/claude/` and `admin/copilot/`, one per channel with `autoUpdate: true` and one pinned to the release tag, each with a `.strict` twin that adds `strictKnownMarketplaces`. `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` stays as a copy of `admin/claude/managed-settings.tag.strict.json` until the next major release." **Follows:** `scripts/plugins/managed-settings.mjs:100-112` (one closure builds the declared source and the allowlist entry, so they cannot differ). **Surface pins moved:** `test/docsPages.test.ts:2320-2337` (four keys → the keys the shown block's options produce). |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** the canonical identity, **when** `renderClaudeManagedSettings(identity, { ref: "plugin-stable", autoUpdate: true })` runs, **then** its keys are exactly `extraKnownMarketplaces`, `enabledPlugins`, `requiredMinimumVersion` in that order, the entry is `{source:{source:"github",repo:"zomarit/stamity",ref:"plugin-stable"},autoUpdate:true}` (key order `source`, `autoUpdate`), `requiredMinimumVersion` is `2.1.277`, and there is no `strictKnownMarketplaces`. **Given** `strict: true` for each ref in `plugins/v1.9.0`, `plugin-stable`, `plugins/v2.0.0-rc.1`, **then** the last key is `strictKnownMarketplaces`, it holds exactly one entry, and that entry deep-equals the declared `source` (the property `test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts:101-115` holds today, kept). **Given** the tag variant, **then** `ref` is `plugins/v<version>` and the entry has no `autoUpdate` key. **Given** `renderCopilotManagedSettings(identity, { ref: "plugin-stable", autoUpdate: true })`, **then** its keys are `extraKnownMarketplaces`, `enabledPlugins` only, the entry's source is `{source:"github",repo,ref}`, and no key outside `COPILOT_MANAGED_SETTINGS_KEYS` appears; with `strict: true` the allowlist entry equals the declared source. **Given** the acme fork identity of `test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts:57-65`, **then** the strict tag variant's bytes equal the literal block at `:117-145` with `requiredMinimumVersion` moved above `strictKnownMarketplaces` (re-pinned with `TEST CHANGE, justified: the spec's REQ-PLUGIN-029 text puts the opt-in key last`). **Given** `adminTemplates` for the default identity and `clients` of all four, **then** it returns exactly the 8 paths `admin/claude/managed-settings.{tag,tag.strict,stable,stable.strict}.json` then the same 4 under `admin/copilot/`; with `channels.canary` configured, 12; with `clients: ["cursor"]`, none. **Given** a full distribution build, **then** every one of those files exists with bytes equal to `jsonDocument(document)`, `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` exists and equals `admin/claude/managed-settings.tag.strict.json` byte for byte, a second build with `--client claude,copilot` writes identical bytes for the files it writes, the leak gate over the `admin/` folder reports "0 hits", and a `--client cursor` build has no `admin/` (in `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`, replacing `:586-648`). **Given** the enterprise-forks rollout section, **then** its first `json` block equals `renderClaudeManagedSettings` for the canonical identity with the `ref` and `autoUpdate` read from the block (`test/docsPages.test.ts:2320-2337` re-pinned). |
| `edgeCases` | A channel template points at a branch that no promotion has created yet: the client cannot reach the marketplace; the README line says to promote before rolling a channel template out. An organization that serves a mirror edits `repo` in the declared source and, in a `.strict` file, in the allowlist entry together, as the guide already says (`docs/enterprise-forks.md:1078-1085`). A fork with a renamed plugin gets its own marketplace key and plugin id in every file (the renderer reads `identity.name`). Copilot's allowlist ref matching is undocumented: the `.strict` Copilot files carry the same source object, and the live check needs a Copilot Business or Enterprise organization, recorded `Not done:` in REQ-PLUGIN-044. Two marketplaces with one name cannot both be registered by a user (Claude, host-marketplace page); a user receives one channel's file, so the shared name `stamity` keeps the plugin id `stamity@stamity` stable when an admin moves a group between channels. |

APM 0.32.0 deploys to the same paths the CLI writes (`.claude/agents/<id>.md`, `.github/prompts/<id>.prompt.md`, …, `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:109-132`, re-observed by the probe) and skips a path it does not manage ("Skipping <path> — local file exists (not managed by APM). Use 'apm install --force' to overwrite.", `apm_cli/integration/base_integrator.py:239-240`). **New, the mode.** `InstallMode = "generated" \| "plugin-backed" \| "apm-backed"`; `INSTALL_MODES = ["generated", "plugin-backed", "apm-backed"]`; `MANIFEST_VERSION` does not move (an additive value on the `plugin` field's own precedent, `src/types/manifest.ts:373-382`; an engine at 1.11.0 refuses the unknown value at `collectEnumError`, so a downgrade fails loudly). `collectPluginErrors` refuses `clients` under `apm-backed` ("`plugin.clients` is not used under `apm-backed`; the targets in apm.yml decide which clients receive APM's classes"). `pluginOwnedClasses`: `if (readInstallMode(manifest) === "apm-backed") return new Set(APM_DELIVERED_CLASSES[tool]);` before today's logic. **New, `src/apm/layout.ts`** (pure, imports `src/types/core.ts` only, `PLAN_MAP` wave 1): `export const APM_DELIVERED_CLASSES: Readonly<Record<Tool, readonly ("agent" \| "skill" \| "command" \| "rule")[]>> = { claude: ["agent", "skill", "command", "rule"], copilot: ["agent", "skill", "command", "rule"], cursor: ["agent", "skill", "command", "rule"], codex: ["agent", "skill"] }` (APM's codex profile carries no command class and compiles instructions only on `apm compile`); `export const APM_CHARTER_ID = "stamity-charter"`; `export function classifyApmPath(path: string): { cls: "agent" \| "skill" \| "command" \| "rule" \| "charter"; id: string; readers: readonly Tool[] } \| null` over the table `.claude/agents/<id>.md` agent/claude, `.claude/commands/<id>.md` command/claude, `.claude/rules/<id>.md` rule/claude, `.claude/skills/<id>/…` skill/claude, `.github/agents/<id>.agent.md` agent/copilot, `.github/prompts/<id>.prompt.md` command/copilot, `.github/instructions/<id>.instructions.md` rule/copilot, `.cursor/agents/<id>.md` agent/cursor, `.cursor/commands/<id>.md` command/cursor, `.cursor/rules/<id>.mdc` rule/cursor, `.codex/agents/<id>.toml` agent/codex, `.agents/skills/<id>/…` skill/[cursor, copilot, codex]; a rule whose id is `APM_CHARTER_ID` is class `charter`; anything else → `null`. A test holds the table equal to `TARGET_LAYOUT` in `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:109-132` (the smoke stays dependency-free, so the two are twins held equal, as `DEFAULT_CLI_PACKAGE_NAME` is, `src/cli/kit/packageName.ts:31-42`). **New, `src/apm/deliveries.ts`** (pure, wave 3): `export interface ApmDelivery { tool: Tool; id: string; cls: string; apmPaths: string[]; cliPaths: string[]; dependency: string }`; `export function findDeliveredTwice({ tools, ledger, dependencies }): ApmDelivery[]` — the CLI delivers a ledger row to its own `adapter` (a row with `artifactId === "charter"` is `(charter, APM_CHARTER_ID)`, otherwise `classifyApmPath(row.path)`); APM delivers a deployed path to every selected tool among its readers; a finding is a selected tool and an id both deliver; `dependency` is `name`, else `repoUrl` plus `"/" + virtualPath` when `virtualPath` is set, else `"an unnamed APM dependency"` (file 1 reads a missing `repo_url` as `null`); `export function apmCoverageGaps({ tools, dependencies }): { tool: Tool; missing: string[] }[]` — under `apm-backed`, a selected tool and a class of `APM_DELIVERED_CLASSES[tool]` that no deployed path delivers; `export function isPackageDependency(dep, { repositorySlug, packageId, shippedIds }): boolean` — `repoUrl` normalized equals the slug (case-insensitive; a `null` `repoUrl` never does), or `name` is `packageId` or `<packageId>-charter`, or a deployed path classifies to an id the corpus ships (u2-apm-floor-current reads it). **New, emission.** `src/emit/ownership.ts` `export function projectionRowOwners(manifest, readers, row: { artifactType: string; artifactId: string }, apmRuleSkillIds: ReadonlySet<string>): Tool[]` — under `apm-backed`: `readers` minus each tool whose `APM_DELIVERED_CLASSES` has `skill` when the row is a `skill` row or a `rule` row whose id is in `apmRuleSkillIds`; otherwise exactly `sharedProjectionOwners(manifest, readers)`. `src/emit/planner.ts:851-865`: `owners = file.origin === "pack" ? projectionReaders : projectionRowOwners(ctx.manifest, projectionReaders, file, apmRuleSkillIds)`, where `apmRuleSkillIds` is empty outside `apm-backed` and otherwise `demotedRuleIds("copilot", <reachable rule items>.map(ruleDeliveryInputOf), RULE_DELIVERY_DEFAULT)` — the predicate the APM generator ships its rule-skills by (`scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:485-489`), so codex keeps its other demoted rules as `.agents/skills/stamity-<id>/` (APM's codex profile delivers no rules) and drops the two APM ships as skills. Net effect, tools all four under `apm-backed`: the CLI writes `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `AGENTS.override.md`, `.claude/settings.json`, the review gate, the hook configuration and scripts of all four clients, MCP documents, `.codex/config.toml`, codex's nine touchpoints (`.agents/skills/st-<id>/`, APM delivers codex no commands) and its rule-skills outside the APM set, and `.stamity/` state; no file of the four classes for claude, copilot or cursor, and no `.codex/agents/`. **New, entry and switch.** `init --install-mode <mode>` (`new Option(…).choices(["generated", "apm-backed"])`, default `generated`) passes `plugin: { mode: "apm-backed" }` to `applyInit` and prints "install mode apm-backed — APM delivers agents, skills, commands and rules; this setup writes the charter, hooks, MCP wiring and state". `config set installMode <generated \| apm-backed>` (a `KEY_SPECS` row: `choices: ["generated", "apm-backed"]`, `read: manifest.plugin?.mode ?? null`, `resolve: readInstallMode`, `apply` sets `draft.plugin = raw === "apm-backed" ? { mode: "apm-backed" } : undefined` and refuses when the current mode is `plugin-backed`: "this setup is plugin-backed; run clean, then init"). Order for a generated repository: `config set installMode apm-backed`, `sync` (reclaims the CLI's copies), then `apm install`, then `check`. The refusal remedy u1-foreign-paths prints names `init --install-mode apm-backed`. **New, the duplicates row** (`plugin-duplicates`, `src/cli/commands/check.ts:991-1015`; the id stays — `check --json` consumers and `docs/troubleshooting.md` key on it): it runs in every mode. Inputs: `readApmLock`, `apm.yml` parsed with `yaml`. Findings: (1) today's plugin findings for recorded clients, severity unchanged (`warn` under `generated`, `fail` under `plugin-backed` — the spec delta's suggested default (a) for REQ-PLUGIN-019); their `apm` source now reads the lock when it is readable — a recorded client's carried class (`agent`, `skill`, `command`) delivered by any lock path → finding, so `acme/stamity-mirror/apm#plugins/v1.12.0` is found (inbox `:67`) — and falls back to today's `apm.yml` line match only when no lock exists; (2) `findDeliveredTwice` findings, `fail` in every mode, one line each: `<tool>: <id> (<cls>) arrives twice — APM <dependency> at <apmPaths>, this setup at <cliPaths> — <remedy>`; remedy under `generated`: "switch with `<packageCommand("config set installMode apm-backed")>` then `<packageCommand("sync")>`, or remove <dependency> from apm.yml and run apm install"; under `apm-backed`: "remove <dependency> from apm.yml and run apm install; this setup writes <cls>"; for the charter: "remove the charter package from apm.yml; this setup writes the charter"; (3) an unparseable `apm.yml` or an unreadable lock, `fail` in every mode, naming the file and the first line of the error (inbox `:68`); (4) under `apm-backed`: no lock → `fail` "apm-backed, but apm.lock.yaml is absent: run apm install"; each `apmCoverageGaps` row → `fail` "<tool>: APM delivers no <classes>; add <tool> to the targets in apm.yml and run apm install". The row's status is the worst finding's. With no plugin record, no `apm.yml` and no lock, the detail stays byte-identical: "no client records a plugin, so nothing can duplicate". **New, the rest.** `pluginOwnedLines` prints `apm-owned` instead of `plugin-owned` under `apm-backed` (the JSON `pluginOwned` keeps its shape). `clean` under `apm-backed` closes with "APM's files stay: remove the dependency from apm.yml and run apm install". **Boundaries:** `PLAN_MAP` rows `"src/apm/layout.ts": { unit: "u2-apm-backed-mode", wave: 1 }` and `"src/apm/deliveries.ts": { unit: "u2-apm-backed-mode", wave: 3 }` (it imports `layout.ts` and the ledger and lock types; wave 3 sits above file 1’s wave-2 `src/detect/apmLock.ts`, another unit’s module, and below its importers `plugin/probe.ts`, wave 14, and `check.ts`, wave 15). **Docs:** `docs/troubleshooting.md`'s `plugin-duplicates` row says it runs in every mode, names the APM findings and their remedies, and that an unparseable `apm.yml` or lock fails; `docs/getting-started.md` gains "APM and the CLI together" (the mode, the two commands, the order). **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**An `apm-backed` install mode.** A repository that installs the APM package and also runs the CLI sets it with `init --install-mode apm-backed` or `config set installMode apm-backed`; the CLI then writes only what APM does not carry — the charter, hooks, MCP wiring and state, and Codex's touchpoints and rules — and `check` fails, in every mode, any agent, skill, command, rule or charter that reaches a client from both the CLI and an APM dependency, read from `apm.lock.yaml`." **Follows:** `src/manifest/manifest.ts:1155-1182` (one resolver decides what the recorded mode means) and `src/cli/commands/plugin/probe.ts:822-869` (`collectPluginDuplicates`: repository-level reads once, then pure per-client answers) |

**given** the same with `clients`, **then** validation names `plugin.clients`. **Given** `parseApmLock` over the transcribed 0.32.0 lock with only its `deployments[]` rows and over the same lock with `deployed_files`, **then** `apmDeployedPaths` is equal for both and each dependency's `virtualPath` and `version` read as the lock writes them (`null` when absent); A lock `.agents/skills/<id>` directory entry beside its files → classified once. Ledger and lock paths are compared as POSIX strings, never through `node:path` (the Windows leg; `.stamity/learnings/the-local-test-gate-is-weaker-than-ci.md`). A tool deselected later → coverage reads the selected tools only. `apm-backed` with codex plus cursor or copilot → codex's touchpoints in `.agents/skills/` are also listed by cursor and copilot next to APM's commands: a cross-read, not a second route, so the row stays green and the capability matrix's residue section names it (Concerns C8). `plugin setup` on an `apm-backed` repository → today's refusal on an existing manifest. |

| `interfaces` | **Current.** Core hook rows carry no matcher (`src/emit/hooksInfra.ts:450-465`); only user and pack hooks may (`:485-491`, `src/hooks/userHooks.ts:351-368`). Copilot's file keys PascalCase event names under `version: 1` with each row's `matcher` (`src/adapters/copilot.ts:925-934`); the runner it launches reads `row.matcher` nowhere (`src/hooks/portableRunner.ts:138-209`). VS Code's Local hooks ignore matchers ("Local parses nested commands but ignores matcher values, so every command for the event runs") and read `.github/hooks/*.json` in a native form (PascalCase, no `version`), a Copilot form (numeric `version`, camelCase) and `.claude/settings*.json` only with `chat.useClaudeHooks`; the Copilot session target runs "the same SDK hook implementation as Copilot CLI" (code.visualstudio.com/docs/agent-customization/hooks and its reference, approved 2026-09-30). Two other clients run this repository's `.claude/settings.json` hooks beside their own: Cursor loads them by default ("Include Third-Party Plugins, Skills, and Other Configs … on by default", "All matching hooks from every source run", Claude `PreToolUse`/`Stop`/`SubagentStop`/`SessionStart` mapped to Cursor events, cursor.com/docs/reference/third-party-hooks), and the Copilot CLI reads `.claude/settings.json` hooks as a repository source (docs.github.com hooks reference; CLI changelog 1.0.12). So in a repository set up for Claude plus Cursor or Copilot, the Claude guard, session-start, tamper-notice and review-gate scripts also run inside the other client, on that client's payload. Documented payload keys: Cursor's common fields include `cursor_version`, `conversation_id`, `workspace_roots` (cursor.com/docs/hooks); Copilot's both formats carry `timestamp` (docs.github.com hooks reference); Claude's common fields carry neither (code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks). The Copilot root README says "VS Code is not on it (read 2026-09-20)" (`scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs:267-269`, pinned at `test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts:550`). VS Code is not installed on the maintainer's machine today (`/usr/local/bin/code` resolves into `Cursor.app`). **New harness `scripts/qa/hook-payload-probe.mjs`** (follows `scripts/qa/hook-runs.mjs:1-60`: one fixture per client from `createFixture`, the hook's own JSONL as the instrument, a missing client is `not-run` with its reason, every quoted line through `scripts/qa/redact.mjs`). Usage: `node scripts/qa/hook-payload-probe.mjs --client <claude\|copilot\|cursor\|codex\|vscode> [--mode observe\|block-once] [--out <dir>]`. It writes into a scratch fixture a `probe.mjs` that appends `{ label, receivedAt, stdin, envNames }` (`envNames` = sorted environment variable NAMES, never values) to `hook-events.jsonl` and exits 0 silently; in `block-once` it holds the first completion event once with the client's documented continue shape (`{"decision":"block","reason":"Reply with exactly HOOK-CONTINUED"}` for Claude, Codex and Copilot; `{"followup_message":"Reply with exactly HOOK-CONTINUED"}` for Cursor) and records whether a second completion event and the marker followed. It registers the probe on every event both this unit and `u2-review-gate-all` read: SessionStart, PreToolUse, Stop/agentStop/stop, SubagentStop/subagentStop, TaskCompleted (Claude), SessionEnd; plus two variants measuring the cross-client imports — a Cursor fixture with only `.claude/settings.json`, and a Copilot fixture with `.github/hooks/` plus `.claude/settings.json`. Commands (flags read from `--help` on this machine): `claude -p "<prompt>" --output-format stream-json --verbose --include-hook-events --no-session-persistence` (once with `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TODO_TOOLS=1`); `COPILOT_ALLOW_ALL=true copilot -p "<prompt>" --allow-all-tools --no-auto-update`; `agent --trust -p "<prompt>" --output-format stream-json`; Codex and VS Code are maintainer steps (below). The prompt asks the agent to delegate one README read to a project subagent named `probe` whose scripted final text is `verdict: approve` / `confidence: high`, then reply done. **Committed fixtures** `test/fixtures/hooks/payloads/<client>/<event>.json`: one payload per client and event, with the values of `session_id`, `sessionId`, `conversation_id`, `generation_id`, `prompt_id`, `turn_id`, `agent_id`, `agentId`, `transcript_path`, `transcriptPath`, `agent_transcript_path`, `cwd`, `workspace_roots`, `user_email`, `model`, `model_id`, `scratchpad_dir` and `timestamp` replaced by `"<redacted:string>"`, and any free-text field kept only when it equals the scripted reply; `index.json` records per client the version, the date, the command, and per event `fired`, `continuedAfterHold` and the key list; the README carries each file's `shasum -a 256` (the convention of `test/fixtures/plugins/README.md:1-20`). **Maintainer steps, recorded with their outcome:** Codex — add `[projects."<fixture absolute path>"] trust_level = "trusted"` to `~/.codex/config.toml`, run `codex exec --dangerously-bypass-hook-trust --json "<prompt>"` in the fixture, then remove the entry (the trust confound of `.stamity/learnings/codex-hooks-default-on-and-codex-exec-ran-none.md`); VS Code — install the current stable VS Code and GitHub Copilot Chat, open the VS Code fixture with the VS Code binary (not `code`), trust it, and send the prompt once with Session Target "Local" and once with "Copilot". A step not performed is `Not done:` with the maintainer as owner, never a pass. **New tables in `src/hooks/model.ts`:** `export const FOREIGN_INVOCATION_SIGNATURES: readonly { client: Tool; payloadKey?: string; envName?: string; citation: { url: string; accessDate: string }; measured: string }[]` — seeded with `{ client: "cursor", payloadKey: "cursor_version", citation: { url: "https://cursor.com/docs/hooks", accessDate: "2026-10-01" } }` and, for Copilot, the key or environment name the fixtures show in every Copilot payload and in no Claude payload (none chosen without that evidence); and `export const HOOK_EVENT_ALIASES: Readonly<Record<CanonicalHookEvent, readonly string[]>>` — each canonical event's wire names across the four clients (e.g. `stop`: `Stop`, `agentStop`, `stop`; `user_prompt_submit`: `UserPromptSubmit`, `userPromptSubmitted`, `beforeSubmitPrompt`), cited per name. **Runner self-filter** (`buildPortableHookRunner`, after `input` is built at `:162`): (a) when `row.matcher` is set and `row.event` is `pre_tool_use` or `post_tool_use`, the runner tests `new RegExp("^(?:" + row.matcher + ")$", "i")` against `input.tool_name` (`*` and the empty string match every tool); no match → exit 0 with no stdout, except Cursor `pre_tool_use`, which writes `{"permission":"allow"}` as the runner already does for a silent child (`:209`); an invalid matcher regex → the runner's own fault path (`safeError("Invalid hook matcher")`); (b) when the payload names its own event (`payload.hook_event_name` or `payload.hookEventName`, read before the runner overwrites it at `:162`) and that name is not in `HOOK_EVENT_ALIASES[row.event]`, exit 0 with no stdout. **Claude-script self-filter** (`src/hooks/scripts.ts`): a generated snippet `FOREIGN_INVOCATION` beside `READ_STDIN` defines `invokedByAnotherClient(payload)` over the JSON-embedded `FOREIGN_INVOCATION_SIGNATURES`; each of the four Claude-layout scripts (`stamity-session-start.mjs`, `stamity-pre-tool-use-guard.mjs`, `stamity-config-tamper-notice.mjs`, `stamity-review-gate.mjs`) calls it right after `readPayload()` and, on a match, writes `stamity: skipped — <client> runs its own copy of this hook` to stderr and exits 0 without deciding; the snippet is emitted only for `tool === "claude"` (the one client whose configuration other clients import). **README** `scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs:267-269` becomes: "VS Code loads this root as an agent plugin (Agent Plugins 1.0, generally available 2026-08-12; `chat.plugins.enabled`, on by default since 1.116) and also picks up a Copilot CLI install from `~/.copilot/installed-plugins/`; its skills appear as `/stamity:<id>`. VS Code's Local hooks ignore matchers, so every hook here decides from its own payload (loaded on VS Code <version>, <date>)." — version and date from the walk, or "not walked; see the route proofs" if the walk is `Not done:`. **VS Code walk (`vscode-load.md`):** build the distribution (`node scripts/build-plugin-runtime.mjs` then `node scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs --out dist/plugins --runtime <runtime>`), install the Copilot root through `copilot plugin marketplace add ./dist/plugins` and `copilot plugin install stamity@stamity`, open a plugin-backed fixture in VS Code, and record: the Agent Plugins view lists stamity at the version; Configure Skills lists `/stamity:st-learn`; the session-start hook ran; in a repository-mode fixture a user hook with matcher `Edit` wrote no line for a read tool call; and which catalog VS Code reads when `zomarit/stamity` is added with no ref (the observation REQ-PLUGIN-034 leaves to execution). **CHANGELOG** `### Fixed`: "**Hooks decide from their own payload.** A hook with a matcher no longer runs on every tool where the client ignores matchers (VS Code), and the Claude hooks stop acting when Cursor or the Copilot CLI runs them from `.claude/settings.json` beside their own." **Follows:** `scripts/qa/hook-runs.mjs:1-60` (harness posture), `test/fixtures/plugins/README.md:1-20` (vendored facts with digests), `src/hooks/model.ts:92-121` (guarantees as cited data). **Surface pins moved:** none. **Regenerate in the same change:** `npx vitest run test/corpus/emissionGoldens.test.ts --update` (the file first, `--update` last — learning `vitest-update-flag-takes-an-optional-value`) and, for the dogfood, `npm run build && node dist/cli.js sync`. |

**with** `toolName: "edit"`, **then** the child runs once (in `test/hooks/portableRunner.test.ts`). **Given** the cursor runner and a non-matching `pre_tool_use` payload, **then** stdout is exactly `{"permission":"allow"}`. **Given** a `stop` row and a payload whose `hook_event_name` is `PreToolUse`, **then** exit 0 and no stdout. **Given** each captured Cursor fixture and each captured Copilot-import fixture, **when** the four generated Claude-layout scripts run on it, **then** each exits 0 with nothing on stdout and the stderr line names the client; a walk not performed is `Not done:` with its owner. |

A user hook matcher written in Claude tool names (`Bash`) never matches VS Code's own tool names: the hook does not run there, which is what its matcher asked for; the docs (file 3) say matchers are client vocabulary. A payload that is not an object or has no tool name: the runner's existing fault and empty-payload paths apply before the filter. The fixtures never hold a value of an identity field (the redaction list above) — a captured line with an unredacted email or path fails the probe before it writes. Windows: the runner change is plain JavaScript inside the generated script; the probe harness runs on the maintainer's machine only and is not part of the suite. |

### u2-review-gate-all — the review gate holds a run's completion on every client that can hold it

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-review-gate-all |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-039 |
| `files` | `src/hooks/model.ts` (`CLIENT_EXTENSION_EVENTS` `:143-190`; new `REVIEW_GATE_WIRING` table), `src/hooks/scripts.ts` (`:1957-2010` gate constants; `ReviewGateScriptOptions` `:2032-2045`; `buildReviewGateScript` `:2090-2930`), `src/adapters/claude.ts` (`:237-255` `REVIEW_GATE_EVENTS`; `:828-845` `buildReviewGate`; `:906-937` wiring in `buildSettingsJson`), `src/adapters/copilot.ts` (rows `:313-321`; `buildCopilotHooksJson` `:925-934`), `src/adapters/cursor.ts` (the hooks rows `:485-499` and `buildHooksJson`), `src/adapters/codex.ts` (the hooks rows and the `.codex/hooks.json` builder `buildHooksJson` `:803-830`, whose entries carry `command` and `commandWindows`), `src/emit/capabilityMatrix.ts` (the `review-gate` cap per client), `docs/capability-matrix.md` (regenerated), `src/cli/commands/check.ts` (new probe `review-gate`; `runDoctor` `:1040-1070`), `docs/troubleshooting.md` (a `review-gate` row in the section "What `check` prints"), `test/hooks/scripts.test.ts`, `test/adapters/copilot.test.ts`, `test/adapters/cursor.test.ts`, `test/adapters/codex.test.ts`, `test/adapters/claude.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts` (`:474-520`), `test/ci/pluginPackages.claude.test.ts` (`:445-470`), `test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginPackages.cursor.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginPackages.codex.test.ts` (each root's hook configuration), `test/corpus/__snapshots__/emissionGoldens.test.ts.snap` (regenerated), `.claude/settings.json` and `.stamity/generated/hooks/claude/*` (the dogfood, regenerated by `sync`), `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** The gate holds only `TaskCompleted` (`REVIEW_GATE_COMPLETION_EVENTS = ["TaskCompleted"]`, `src/hooks/scripts.ts:1984`) and counts rounds on `SubagentStop`, wired by the Claude adapter alone from the two Claude rows of `CLIENT_EXTENSION_EVENTS` (`src/adapters/claude.ts:253-255`, `:935-937`); it reads the run from `session_id`/`run_id` (`:2737`), the finishing agent from `agent_type`/`subagent_type` (`:2738`), the event from `hook_event_name` (`:2739`), the verdict from `last_assistant_message`/`lastAssistantMessage` (`:1998`); a hold exits 2 with the message on stderr (`:2922-2940`, `BLOCK_EXIT` = `BLOCKING_EXIT_CODE`); fault paths open the gate (`:2847-2885`). **What the 2026-10-01 docs say against that:** `TaskCompleted` fires only when the TaskUpdate tool marks a task done or a teammate ends with open tasks, and the task tools are off by default on models newer than Opus 4.7 unless `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TODO_TOOLS=1` (code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks; tools-reference; changelog 2.1.233), so on the models this project runs the gate likely never holds — measured by `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`'s fixtures; the `TaskCompleted` note (`src/hooks/model.ts:180`) says its message reaches "the operator rather than … the model", and the page says stderr is "fed back to the model"; the `SubagentStop` note (`:187`) says exit 2 shows a message "only the operator sees", and the page says a block "keeps the subagent running and delivers reason to the subagent as its next instruction". Each client's completion event and how it continues the agent (docs, 2026-10-01): Claude `Stop` — exit 2 or `{"decision":"block","reason"}`, guard `stop_hook_active`, cap 8 consecutive; Copilot CLI `agentStop`/`Stop` — JSON `{"decision":"block","reason"}` only (exit 2 is a warning), guard `stop_hook_active`, cap 8; `subagentStop`/`SubagentStop` carries `agentType`/`agentName` and `response` (camelCase) or `agent_type` and `last_assistant_message` (PascalCase); Codex `Stop` — exit 2 or `decision:"block"` becomes "a new continuation prompt", `SubagentStop` carries `agent_type`, `last_assistant_message`, `session_id` = the parent's; Cursor `stop` — `{"followup_message":"…"}` is submitted as the next user message, `loop_count` input, per-hook `loop_limit` (default 5), and `subagentStop` carries `subagent_type`, `summary`, `status` but no full final text; VS Code Local `Stop`/`SubagentStop` carry no final-message field. **Step 1 — read the measurement.** From `test/fixtures/hooks/payloads/index.json` (written by `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`), for each client: (a) did the completion event fire headless, (b) did a hold continue the agent (`continuedAfterHold`), (c) does the subagent-stop payload carry the finishing agent's id and its final text, (d) does the run key match between the two events. A client is wired only when (a), (b), (c) and (d) all hold; otherwise it is prose-only with the failing letter as its reason. **New data in `src/hooks/model.ts`:** `export interface ReviewGateWiring { tool: Tool; completionEvents: readonly string[]; roundEvents: readonly string[]; holdOutput: "exit-2" \| "json-decision-block" \| "cursor-followup"; runKeyFields: readonly string[]; agentFields: readonly string[]; messageFields: readonly string[]; measured: { clientVersion: string; date: string; fixture: string } }` and `export const REVIEW_GATE_WIRING: readonly ReviewGateWiring[]` plus `export const REVIEW_GATE_PROSE_ONLY: readonly { tool: Tool; reason: string; measured: { clientVersion: string; date: string } \| null }[]`, together covering each of the four tools exactly once (asserted like `CLIENT_HOOK_GUARANTEES`). Expected rows, each confirmed or overturned by the fixtures: claude `completionEvents: ["Stop", "TaskCompleted"]`, `roundEvents: ["SubagentStop"]`, `holdOutput: "exit-2"`; copilot `["Stop"]` / `["SubagentStop"]` (PascalCase keys, so the payload is snake_case) / `"json-decision-block"`; codex `["Stop"]` / `["SubagentStop"]` / `"exit-2"` (prose-only if `codex exec` ran no hook even with the folder trusted); cursor prose-only unless `subagentStop.summary` carries the reviewer's `verdict:` line. The two Claude rows of `CLIENT_EXTENSION_EVENTS` get notes that say what the page says (`TaskCompleted` stderr reaches the model; a `SubagentStop` block keeps the subagent running), and the gate keeps never holding on a round event. **Gate script:** `ReviewGateScriptOptions` gains `wiring: ReviewGateWiring`; the generated constants `COMPLETION_EVENTS`, `MESSAGE_FIELDS` and the field lists of `:2737-2739` come from it (`conversation_id` joins the run-key fields for Cursor); a hold writes per `holdOutput` — `exit-2`: the message on stderr and exit 2, as today; `json-decision-block`: `{"decision":"block","reason":<message>}` on stdout and exit 0; `cursor-followup`: `{"followup_message":<message>}` on stdout and exit 0. A continuation cap: each run's state entry gains `holds` (consecutive holds since its last recorded round), a recorded round resets it to 0, and at `HOLD_CAP = 3` the gate releases with reason code `CONTINUATION_CAP` and the message "Review round <n> of <cap> is still open after 3 held completions; the run ends here as BLOCKED_FAILURE with the open findings attached." — bounding every client whatever its own cap (Codex documents none). `REVIEW_GATE_STATE_SCHEMA` moves to a new value and a state file of the old schema reads as `STATE_ABSENT` for the run (the gate opens once, then records anew). The `layout` option (`generated`/`container`) is unchanged. **Adapters.** Claude: `REVIEW_GATE_EVENTS` derives from the wiring row (completion plus round events); the `Stop` entry joins any canonical `Stop` rows in `buildSettingsJson` rather than replacing them; the comment at `:240-252` ("`Stop` would gate every session") is rewritten: the gate holds only a run with a recorded round and no approval, and at most `HOLD_CAP` times. Copilot, Codex, Cursor (where wired): each emits `.stamity/generated/hooks/<tool>/stamity-review-gate.mjs` from `buildReviewGateScript` with its wiring and wires it directly (`node <path>`, not through the portable runner, whose event set is the six canonical events, `src/hooks/portableRunner.ts:130`) on its completion and round events — Copilot under the PascalCase keys `Stop`/`SubagentStop` in `.github/hooks/stamity.json`, Codex under `Stop`/`SubagentStop` in `.codex/hooks.json` (with `commandWindows` beside `command`, as `buildHooksJson` writes every entry), Cursor under `stop`/`subagentStop` in `.cursor/hooks.json` with `loop_limit: 3`; each row's owner is `<tool>-review-gate`, and that id joins the adapter's `HOOK_INFRA_ARTIFACT_IDS` (`src/adapters/copilot.ts:270-273`, `cursor.ts:280`, `codex.ts:292`) so a plugin that carries hooks owns it. The plugin roots need no client-module edit: `.stamity/generated/hooks/<tool>/` rows land in `hooks/` by the existing prefix rules (`scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs:104`, `cursor.mjs:78`, `codex.mjs:92`), and the hook configs are re-addressed as today. **check:** a new probe `const id = "review-gate"` placed after `plugin-duplicates`, before `invariants`; status `pass`; detail one line per selected tool: `claude: enforced on Stop, TaskCompleted (measured Claude Code 2.1.286, 2026-10-…)` or `cursor: prose ladder — <reason>`. **Capability matrix:** each client's `review-gate` cap renders from the table (`docs/capability-matrix.md:151` today names Claude only). **CHANGELOG** `### Changed`: "**The review gate holds a run's completion on every client that can hold it.** It rides Claude Code's `Stop` as well as `TaskCompleted`, and the Copilot CLI's and Codex's `Stop` where measured, and stops holding after three held completions with no new round; `check` names, per client, whether the gate or the prose ladder applies." **Follows:** `src/hooks/model.ts:92-121` (guarantees as data rendered into docs and adapters) and `src/hooks/scripts.ts:2090` (one generated gate body, parameterized by the wiring adapter). **Surface pins moved:** `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts:474` ("the fourteen doctor rows" → fifteen, name and array, with a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` line) and the doctor table of `docs/troubleshooting.md` (set-equal to the probes, `test/docsPages.test.ts:2755-2785`). **Regenerate in the same change:** `npx vitest run test/corpus/emissionGoldens.test.ts --update` (the file first, `--update` last), `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs`, and, for the dogfood, `npm run build && node dist/cli.js sync`. |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** each wired client's captured subagent-stop fixture carrying the scripted `verdict: request-changes` text, **when** the generated gate runs on it, **then** the state file records round 1 for that run key and the gate does not hold; **given** that client's captured completion fixture with the same run key, **then** the gate holds in the client's form — exit 2 with the message on stderr (claude, codex), stdout `{"decision":"block","reason":…}` with exit 0 (copilot), stdout `{"followup_message":…}` with exit 0 (cursor, if wired); **given** a later round with `verdict: approve` and `confidence: high`, **then** the completion passes with exit 0 and empty stdout (in `test/hooks/scripts.test.ts`). **Given** three consecutive held completions with no new round, **then** the fourth exits 0 with reason code `CONTINUATION_CAP` on stderr. **Given** a completion for a run with no recorded round, **then** exit 0, empty stdout, and the state file unchanged. **Given** a state file of the previous schema, **then** the gate opens once with `STATE_ABSENT` and records the next round under the new schema. **Given** `REVIEW_GATE_WIRING` and `REVIEW_GATE_PROSE_ONLY`, **then** the four tools appear exactly once across the two, and each wiring row's `measured.fixture` names a file under `test/fixtures/hooks/payloads/` that exists. **Given** a sync for each tool, **then** a wired tool's hook configuration names the gate script on exactly its `completionEvents` and `roundEvents` and nowhere else, and a prose-only tool's names it nowhere (in `test/adapters/<tool>.test.ts`); the Claude root's `hooks/hooks.json` carries the gate on `Stop`, `TaskCompleted` and `SubagentStop` (`test/ci/pluginPackages.claude.test.ts:445-470` re-pinned with justification). **Given** `check` on a repository selecting all four tools, **then** the `review-gate` row passes and names each tool once with "enforced on" or "prose ladder"; the doctor list pin and the troubleshooting table both carry the new row. **Given** `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs`, **then** `docs/capability-matrix.md` shows the gate per client and `git diff --exit-code` passes after regeneration. |
| `edgeCases` | Claude's auto permission mode hands a subagent's report back through the SubagentHandback tool, and `last_assistant_message` "is not the delivered report" (code.claude.com hooks, SubagentStop): the verdict then reads as unrecorded and the gate holds to `HOLD_CAP` — a follow-up row; the fixture records whether the reviewer's text arrived. A non-work session never has a recorded round, so `Stop` never holds it. A resumed session with a stale open round is held at most `HOLD_CAP` times. Cursor's imported `.claude/settings.json` `SubagentStop` no longer double-counts a round, because `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode` made the Claude script skip a Cursor payload. Codex `exec` running no project hook even when trusted → Codex is prose-only with that reason, and the TUI result, if the maintainer runs it, is recorded beside it. Windows: the gate's lock and rename budgets are unchanged; the new output forms are stdout writes. |

**when** `sync` runs, **then** `.cursor/rules/` is written. **Given** generated and plugin-backed setups selecting Cursor, **then** `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` holds one managed block whose `##` headings are exactly the descriptions of the admitted `review`-tagged rules, each glob-scoped rule's globs follow its heading, and `[...content].length <= 30000` (in `test/adapters/cursor.test.ts`). **Given** a fixture review rule whose body pushes the file past 30,000 characters, **then** the row carries `sourceRefusal` naming 30,000 and `check` exits 1 with nothing written. **Given** a selection that admits no `review` rule, **then** no `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` row is emitted. **Given** an existing `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` with user text, **when** `sync` then `clean -y` run, **then** the user text is byte-identical after both and only the block was added and then removed. **Given** `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs`, **then** the Cursor container row shows `rule` repository-owned and `git diff --exit-code` passes after regeneration. |

A nested `BUGBOT.md` in a subfolder is the team's own and untouched. A rule's description holding a newline or `#`: headings collapse whitespace like `yamlScalar` does (`src/adapters/copilot.ts:892-894`) and escape a leading `#`. The 100,000-character combined cap counts team and dashboard rules this engine cannot see; only the per-rule 30,000 limit is enforced here, and the block's sentence names the combined cap. Windows: content paths are POSIX in the ledger; the `.mdc` rows already pass the Windows leg. |
