Copilot CLI — "Agents and skills use first-found-wins precedence … the agent or skill in the plugin is silently ignored" (docs.github.com cli-plugin-reference); a personal agent outranks the project one. VS Code — plugin skills are prefixed `/<plugin>:<skill>` and listed beside workspace skills; no order for agents. Cursor — no documented order between a plugin and a project artifact of one name. Codex — "If two skills share the same `name`, Codex doesn't merge them; both can appear in skill selectors" (learn.chatgpt.com/docs build-skills); Codex agents are repository-owned under a plugin already (`scripts/plugins/clients/codex.mjs:46-48`). **Catalog:** `CatalogItem` gains `patchedBy?: "fork" \| "user"`, set on the merged object `applyOverlays` returns for that stage (absent on every unpatched item, so an overlay-free walk is byte-identical); `ProjectedFile` copies it beside `origin` at the three sites. **`src/roster/modelLadder.ts`:** `export function modelPinDiffers(modelClass: unknown, tool: Tool, models: ModelConfig \| undefined): boolean` = `resolveModelValue(cls, tool, pins, efforts) !== resolveModelValue(cls, tool)` or `resolveEffortValue(cls, tool, efforts) !== resolveEffortValue(cls, tool)` (false for a non-class value). **`src/emit/ownership.ts`:** `export type ProjectCopyCause = "user-override" \| "user-patch" \| "pack" \| "model-pin"`; `export function projectCopyCause(item: Pick<CatalogItem, "type" \| "origin" \| "patchedBy" \| "frontmatter">, tool: Tool, models: ModelConfig \| undefined): ProjectCopyCause \| null` — `pack` for origin `pack`, `user-override` for origin `user`, `user-patch` for `patchedBy: "user"`, `model-pin` for an agent whose `frontmatter.model_class` `modelPinDiffers`, else null (a fork-layer replacement or patch is null: the fork's own plugin build stages `fork/` and already carries it, `scripts/generate-plugin-packages.mjs:458-461`); `export function projectCopyKeys(items: readonly CatalogItem[], tool: Tool, models): Map<string, ProjectCopyCause>` keyed `<type>:<id>`. 100% coverage holds (`vitest.config.ts:123-128`). **Adapters:** each builds `projectCopyKeys(index.items, TOOL, ctx.manifest.models)` and passes as `exemptPaths` every row whose `<owner.artifactType>:<owner.artifactId>` is a key — Claude replaces its pack-only `packSkillPaths` with this set (skills, agents, commands); codex (skills; its agents and commands are repository-owned), copilot (agents, commands, skills), cursor (agents, commands-as-skills, skills); the planner's shared tree (`:865`) gives every reader a file whose `origin` is `pack` or `user` or whose `patchedBy` is `user`. **Precedence table `src/plugins/precedence.ts`** (wave 2, imports `src/types` only): `export const PLUGIN_COPY_PRECEDENCE: readonly { tool: Tool; cls: PluginOwnedClass; rule: "project-wins" \| "bare-name" \| "undocumented"; note: string; citation: { url: string; accessDate: string }; measured: { clientVersion: string; date: string } \| null }[]` — one row per (tool, carriable class): claude skill/command/agent `bare-name` ("the project copy owns the bare name; the plugin copy stays listed as `stamity:<id>`; a personal copy outranks both"); copilot skill/agent/command `project-wins` (note: "VS Code lists the plugin skill as `/stamity:<id>` beside it"); cursor every class `undocumented`; codex skill `undocumented`. **Measurement step** (the record `precedence-measurement.md`): Copilot — `copilot skill list --json` in a fixture holding a project override and the plugin installed (no model turn); Claude and Cursor — one headless listing turn each (`claude -p "List the subagent types and slash commands you can use, one per line" --plugin-dir <root>`; `agent --trust --plugin-dir <root> -p "<same>"`); Codex — the `/skills` picker in the TUI (maintainer step) or `Not done:`; a measured result fills `measured` and may tighten `undocumented` to `project-wins` only on a run where the plugin copy did not appear. **Reach report:** `export async function collectPluginReach(rootDir, manifest): Promise<PluginReachEntry[]>` in `probe.ts`, built from the same content roots `sync` plans with (bundled corpus, fork layer, `.stamity/overrides/`, installed packs — `src/cli/engine/emission.ts`, re-read for the builder) and `manifest.models`, for every recorded plugin client and each of its owned classes: `PluginReachEntry = { tool: Tool; class: PluginOwnedClass; id: string; cause: ProjectCopyCause; path: string; reaches: boolean; precedence: "project-wins" \| "bare-name" \| "undocumented"; reason?: string }`, with `reaches: true` when the ledger records the copy's path and `false` with `reason: "not written yet; run sync"` otherwise, and `reason` naming the client's rule when `precedence` is `undocumented`. `duplicatesForClient` skips a ledger row whose `<artifactType>:<artifactId>` is a reach entry's key for that tool. **check:** new probe `const id = "plugin-reach"` after `plugin-duplicates` (and after `review-gate` from `u2-review-gate-all`): `pass` with "no plugin client recorded" or "no customized artifact under a plugin-owned class"; `pass` listing each entry when every entry `reaches` with a documented rule; `warn` when any entry has `reaches: false` or `precedence: "undocumented"`, one line per entry: `cursor: agent stamity-reviewer (model-pin) at .cursor/agents/stamity-reviewer.md — project copy beside the plugin's; Cursor documents no order`. **plugin status --json** gains `reach: PluginReachEntry[]`; the human status prints one `reach` row per entry. The `unmanaged` remedy (`probe.ts:590-592`) keeps its words, now true. **Capability matrix:** a "Plugin copy precedence" section rendered from `PLUGIN_COPY_PRECEDENCE` with each citation and measured version. **CHANGELOG** `### Fixed`: "**Overrides, patches, packs and model pins reach a plugin-backed client.** Under a plugin, an override or patch of a plugin-owned agent, skill or command, an installed pack's skill, and an agent with a `stamity config` model pin are written as project-level copies; `check`'s new `plugin-reach` row and `plugin status --json` list each copy per client with the order that client documents, and `plugin-duplicates` no longer reports them." **Follows:** `src/emit/ownership.ts:139-161` (the adapter that knows names the exempt paths) and `src/hooks/model.ts:92-121` (per-client facts as cited data). **Surface pins moved:** `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts:474` (the doctor list, one more row, with a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` line), the doctor table of `docs/troubleshooting.md` (set-equal, `test/docsPages.test.ts:2755-2785`), and `test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts` `PLAN_MAP`. **Regenerate in the same change:** `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs`; generated mode emits no new row, so the golden snapshot and the Claude-only dogfood do not move (a moved snapshot row is a defect here). |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** a plugin-backed manifest recording claude `[agent, skill, command, hooks]` and `.stamity/overrides/agents/stamity-reviewer.md`, **when** `sync` runs, **then** `.claude/agents/stamity-reviewer.md` holds the override's body and a ledger row, and no other corpus agent is written. **Given** a user patch `.stamity/overrides/skills/qa/SKILL.customize.md` (the skill overlay path, `docs/customization.md:222`), **then** `.claude/skills/st-qa/SKILL.md` holds the merged body; **given** the same patch in a copilot-recorded repository, **then** the shared `.agents/skills/` copy of that skill holds it, owned by copilot. **Given** `models.pins` setting a class `stamity-reviewer` uses, **then** each recorded plugin client that carries agents (claude, copilot, cursor) gets that agent at project level with the pinned `model:`, and an agent whose class is unpinned gets none. **Given** an installed pack skill, **then** all four clients get it under a plugin, as Claude alone did before. **Given** a fork-layer patch only, **then** no copy is written. **Given** an overlay-free corpus, **then** `buildContentIndex`'s items deep-equal today's (no `patchedBy` key appears). **Given** the repository above, **when** `check` runs, **then** `plugin-duplicates` passes and `plugin-reach` lists `claude: agent stamity-reviewer (user-override) … bare-name`; **given** cursor recorded with a model pin, **then** `plugin-reach` warns naming "Cursor documents no order"; **given** `plugin status --json`, **then** `reach` holds the same entries with `reaches: true` (in `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/plugin.test.ts`). **Given** an override added after the last sync, **then** its entry reads `reaches: false` with "run sync" and the row warns. **Given** `PLUGIN_COPY_PRECEDENCE`, **then** it holds exactly one row per (tool, class) of `CARRIABLE_CLASSES`, each with a citation dated 2026-10-01 or later. **Given** `npx vitest run test/emit/ownership.test.ts --coverage`, **then** `src/emit/ownership.ts` stays at 100% statements, branches, functions and lines. |
| `edgeCases` | A user override that adds a new id the plugin does not carry is plain repository content and was never filtered; it still gets a reach entry only if its class is plugin-owned, with `precedence` for a name no plugin copy shares reported as `project-wins`. A user's personal copy (`~/.claude/skills/<id>`, a personal Copilot agent) outranks the project copy on those clients; the note says so and `check` cannot see it. Removing an override: the next `sync` stops emitting the copy and reclaims it through the ledger, so `plugin-duplicates` cannot flag a stale copy. A Claude bare name used by both the project copy and the plugin's namespaced copy: the corpus dispatches by bare name, so the project copy runs; whether the model, invoking a skill on its own judgement, picks `st-x` or `stamity:st-x` is undocumented, recorded by the measurement. Hooks are not reach entries: an accepted user or pack hook under a plugin that owns hooks stays the sync warning it is today (`src/emit/hooksInfra.ts:500-511`). |

Today's docs ask the operator to write the declaration by hand (`docs/plugins.md:152-163`). Vendor facts (2026-10-01): Claude reads `extraKnownMarketplaces` from project settings only after the folder is trusted, and a plugin whose catalog entry is an external source (the distribution's Claude entry is `git-subdir`) is not installed from project settings — each contributor sees "Plugin "<name>" is enabled in project settings but isn't installed here" until `claude plugin install <p>@<m> --scope project` (code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/org, plugins/loading); `claude plugin marketplace add <src> --scope project` writes `extraKnownMarketplaces` into project settings (cli-reference); the Copilot CLI reads `.github/copilot/settings.json` keys `enabledPlugins` ("Declarative plugin auto-install") and `extraKnownMarketplaces` (`source` required, `autoUpdate` "accepted … but currently ignored"), and also reads `.claude/settings.json`'s `enabledPlugins` and `extraKnownMarketplaces` (docs.github.com cli-config-dir-reference; CLI 1.0.12); the cloud agent reads `.github/copilot/settings.json`; VS Code reads either file as workspace plugin recommendations (code.visualstudio.com/docs/agent-customization/agent-plugins). **From `docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md (u1-settings-key-ownership)`:** `LedgerEntry.coOwned?: CoOwnership` (`src/types/manifest.ts`), where `members: Record<pointer, sha256>` maps each engine-owned JSON member — an RFC 6901 pointer of depth 1 (`/permissions`) or depth 2 (`/enabledPlugins/stamity@stamity`) — to the sha256 of `JSON.stringify(value)` as the engine wrote it, and `createdFile: true` marks a file the engine created; the pure module `src/manifest/jsonMembers.ts` exports `memberPointer`, `parseMemberPointer`, `memberHash`, `readMember`, `memberHashes`, `proveMember` and `removeOwnedMembers`; the settings reclaim reducer takes an `ownedMembers` option. **This unit adds** `setMember(doc, pointer, value)` to `jsonMembers.ts`, an `ownedMembers` option (depth-2 pointers, never also a depth-1 owned key) to `SettingsOwnership` and `planClaudeSettings`, and passes the declared pointers to `settingsCoOwnership` and `coOwnedReclaimReducers`. A declared member that already holds an equal value no ledger row wrote stays foreign: it is neither recorded nor removed. **New flag** (`configure`): `cmd.option("--declare <ref>", "also declare the plugin for this repository's contributors: a channel (stable, canary) or a distribution tag (plugins/v<version>); writes extraKnownMarketplaces and enabledPlugins into .claude/settings.json and .github/copilot/settings.json")`; on `status` it is a usage error (`--declare applies to plugin setup`, exit 2). **`src/cli/kit/packageName.ts`:** `export function distributionRefs(): { channels: Readonly<Record<string, string>>; tagPattern: string }` reading the running package's `stamity.distribution` with the defaults of `scripts/distribution-identity.mjs` (`channels` `{ stable: "plugin-stable" }`, `tagPattern` `plugins/v<version>`), cached like `repositorySlug`; a test reads those two literals out of the `.mjs` source and pins them equal (the device of `test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts:197-205`). **`src/cli/commands/plugin/declare.ts`** (new, wave 15 beside `setup.ts`): `export interface PluginDeclaration { marketplace: string; repo: string; ref: string }`; `export function resolveDeclaration(value: string, refs, identity: { name: string; slug: string \| null }): PluginDeclaration` — a configured channel name maps to its branch; a value equal to `tagPattern` with a `PLUGIN_VERSION`-valid version is taken as the tag; `canary` unconfigured → `CONFIG_ERROR` "--declare canary: this distribution configures no canary channel (stamity.distribution.channels.canary)."; anything else → `USAGE` "--declare takes a channel (stable, canary) or a distribution tag such as plugins/v1.12.0."; `slug === null` → `CONFIG_ERROR` "this package names no github.com repository, so there is no marketplace to declare."; `marketplace` = the package name without its scope (the catalog's `name`, `scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs:309`); `export async function existingDeclarations(rootDir, marketplace): Promise<string[]>` — the files (`.claude/settings.json`, `.github/copilot/settings.json`) whose parsed `extraKnownMarketplaces` already has the key `marketplace` (an `enabledPlugins` entry alone, the client's own install record, is not a declaration). **Setup:** `PluginSetupInput` gains `declaration?: PluginDeclaration`; `runSetup` resolves `--declare`, refuses before planning when `existingDeclarations` is non-empty (`VALIDATION_ERROR` "This repository already declares the <m> marketplace in <file>; remove that entry or run plugin setup without --declare.", exit 1, nothing written) and when no selected client is `claude` or `copilot` (`CONFIG_ERROR` "--declare writes the Claude Code and Copilot declarations; set up claude or copilot."); `planPluginSetup` returns `plugin: { mode: "plugin-backed", clients, declaration }`. **Manifest:** `PluginConfig` gains `declaration?: { marketplace: string; repo: string; ref: string }`; the validator refuses unknown keys, a `marketplace` outside `/^[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9.-]*[a-z0-9])?$/`, a `repo` not `owner/repo`, a `ref` failing the ref-name rule; `MANIFEST_VERSION` does not move (additive and optional, on the `ModelConfig` precedent, `src/types/manifest.ts:356-360`). **Emission.** Claude adapter, when `plugin.declaration` is set and `claude` is selected: `buildSettingsJson` adds `extraKnownMarketplaces: { <m>: { source: { source: "github", repo, ref } } }` and `enabledPlugins: { "<name>@<m>": true }`, and passes `/extraKnownMarketplaces/<m>` and `/enabledPlugins/<name>@<m>` (pointer-escaped) as `ownedMembers`; `claudeSettingsOwnedKeys` is unchanged. Copilot adapter, when declared and `copilot` is selected: a row `.github/copilot/settings.json` holding the same two objects, owner `{ adapter: "copilot", artifactId: "copilot-declaration", artifactType: "infra" }`, merged per pointer through `src/manifest/copilotSettings.ts` (the co-owned-JSON core of `claudeSettings.ts` with its own path, pointer list and link refusal; no `hooks` widening), registered at the four special-cased sites above and in `coOwnedReclaimReducers`. Both files carry identical values, so the Copilot CLI reading both cannot see two refs. The Copilot plugin container drops the path (`place` → `null`, repository-owned). **Output:** `runSetup` prints one line per declared file and, for Claude, the next step "each contributor runs `claude plugin install <name>@<m> --scope project` once: Claude Code does not install a plugin whose catalog entry is an external source from project settings"; the JSON result gains `declared: { file: string; marketplace: string; ref: string }[]`. **Docs:** `docs/plugins.md:152-163` replaces the hand-written declaration with `plugin setup --declare`. **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**`plugin setup --declare <channel\|tag>` declares the plugin for a repository's contributors.** It writes `extraKnownMarketplaces` and `enabledPlugins` into `.claude/settings.json` and `.github/copilot/settings.json` as keys the ledger owns one by one, so `clean` removes exactly those and keeps every key another tool wrote." **Follows:** `src/cli/commands/plugin/setup.ts:85-153` (refuse before planning), `src/manifest/claudeSettings.ts` (per-key co-ownership) and `src/cli/kit/packageName.ts:187-224` (running-package identity, cached). **Surface pins moved:** `docs/cli-reference.md` (regenerated by `node scripts/generate-docs.mjs`; byte-gated by its suite) and `test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts` `PLAN_MAP` (two rows); no new verb, so `src/cli.ts` counts, `ADVERTISED` and the two `test/docsPages.test.ts` verb arrays do not move. |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** a fresh repository and plugin roots for claude and copilot, **when** `plugin setup --client claude,copilot --declare stable -y` runs, **then** `.claude/settings.json` and `.github/copilot/settings.json` each carry `extraKnownMarketplaces.stamity.source` = `{source:"github", repo:"<slug>", ref:"plugin-stable"}` and `enabledPlugins["stamity@stamity"] = true`, and the ledger rows of both files record those two pointers (in `test/cli/commands/pluginSetup.test.ts`). **Given** `--declare plugins/v1.12.0`, **then** the declared `ref` is `plugins/v1.12.0`. **Given** `.claude/settings.json` already holding `extraKnownMarketplaces.stamity` (any value), **then** the command exits 1 naming `.claude/settings.json` and `git status --porcelain` shows no change. **Given** `.claude/settings.json` holding only `enabledPlugins["stamity@stamity"]: true` written by the client, **then** setup succeeds, the key is untouched and unrecorded, and a later `clean -y` leaves it in place. **Given** a declared setup and a foreign key `extraKnownMarketplaces.other` added afterwards, **when** `clean -y` runs, **then** `extraKnownMarketplaces` holds only `other`, `enabledPlugins` holds only foreign entries, no `.bak` exists, and `.github/copilot/settings.json`, which the engine created and which holds nothing foreign, is gone (in `test/merge/settingsKeyOwnership.test.ts`). **Given** `--declare canary` with no canary configured, **then** exit 1 naming `stamity.distribution.channels.canary`; **given** `--declare main`, **then** exit 2 naming the accepted forms; **given** `plugin status --declare stable`, **then** exit 2. **Given** `--client cursor --declare stable`, **then** exit 1 with "set up claude or copilot". **Given** a manifest whose `plugin.declaration.ref` is `plugins/../x`, **then** manifest validation fails naming `plugin.declaration.ref`. **Given** `distributionRefs()` and `scripts/distribution-identity.mjs`, **then** the default channel and tag pattern are equal. **Given** `node scripts/generate-docs.mjs`, **then** `docs/cli-reference.md` lists `--declare <ref>` and `git diff --exit-code` passes after regeneration. |
| `edgeCases` | Claude Code still needs one `plugin install … --scope project` per contributor (external source); setup prints it, and the route guides (file 3) say so. An untrusted folder ignores the Claude declaration silently (vendor page); nothing for the engine to do. A later channel change on an existing setup is not this flag's: `plugin setup` refuses an existing manifest, so it is clean-then-setup until a follow-up adds a config route (inbox row). A fork with a renamed plugin declares its own marketplace name and repository (both read from the running package). A `.github/copilot/settings.json` that already exists with foreign keys (`hooks`, `disableAllHooks`) keeps them; one that is not a JSON object is a collision, as for the Claude file. Windows: settings files are written through the same atomic writer; pointers are strings, no path separators. |

### u2-apm-renovate-preset — a Renovate preset for the APM refs, retargeted by `fork-identity.mjs`

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-apm-renovate-preset |
| `requirements` | REQ-APM-015 |
| `files` | `renovate/apm.json` (new); `scripts/fork-identity.mjs` (header `:1-37`; `plan()` `:216-252`); `test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts` (`TARGETS` `:35`; new cases); `test/ci/renovateApmPreset.test.ts` (new); `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (`:163-164`; the APM Renovate rules `:726-760`); `docs/getting-started.md` (APM section, one line); `test/docsPages.test.ts` (`REATTESTATION_DATE`); `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Renovate's `apm` manager** (renovatebot/renovate `main`, `lib/modules/manager/apm/extract.ts`, read 2026-10-01): an entry `owner/repo/path#ref` gives `depName = "owner/repo/path"` (the text before `#`) and, on github.com, `packageName = "owner/repo"` (the first two segments) with datasource `github-tags`; a 40-hex ref followed by a `# <tag>` comment is a pinned digest; only string entries are read (object entries are dropped). So the rules match `matchDepNames` — `matchPackageNames` would match the source-tree and the slim dependency alike. **Assumed from `docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md (u1-renovate-suffix)`:** `scripts/fork-identity.mjs` accepts `--suffix <id>` (`[a-z0-9]+`) and passes it into `plan(options)` as `options.suffix` (`string \| undefined`). **New, `renovate/apm.json` (canonical, strict — default 2):** `{ "$schema": "https://docs.renovatebot.com/renovate-schema.json", "description": "APM pins of stamity. Renovate's apm manager reads only the string form owner/repo[/path]#<ref> and drops object entries silently; it runs apm install, so apm must be on its PATH, with GITHUB_APM_PAT for a private repository.", "packageRules": [ { "description": "The APM packages in a release's distribution tree: refs are plugins/v<version> tags.", "matchManagers": ["apm"], "matchDepNames": ["zomarit/stamity/apm", "zomarit/stamity/apm-charter"], "versioning": "regex:^plugins/v(?<major>\\d+)\\.(?<minor>\\d+)\\.(?<patch>\\d+)$", "pinDigests": true, "groupName": "stamity APM packages" }, { "description": "The source tree's APM package: refs are v<version> tags.", "matchManagers": ["apm"], "matchDepNames": ["zomarit/stamity"], "versioning": "semver", "pinDigests": true, "groupName": "stamity APM packages" } ] }` (FALLBACK: rule 0's dep names are `zomarit/stamity` with `matchCurrentValue: "/^apm\\/v/"` and the regex prefix `apm/v`). **New, the fork retarget** (`plan()` gains entries in the `retargetPreset` style, `scripts/fork-identity.mjs:197-214`, parse-replace-reparse): rule 0 `matchDepNames` → `["<owner>/<repo>/apm", "<owner>/<repo>/apm-charter"]`, rule 1 → `["<owner>/<repo>"]`; with `--suffix <id>`: rule 0 `versioning` → `regex:^plugins/v(?<major>\\d+)\\.(?<minor>\\d+)\\.(?<patch>\\d+)-<id>\\.(?<build>\\d+)$` (built by `tagVersioningTemplate({ prefix: "plugins/v", suffix: <id>, required: true })`, exported by `scripts/fork-identity.mjs` in file 1's u1-renovate-suffix), rule 1 gains `"ignoreUnstable": false` and `"allowedVersions": "/^v?\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+-<id>\\.\\d+$/"` — the suffix is required in both, because a fork imported by plan 014's recipe carries upstream's `v*` tags (`refs/tags/v*`), which an optional suffix would offer to its consumers. `TARGETS` and the header move from two presets to three. **Docs:** `docs/enterprise-forks.md:163-164` names three presets; `:726-760` points at `renovate/apm.json` (`"extends": ["github><owner>/<repo>//renovate/apm.json"]`) instead of the hand-written rules, keeps the measured facts (apm on Renovate's `PATH`, `GITHUB_APM_PAT`, the lockfile error without them) and states the string-form limit; `docs/getting-started.md`'s APM section names the preset. **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**`renovate/apm.json` keeps APM pins current.** It orders the distribution's `plugins/v<version>` refs and the source tree's `v<version>` tags, pins digests, groups the APM packages into one pull request, and `fork-identity.mjs` retargets it — with `--suffix`, to the fork's own suffixed tags only." **Follows:** `renovate/plugins.json:1-18` (a preset carrying the identity as data that `fork-identity.mjs` retargets) |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** `renovate/apm.json`, **then** it parses as JSON with exactly two `packageRules` whose `matchManagers` are `["apm"]`, `pinDigests` true and one shared `groupName`; rule 0's regex (without the `regex:` prefix) accepts `plugins/v1.12.0` and refuses `plugins/v1.12.0-rc.1`, `v1.12.0` and `plugins/v1.12` (`test/ci/renovateApmPreset.test.ts`). **Given** a temp copy of the repository, **when** `node scripts/fork-identity.mjs --repository https://github.com/acme-corp/stamity-fork --suffix acme` runs, **then** rule 0's dep names are `acme-corp/stamity-fork/apm` and `acme-corp/stamity-fork/apm-charter`, its regex accepts `plugins/v1.12.0-acme.1` and `plugins/v1.12.1-acme.3` and refuses `plugins/v1.12.0` and `plugins/v1.12.0-other.1`, the named groups order `1.12.0-acme.1` < `1.12.0-acme.2` < `1.12.1-acme.1`, rule 1 carries `ignoreUnstable: false` and an `allowedVersions` that accepts `v1.12.0-acme.2` and refuses `v1.13.0`; **when** it runs again, **then** it prints `unchanged renovate/apm.json`; **when** `--check` runs on an untouched copy, **then** it prints `drift renovate/apm.json (matchDepNames)` and exits 1. **Given** the canonical checkout, **when** `fork-identity.mjs --repository https://github.com/zomarit/stamity --check` runs, **then** `renovate/apm.json` reads unchanged. Re-pin `test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts:35` `TARGETS` to three presets (`TEST CHANGE, justified:`). |
| `edgeCases` | A fork without `--suffix` → only the dep names move; versioning stays strict. An `apm.yml` entry written as an object → Renovate drops it silently (the preset description and the guide say so). A consumer pinning by digest (`<slug>/apm#<sha> # plugins/v1.12.0`) → Renovate's digest path updates both. FALLBACK route → the prefix in rule 0 becomes `apm/v`; the tests read the prefix from the record's verdict. |

exports its pure verdict functions): sources are `--slim` (default: a clean export of this checkout's root `apm.yml` + `.apm/`, byte-identical to the release's `apm/` copy by u2-apm-slim-package's test) and `test/fixtures/apm/coexist-package` (ids `acme-auditor`, `acme-report`, `acme-style` with `applyTo: "**/*.md"`, `acme-notes` — none starting `st-` or `stamity-`). **Consumer A, `apm-backed`:** `git init`; `apm.yml` with both local dependencies and `targets: [claude, copilot, cursor, codex]`; `apm install`; assert exit 0, both lock rows typed `apm_package`, their `deployed_files` disjoint, every listed file present; then `node <cli> init -y --tools claude,copilot,cursor,codex --install-mode apm-backed`; then `node <cli> check --json`: `ok` true, the `plugin-duplicates` row `pass`, drift clean, and no ledger path inside either dependency's deployed set. **Consumer B, `generated`:** `git init`; `node <cli> init -y --tools claude,cursor` first; then both dependencies with `targets: [claude, cursor]` and `apm install` (apm skips the files the CLI wrote, "local file exists (not managed by APM)"); then `check --json`: `ok` false and the `plugin-duplicates` row `fail`, its detail naming `cursor`, `st-work` and the stamity dependency. Exit 0 all held; 1 an assertion failed (each listed); 2 apm or the CLI could not run (the split of `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:56-68`). **New, CI:** matrix row `{ apm: '0.32.0', role: coexistence }`; steps `if: matrix.role == 'coexistence'`: "Install" `npm ci`, "Build" `npm run build`, "APM coexistence smoke" `node scripts/apm-coexistence-smoke.mjs --apm "$RUNNER_TEMP/apm-venv/bin/apm" --cli dist/cli.js`; the route smoke step gains `&& matrix.role != 'coexistence'`; the no-`npm ci` comment (`:404-406`) says only the coexistence row installs dependencies, because it drives the CLI. The aggregator already needs `apm-install` (no new job, no `all-ci-checks` pin moves). **New, fork release** — a job of its own rather than a step in `publish` (which holds write credentials; see Concerns C12): `apm-route: { name: "apm route smoke (published slim ref, apm ${{ matrix.apm }})", needs: [gates, publish], if: "needs.publish.result == 'success'", runs-on: ubuntu-latest, timeout-minutes: 15, permissions: { contents: read }, strategy: { fail-fast: false, matrix: { apm: ['0.29.1', '0.32.0'] } } }` with steps: checkout depth 1 `persist-credentials: false` (for the smoke script only), setup-node `22.22.2`, setup-python `3.13` (the SHAs `ci.yml` pins), then "Smoke the published slim ref" with `env: { APM: ${{ matrix.apm }}, GITHUB_APM_PAT: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}, SPEC: ${{ github.repository }}/apm#${{ needs.gates.outputs.plugins_tag }} }` running `python -m venv "$RUNNER_TEMP/apm-venv"`, `pip install --quiet "apm-cli==${APM}"`, `node scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs --apm "$RUNNER_TEMP/apm-venv/bin/apm" --source "$SPEC" --targets claude,copilot,cursor,codex`, then appending `apm install $SPEC` under `### APM install spec` to `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` (`GITHUB_APM_PAT` is APM's documented token variable; the job token is read-only and scoped to the fork). The dry-run summary (`:842`, already rewritten by u2-apm-slim-package) also prints the spec it would test. FALLBACK: `SPEC` names the APM tag. **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**The APM route is proved beside another APM package, and a fork release smoke-tests its own slim ref.** A CI leg installs this package next to a fixture package, then proves the APM-backed mode green and the generated mode red with its duplicates row; `fork-release.yml` installs the fork's freshly published `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>` at apm-cli 0.29.1 and 0.32.0 and prints the spec." **Follows:** `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:548-745` (a throwaway consumer, a deployed-tree verdict, exit 0/1/2) and `.github/workflows/release.yml:554-623` (`apm-route`: a credential-free job running the third-party client) |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** the smoke's exported verdict functions and fixture locks and envelopes (`test/ci/apmCoexistence.test.ts`), **then** two dependencies with one shared deployed path → a failed assertion naming that path; consumer A's envelope with `plugin-duplicates` `warn` → failed; consumer B's envelope with the row `pass` → failed; the expected shapes → passed. **Given** `STAMITY_APM_BIN` armed after `npm run build`, **when** the real smoke runs, **then** it exits 0 (`describe.skipIf`, like `test/ci/apmInstall.test.ts:527`). **Given** `ci.yml` parsed, **then** the `apm-install` matrix holds a `coexistence` row at `0.32.0`, its three gated steps exist in order, and the route smoke step does not run on it. **Given** `fork-release.yml` parsed, **then** the job list is `["probe", "gates", "publish", "apm-route", "dry-run-summary"]` (re-pin `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts:170`, `TEST CHANGE, justified:`), `apm-route.needs` is `["gates", "publish"]`, its permissions are exactly `{ contents: read }`, its matrix is `["0.29.1", "0.32.0"]`, its `SPEC` contains `/apm#` and `needs.gates.outputs.plugins_tag`, and no step of it references any secret other than `GITHUB_TOKEN`. **Given** the "Push plugin distribution" step pair, **then** `release.yml` and `fork-release.yml` still hold it byte-equal (the existing test). |
| `edgeCases` | A future apm that overwrites instead of skipping in consumer B → `check` still fails (drift plus the duplicates row); the smoke asserts only the row's failure. A fork whose distribution lives in another repository → `GITHUB_TOKEN` cannot read it; the job fails visibly and the guide names a fine-grained read token as `GITHUB_APM_PAT` (repository secret) as the remedy. A re-run of a published release → the smoke reruns harmlessly. The 0.29.1 leg on a slim ref → covered by u2-apm-sparse-measure's criterion (c). |
| `depends_on` | u2-apm-backed-mode, u2-apm-floor-current, u2-apm-slim-package, u2-apm-renovate-preset, docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md (u1-npm-dist-tag) |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/ci/apmCoexistence.test.ts test/ci/workflow.test.ts test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts && npm run build && STAMITY_APM_BIN=<scratch>/apm-0.32.0/bin/apm npx vitest run test/ci/apmCoexistence.test.ts && npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm test` |

## Execution order

1. **Intake, once for the session** (the freshness guard): file 1 (`docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md`) and
   Packages 19 and 18 merged; every anchor this file cites re-read against the landed tree. Record in the run record
   the names file 1 landed that these units build on: the `src/manifest/jsonMembers.ts` API and `LedgerEntry.coOwned`
   (u1-settings-key-ownership), the `apm.lock.yaml` reader and its export name (u1-foreign-paths), and
   `scripts/fork-identity.mjs --suffix` (u1-renovate-suffix).
2. **Measurements first, beside the first builders:** `u2-apm-sparse-measure` writes no repository file and decides the
   slim route (FOLDER or FALLBACK) and the root copy (KEEP or STOP) before `u2-apm-slim-package` starts; the payload
   probe of `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode` runs as soon as that unit's harness exists, and its maintainer steps
   (installing VS Code with Copilot Chat; the Codex trust entry) are scheduled early in the session.
3. **One valid serial order**, honouring every `depends_on` edge above: `u2-apm-sparse-measure`, `u2-repo-file-waste`,
   `u2-upgrade-verb`, `u2-main-catalogs`, `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`, `u2-apm-placeholders`, `u2-apm-slim-package`,
   `u2-apm-charter`, `u2-channels-promote`, `u2-admin-templates`, `u2-apm-backed-mode`, `u2-apm-floor-current`,
   `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`, `u2-review-gate-all`, `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot`, `u2-plugin-overrides`, `u2-declare`,
   `u2-apm-renovate-preset`, `u2-apm-coexistence-fork`.
4. **Parallel lanes the edges allow**, each in its own worktree: `u2-repo-file-waste` → `u2-upgrade-verb` beside
   `u2-main-catalogs` → `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`; then `u2-channels-promote` → `u2-admin-templates` beside
   `u2-apm-backed-mode` → `u2-apm-floor-current`. Every other step waits for its predecessors.
5. **Shared files have one writer at a time**, through the session's integrator in integration order: `CHANGELOG.md`
   (each unit's bullet text is fixed in its `interfaces`; sections `### Added`, `### Changed`, `### Fixed` under
   `## [Unreleased]`), `docs/getting-started.md`, `docs/enterprise-forks.md`, `docs/plugins.md`,
   `docs/troubleshooting.md`, `test/docsPages.test.ts` (`REATTESTATION_DATE` and the hand-page headers),
   `docs/cli-reference.md` (regenerated by `u2-upgrade-verb`, then `u2-declare`), `docs/capability-matrix.md`
   (regenerated), the golden snapshot `test/corpus/__snapshots__/emissionGoldens.test.ts.snap` (regenerated with the test
   file named first and `--update` last), `src/cli/commands/check.ts` and its doctor-row list (`apm-client`, then
   `review-gate`, then `plugin-reach`), and the four `test/ci/pluginPackages.*.test.ts`.
6. **One owner per shared contract** (the contract census): the owner rule at `src/emit/planner.ts:865` and
   `pluginOwnedClasses` change first in `u2-apm-backed-mode`, and `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot` and `u2-plugin-overrides`
   build on them; `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs`'s `substitute` changes in `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`, and
   `u2-apm-placeholders` calls it without the plugin runtime; `PluginConfig` gains `mode: "apm-backed"` in
   `u2-apm-backed-mode` before `u2-declare` adds `declaration`.
7. **The Prove phase** (`/st-work`) merges this file's spec delta.
8. **The gates:** `npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test`, then `npm test -- --coverage` (the `src/emit/` and
   `src/merge/` floors) and `npm run check`; every unit that changes a generated hook or a Claude configuration document
   re-syncs the dogfood (`npm run build && node dist/cli.js sync`); CI's Windows leg and coverage are required. The pull
   request title is a conventional-commit subject, for example `feat(routes): APM slim package and apm-backed mode,
   release channels, plugin-mode overrides and the review gate on every client`.

## Risks

| Risk | Severity | Guard |
|---|---|---|
| The distribution tree's root copy, kept so the 1.9–1.11 spec `<slug>#plugins/v<ver>` keeps working, deploys a primitive twice for a root install | Warning | `u2-apm-sparse-measure`'s arm D measures it before the slim package is built; `APM root copy: STOP` holds the unit for the maintainer's choice |
| An apm-backed repository also adds the charter package, so Claude loads two charters with contradicting facts | Warning | the duplicates row fails with the remedy; the README and docs say APM-backed repositories leave the charter package out |
| `release.json`'s new key breaks validation of earlier releases' manifests on promote or rollback | Warning | `charterInstallSpec` is optional in the validator; a test validates a manifest without it |
| apm-cli drops the per-dependency `deployed_files` view (kept "one cycle" in 0.32.0), blinding the duplicates and floor rows | Warning | `u2-apm-backed-mode` extends u1's reader to fall back to the top-level `deployments[]` rows (decision S14); a fixture lock without `deployed_files` is in `test/detect/apmLock.test.ts` |
| `upgrade` parses another release's `sync --json`, a cross-version contract | Warning | it reads only the envelope keys (`ok`, `command`, `version`) and `entries`, `reclaim`, `refused`, `tools`; an absent `tools` refuses without `--allow-client-change` |
| `npx` spawn on Windows | Warning | npm's JavaScript entry run through `process.execPath` with `shell: false` on every platform (`runNpm`'s probes); the `npx.cmd` shell fallback only when both probes miss, refusing a space or a cmd.exe metacharacter in any argument; `registry` validated as an http(s) URL first; unit tests on the builder; the Windows CI leg |
| Ownership changes under `src/emit/` break the 100% floor | Warning | `test/emit/ownership.test.ts` covers every `projectionRowOwners` branch; `npm test -- --coverage` before the PR |
| The census finds `unverified` readings for most Copilot IDE surfaces, so few dedupes are admitted | Minor | by design: the rule keeps a copy unless no client loses it, and the residue section says why |
| The distribution root's archives ride in APM's sparse cone (about 7.3 MB) | Minor | documented; a follow-up row proposes moving them under `archives/` |
| The shipped review gate likely never holds on the models this project runs: `TaskCompleted` needs the task tools, off by default on models newer than Opus 4.7 since Claude Code 2.1.233 | Warning | `u2-review-gate-all` re-maps Claude to `Stop` after the probe measures it; follow-up row to measure sooner (one `claude -p` run with and without `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TODO_TOOLS=1`) |
| A foreign-invocation signature that ever appears in a Claude payload would disarm Claude's own guard silently | Warning | signatures enter the table only with measured evidence (present in every captured payload of that client, absent from every captured Claude payload, asserted by test); `cursor_version` is vendor-specific |
| The measurement steps need the maintainer: VS Code is not installed, Codex needs a home-config trust entry, and the probe runs use the maintainer's logins | Warning | each step's absence is `Not done:` with its owner; the units wire only measured clients and keep the prose ladder elsewhere |
| File 1's per-key ownership lands top-level names only, so the nested declaration keys cannot be owned | Warning | `u2-declare`'s conditional first step widens names to pointers under the same tests; better: the orchestrator adds pointer depth 2 and "an equal foreign value stays foreign" to `u1-settings-key-ownership`'s criteria now |
| A ruleset that blocks force pushes on a channel branch refuses `GITHUB_TOKEN` | Warning | `promote.yml` pushes with the `STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY` environment secret when set; `promote-channel.mjs` names the bypass on a `GH013` refusal; u3-settings-constraints documents "Deploy keys" in the bypass list |
| `plugins/v*` tags (which the default branch's catalogs now pin) are outside every ruleset, so only the release step's refusal keeps them immutable | Warning | `.github/workflows/release.yml:977-983` refuses to move a tag; u3-settings-constraints recommends a `plugins/v*` tag ruleset with a bypass for the release identity |
| Package 18 (plan 014 file 2) rewrites `docs/plugins.md`, `docs/enterprise-forks.md`, `test/docsPages.test.ts` (records split), `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`, `release.yml`, `CONTRIBUTING.md` and the release-controls checklist before this file runs; file 1 rewrites `claudeSettings.ts`, the reclaim reducer and the fork tests | Warning | every unit re-reads its cited lines at intake (the freshness guard) and applies its change to the text it finds |
| Cursor IDE users on an old plugin root load each rule twice after a repository gains `.cursor/rules/` | Minor | the CHANGELOG bullet says to update the plugin with the CLI; the old root's rules are no longer reported as duplicates |
| A ref-less add fails between a release's version commit and the tag's publication | Minor | loud failure, nothing wrong installs; the Codex README sentence names the window |
| Claude's auto permission mode hands a subagent's report through `SubagentHandback`, so `last_assistant_message` may hold no verdict | Minor | the gate then holds at most `HOLD_CAP` times; follow-up row |
| A promotion to a lower version may not reach clients whose auto-update only moves forward | Minor | `plugin update` moved down in the 2026-09-22 walk; the auto-update legs are `Not done:` rows in u3-route-proofs |
| The Windows leg cannot run the `pre-receive` hook fixture | Minor | that one case is skipped on win32 with its reason in the test, never counted as passed |
| Live probe turns can be stopped by a safety classifier | Minor | a stopped session is a `Not done:` row; its transcript is never read back in (memory note `safety-classifier-stopped-sessions`) |
| Two drafts' units edit the same adapters, `probe.ts`, `check.ts`, `planner.ts`, `tokens.mjs`, `build-plugin-distribution.mjs` and `PluginConfig` | Warning | the cross-draft `depends_on` edges, one owner per contract (Execution order step 6), and a contract census at each unit's intake |
| A vitest call with an optional-value flag ahead of a path (`--json`, `--outputFile`, `-u`, `--update`) writes into a test file — it overwrote `test/merge/coverageGaps.test.ts` during this plan's drafting on 2026-10-01 (restored from HEAD) | Warning | every command names its files before any flag; snapshot updates use `npx vitest run <file> --update` (learning `vitest-update-flag-takes-an-optional-value`) |

## Follow-ups (appended to `.stamity/inbox.md` at this plan's write)

- Minor · scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:513 · the four plugin archives sit at the distribution root, so APM's sparse cone (which always materializes root-level files) fetches about 7.3 MB with any subfolder; moving them under `archives/` would cut the slim route's fetch to a few KB (probe 2026-10-01).
- Minor · scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:669-689 · `.apm/skills/*/agents/openai.yaml` reaches every APM consumer whatever its targets (APM deploys skill directories whole, `.claude/skills/` included); apm-cli 0.32.0's per-dependency filters (`skill_subset`, `target_subset`) cannot drop one companion — revisit if APM gains per-target companion filtering.
- Minor · scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs:70-79 · the charter-reference phrases say "in AGENTS.md", while an APM-only Claude consumer receives the charter as `.claude/rules/stamity-charter.md` and may have no `AGENTS.md`; a route-neutral phrase ("listed under Verification gates in the charter") would read true on every route.
- Minor · content/charter/stamity-charter.md:68-70 · the Touchpoints paragraph promises the nine as skills to a client reading `.agents/skills/`, but APM-only Codex receives none (APM's codex profile carries no command class); reword per route in u3-route-guide.
- Minor · src/cli/notice/updateNotice.ts:310-322 · the update banner still prints `npx -y <name>@<v> sync`; with `upgrade` shipped it could print `upgrade --to <v>` — decide after the first release carrying the verb.
- Minor · .github/workflows/release.yml:554 · no standing post-publication smoke of `zomarit/stamity/apm#plugins/v<ver>` in the canonical release; file 03's u3-route-proofs covers 1.12.0, a job would catch a later regression.
- Minor · src/cli/commands/plugin/probe.ts:605-686 · the `apm.yml` dependency-line match survives only as the no-lock fallback; retire it once consumers carry locks.
- Minor · docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:48-58 · a redirect root `apm.yml` for consumers of the old `#plugins/v*` spec is unmeasured (arm R needs a throwaway public repository); measure on the maintainer's approval.
- Warning · `src/hooks/scripts.ts:1984` · The review gate holds only `TaskCompleted`, which Claude Code fires through the task tools, off by default on models newer than Opus 4.7 since 2.1.233; plan 016 file 2's `u2-review-gate-all` re-maps it; measure sooner (one `claude -p` run with and without `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TODO_TOOLS=1`) if the gate matters before Package 20.
- Minor · `renovate/plugins.json:6-11` · The preset's file patterns also match the default branch's catalogs, so a fork that extends it gets ref-bump pull requests the generator's `--check` rejects.
- Minor · `src/hooks/scripts.ts:1998` · In Claude's auto permission mode `last_assistant_message` is not the delivered subagent report (`SubagentHandback`); read `tool_input.message` from a PostToolUse on that tool if reviewers run in auto mode.
- Minor · `src/adapters/cursor.ts:120,305`, `scripts/plugins/clients/cursor.mjs:119` · The cited `cursor.com/docs/context/rules` and `/docs/agent/subagents` now redirect to `/docs/rules` and `/docs/subagents`.
- Minor · `scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs:144-166` · A relative-path Claude entry in the distribution catalog would let a project or managed declaration install without a per-contributor `plugin install` (Claude does not install an external-source plugin from project settings); probe before switching.
- Minor · `src/cli/commands/plugin.ts` · Changing the declared channel on an existing plugin-backed setup is clean-then-setup; add a config route when someone asks.
- Minor · `.stamity/learnings/codex-hooks-default-on-and-codex-exec-ran-none.md` · Upstream says `codex exec` runs hooks since openai/codex PR #26434 (2026-06-16); the probe's trusted-folder run settles the learning's open question — update or retire it with the result.
